# AWJ-TAX-ZATCA-AUDIT-1 — Deliverable B: Gap Matrix

> Requirement-by-requirement: **Official requirement → AWJ implementation → Evidence → Gap → Risk**, with document, section, version and date for every requirement. Audit-only; the audited commit is `0534ef16543b01c69b416b58897337e012a9d1ab`. A passing test suite alone does not establish regulatory compliance, and nothing here is a certification or an official ZATCA view.

| | |
|---|---|
| Requirements covered | **295** — 229 regulatory (register R1) + 66 technical (register R2); plus the BR-KSA/XML element coverage (Part 3), the Top-25 cross-check (Part 4) and the VAT-return box coverage (Part 5) |
| Sources | Documents retrieved from `zatca.gov.sa` on 2026-10-10 (Arabic prevails over unofficial English translations) and the ZATCA SDK 238-R3.4.8 run offline; see the inventories in deliverable A §10 and the registers in the evidence folder |
| Companion | A `AWJ-TAX-ZATCA-AUDIT-1.md` (findings, verdict) · C `AWJ-TAX-ZATCA-REMEDIATION-PLAN.md` |

## How to read

* **Status** uses the audit labels: `VERIFIED IMPLEMENTED`, `VERIFIED DEFECT`, `PARTIALLY IMPLEMENTED`, `NOT IMPLEMENTED`, `NOT VERIFIED`, `NOT APPLICABLE`. `NOT APPLICABLE` marks taxpayer/Authority obligations and penalty amounts that are not a system capability; for penalty rows the linked findings are the ones that would *trigger* the violation.
* **Severity** in the last column is the worst final severity of the linked findings (not a separate opinion). **Tasks** are the Remediation Plan items that close those findings.
* Requirement text is clipped to 450 characters; the full text, notes and verification label are in the registers (evidence folder). Source keys such as `[IR]`, `[TIG-EN]`, `D1` are defined in deliverable A §10.1–10.2.
* `new_gap` flags a requirement that is not met but is not covered by any numbered finding.

## Part 1 — Regulatory requirements (R1)

### R1 2.A Scope and applicability of e-invoicing

| Official requirement (source · section · version) | Status | AWJ implementation · evidence | Gap · risk | Findings · severity | Tasks |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| **R1-001** · Scope<br>Persons subject to the E-Invoicing Regulation: (1) taxable person resident in KSA; (2) customer or any third party issuing a tax invoice on behalf of a resident taxable person.<br>*[REG-AR — E-Invoicing Regulation (AR), 4 Dec 2020]/[REG-EN — E-Invoicing Regulation (EN), 4 Dec 2020] 2020 · Art. 3(A), p.3* | **PARTIALLY IMPLEMENTED** | Every posted sales invoice gets Phase-1 artefacts (UUID, ICV, PIH, XML, 5-tag QR) whatever the tenant or app state (app/Services/Accounting/InvoiceService.php:1026; ZatcaService.php:38-108), yet e-invoicing is catalogued as an optional app that new tenants start without (app/Support/ApplicationCatalog.php:91-96). Issuance on behalf of another taxable person is unsupported: codes are only 0100000/0200000 (ZatcaService.php:254) and the delivery 'platform_on_behalf' mode is blocked …<br>*Evidence:* PH1-03 (C Probe 3: fresh tenant compliance.zatca=false, GET /invoices/{id}/zatca 403, A4 printed without QR); SEC-08 (F08: artefacts generated while the app is disabled); PH2-13; GM2 R1-087/R1-088 (self-billing and third-party billing NOT IMPLEMENTED); grep 'self.?bill\|third.?party.?bill\|on_behalf' in app/: delivery gate only. | Applicability is not tied to the tenant's status: e-invoicing is presented as opt-in and the A4 QR disappears for new tenants; a customer or third party cannot issue e-invoices on behalf of a resident taxable person (no self-billed or third-party flag).<br>*Risk:* Subject tenants can issue non-compliant e-invoices by default, and tenants using self-billing or third-party billing cannot comply through AWJ.<br>*Note:* Scope row. Annex 2 self-billing flag 1.2 is enforced from 4 Dec 2021; the third-party flag 1.3 from Integration. R1-016 notes that no general exemption exists, which supports making compliance.zatca mandatory for KSA VAT-registered tenants. | PH1-03, SEC-08, PH2-13<br>**P1** | REM-20, REM-16 |
| **R1-002** · Scope<br>Non-resident persons are not required to issue e-invoices or e-notes for supplies taxable in KSA.<br>*[REG — E-Invoicing Regulation, 4 Dec 2020] 2020 · Art. 3(C), p.3* | **NOT APPLICABLE** | Tenants carry country (default 'SA') and a nullable vat_number (database/migrations/2025_01_01_000001_create_tenants_and_users.php:16-19) but no residency or registration status; neither field is consulted when e-invoice artefacts are generated for every posted invoice (InvoiceService.php:1026; PrintTemplateContract.php:158-161).<br>*Evidence:* none (see reason); grep of invoicing and print code for tenant country or registration status: none. | none (relief for non-resident persons; issuing e-invoices anyway is not prohibited).<br>*Risk:* none identified<br>*Note:* Rule for a different actor. A non-resident tenant would still receive Phase-1 artefacts (over-compliance). A non-resident VAT-registered supplier must still issue a tax invoice showing the customer's VAT number (R1-015), which the standard invoice print supports. | —<br>**—** | — |
| **R1-003** · Scope<br>Subject persons must issue e-invoices for *all* transactions requiring tax invoices and e-notes in all cases the VAT Law/IR require.<br>*[REG — E-Invoicing Regulation, 4 Dec 2020] 2020 · Art. 3(B), p.3* | **PARTIALLY IMPLEMENTED** | All sales invoices (sales module, POS, fuel, recurring, posted public-API drafts) are issued through InvoiceService::post, which always builds the e-invoice artefacts (InvoiceService.php:1026, the only ZatcaService caller). Credit notes and sales returns produce none (CreditNoteService.php:50-176); no sales debit note, advance-payment or deemed-supply invoice exists; storefront orders never become invoices.<br>*Evidence:* PH1-06/PH2-05 (C Probe 1: credit_notes and return_documents have no zatca columns; D P2: max ICV unchanged after a credit note); CON-03 (no 386, receipts to AR only); CON-08 (grep deemed: none); ACC-08 (no CommerceOrder-to-Invoice path); PH1-03 (A4 QR missing for post-cutover tenants); grep 'ZatcaService\|buildFor' in app/. | E-notes are never generated; advances, deemed supplies and storefront sales get no e-invoice; simplified invoices printed on A4 lack the QR for post-cutover tenants.<br>*Risk:* Transactions that legally require e-invoices or e-notes are documented without them, exposing tenants to e-invoicing violations.<br>*Note:* Same pipeline as GM2 R1-086. The commerce exclusion is by design (CommerceOrder is not an Invoice) but still needs the order-to-invoice ADR that ACC-08 requests. | PH1-06, PH2-05, CON-03, CON-08, ACC-08, PH1-03<br>**P0** | REM-18, REM-29, REM-32, REM-30, REM-20 |
| **R1-004** · Legal status<br>E-invoices/e-notes *are* tax invoices and credit/debit notes; all VAT provisions apply, "including but not limited to" penalties (VAT Law ch. 16), content (IR Art. 53), notes (IR Art. 54), record-keeping (IR Art. 66).<br>*[REG — E-Invoicing Regulation, 4 Dec 2020] 2020 · Art. 4(A)(1)-(4), pp.3-4* | **PARTIALLY IMPLEMENTED** | The posted invoice row is the tax invoice and stores its e-invoice artefacts (InvoiceService.php:1035-1056). The artefacts and the print diverge: QR/XML carry the draft's created_at while the print shows invoice_date (ZatcaService.php:163,253), and reprints read live party data. Notes are not e-notes, and record-keeping controls are partial.<br>*Evidence:* PH1-01 (C Probe 1: printed 2026-09-15 vs QR 2026-10-01T09:00); PH1-02 (Probe 1: no seller/buyer snapshot columns); CON-06, PH1-04, PH1-05 (IR 53 content, GM2 R1-083); PH1-06, PH2-05, ACC-06 (IR 54 notes, GM2 R1-113); PH1-16, ACC-10 (IR 66 records). | The e-invoice and the human-readable tax invoice are not one consistent document; IR 53 content, IR 54 note rules and IR 66 record-keeping are only partly met (detailed in R1-075 to R1-123 and the records rows).<br>*Risk:* Because e-invoices are tax invoices, every content, note and record defect is sanctionable under the VAT penalty provisions.<br>*Note:* Umbrella legal-status row; field-level determinations are in GM2 (sections 2.D/2.E) and the records rows (section 2.K). | PH1-01, PH1-02, PH1-04, PH1-05, CON-06, PH1-06, PH2-05, ACC-06, PH1-16, ACC-10<br>**P0** | REM-13, REM-14, REM-15, REM-34, REM-18, REM-02, REM-36, REM-33 |
| **R1-005** · Scope<br>Transactions that require e-invoices include: taxable supplies at standard or zero rate; exports of goods/services; intra-GCC supplies; nominal (deemed) supplies; any payments received before supply (advances).<br>*[RES-EN — Implementation Resolution (EN), 19 May 2023] 2023-05-19 · First(5)(A)-(E), p.1* | **PARTIALLY IMPLEMENTED** | Sales invoice lines carry 15% or 0%, so standard-rated and zero-rated supplies are e-invoiced. Nothing identifies exports or intra-GCC supplies (only an integer tax_rate per line, no category), there is no deemed-supply or advance-payment document, and the transaction code is always 0100000/0200000 with type 388 (ZatcaService.php:254,272).<br>*Evidence:* VAT-03 (grep VATEX\|tax_category\|exempt in app/: none); PH2-13 (no export, nominal or summary flags); CON-03 (receipts post to AR only, no 386); CON-08 (grep deemed in app/: none); ACC-08 (storefront orders never invoiced). | Exports (B) and intra-GCC supplies (C) cannot be identified as such; deemed supplies (D) and advances (E) get no e-invoice; zero-rated sales are indistinguishable from exempt ones.<br>*Risk:* Tenants cannot evidence zero-rating of exports or issue the e-invoices required for advances and deemed supplies, leading to under-declared output VAT and invoicing violations.<br>*Note:* The export, nominal and summary flags (Annex 2 1.4) are Integration-phase fields; the Phase-1 duty is to issue the e-invoice itself. | VAT-03, PH2-13, CON-03, CON-08, ACC-08<br>**P1** | REM-12, REM-16, REM-29, REM-32, REM-30 |
| **R1-006** · Scope<br>E-notes are required in all cases where the IR requires notes.<br>*[RES-EN — Implementation Resolution (EN), 19 May 2023] · First(6), p.2* | **PARTIALLY IMPLEMENTED** | Sales credit notes (CreditNoteService) and sales returns (ReturnService) are system-generated but carry no QR, UUID, ICV, hash or XML; the original-invoice reference and reason are optional (migration 000018:29-30) and the print omits the seller address. No sales debit note exists: credit_notes.type is sales or purchase only (migration 000039:28).<br>*Evidence:* PH1-06 (C Probe 1: CN-2026-00001 posted with null reference and reason; no zatca columns); PH2-05 (D P2: max ICV unchanged after a credit note); ACC-06 (B probe: notes against a draft, another partner, 10x the amount accepted); CON-06 (from-credit-note.ts:48-56); GM2 R1-111 NOT IMPLEMENTED. | Notes are not e-notes: no QR on notes to simplified invoices, no mandatory invoice reference, no chain membership; debit notes for upward adjustments cannot be issued.<br>*Risk:* VAT decreases and increases are documented with non-compliant notes, invalidating the adjustments and exposing tenants to penalties.<br>*Note:* Resolution Annex 1 (mandated from 4 Dec 2021) requires the ability to generate the credit/debit notes associated with both invoice types. | PH1-06, PH2-05, ACC-06, CON-06<br>**P0** | REM-18, REM-02, REM-34 |
| **R1-007** · Scope<br>Excluded from e-invoicing: (A) supplies fully exempt from VAT; (B) advances for exempt supplies; (C) supplies subject to reverse charge; (D) import of goods.<br>*[RES-EN — Implementation Resolution (EN), 19 May 2023]; [FAQ-21 — Phase-1 FAQ, 10 Jun 2021] · [RES — Implementation Resolution, 19 May 2023] First(7), p.2; [FAQ-21] p.13* | **PARTIALLY IMPLEMENTED** | E-invoice artefacts are generated only for posted sales invoices (sole caller InvoiceService.php:1026), never for purchases, expenses or imports, so reverse-charge purchases and imports stay outside e-invoicing. A fully exempt sale cannot be identified: lines carry only tax_rate, so it is issued as a 'فاتورة ضريبية' with QR and ICV at 0%, which reads as zero-rated.<br>*Evidence:* grep 'ZatcaService\|buildFor' in app/: InvoiceService.php only; VAT-03 (no S/Z/E/O category; SalesConfigController.php:35-39 lists 'Zero Rated' and 'Tax Free' both at rate 0); PrintTemplateContract.php:158-161 (title from document type only). | The exclusion for fully exempt supplies (A) cannot be applied, because exempt supplies are indistinguishable from zero-rated ones; (B) to (D) are honoured by construction.<br>*Risk:* Exempt supplies may be documented, and later reported, as zero-rated tax invoices, misstating the VAT treatment.<br>*Note:* The reverse-charge/import exclusion is honoured only because AWJ has no reverse-charge or import model at all (CON-04 is a return-side gap, not an e-invoicing breach). | VAT-03<br>**P1** | REM-12 |
| **R1-008** · Scope<br>E-invoices comprise Tax Invoices and Simplified Tax Invoices as defined in IR Art. 53.<br>*[RES-EN — Implementation Resolution (EN), 19 May 2023] · First(4), p.1* | **PARTIALLY IMPLEMENTED** | Both types exist: zatca_document_type standard\|simplified is stored per invoice (InvoiceService.php:429-447,1020), validated (StoreInvoiceRequest.php:19), and drives the printed title and transaction code (PrintTemplateContract.php:158-161; ZatcaService.php:254). Classification is advisory, keyed to the partner VAT regex, and POS receipts title simplified invoices 'فاتورة ضريبية'.<br>*Evidence:* C PC-5, D PC-9 (ZatcaTest classification tests); PH1-05 (C Probe 1: standard posted without buyer VAT; ZatcaTest::an_explicit_document_type_overrides_the_customer_suggestion); PH1-11 (pos/page.tsx:1464-1470; thermal-receipt.tsx:53). | The IR 53 definitions are not enforced at issuance, so the wrong type can be issued, and POS simplified invoices are presented as tax invoices.<br>*Risk:* Customers receive the wrong invoice type, affecting their input-VAT recovery and the seller's compliance.<br>*Note:* Detailed type rules are in R1-075, R1-077 and R1-101 (GM2). | PH1-05, PH1-11<br>**P1** | REM-15, REM-34 |
| **R1-009** · Deduction link<br>Only e-invoices generated per the Resolution (and, from the Integration phase, cleared or reported) are tax invoices for exercising input-tax deduction (Unified Agreement Art. 48(1)); from the date announced by the Authority.<br>*[RES-EN — Implementation Resolution (EN), 19 May 2023]; [DG-EN — Detailed E-Invoicing Guidelines v2, May 2023] May 2023 · [RES — Implementation Resolution, 19 May 2023] First(3), p.1; [DG — Detailed E-Invoicing Guidelines v2, May 2023] 7.1 p.37; failure-scenario footnotes pp.49-50 ("Uncleared …* | **NOT IMPLEMENTED** | Purchases record only an optional supplier_invoice_no (app/Models/Purchase.php:27); there is no field for the supplier e-invoice UUID, QR or clearance status, no QR/TLV parser outside the sales ZATCA path, and input VAT is debited to tax_input on posting regardless (PurchaseService.php:476). On the sales side, standard invoices are released to buyers before clearance.<br>*Evidence:* CON-08 (input-tax evidence not validated; StorePurchaseRequest.php:26); grep 'tlv\|qr' over app/ excluding Zatca and print code: no purchase or document-centre parser; SUB-01 (E Appendix C: no release path consults clearance). | No buyer-side control flags uncleared, manual or non-e-invoice supplier documents before input VAT is claimed, and AWJ's own B2B invoices reach buyers uncleared.<br>*Risk:* Tenants may deduct input VAT on documents that are not valid tax invoices, and their customers may lose deduction on uncleared AWJ invoices.<br>*Note:* Settled by capturing supplier e-invoice identifiers/QR and a configurable warn/block policy (CLAUDE.md rule 6). | CON-08, SUB-01<br>**P0** | REM-32, REM-24 |

Phase-2 integration is not operational: the XML, stamp and QR fail the official SDK on every invoice shape, credit/debit notes are not e-invoiced, there is no onboarding, no clearance gate, no automatic reporting, and the shipped deployment has no queue worker.<br>*Evidence:* D S3: SDK GLOBAL VALIDATION FAILED 18/18 (PH2-01..04); PH2-05; PH2-09 (no CSR/OTP/CSID code); SUB-01 (E Appendix C); SUB-05 (no scheduler, no automatic attempt); OPS-01 (Dockerfile:47-53, deploy/entrypoint.sh:98); register section 4 (Waves 1-24 deadlines passed; Wave 25 by 1 Feb 2027). | AWJ supports Phase 1 only, while Phase 2 already applies to essentially every registered tenant.<br>*Risk:* Tenants past their wave date cannot meet the integration obligation through AWJ (violation #8, R1-213).<br>*Note:* Timeline row, judged against the register's section-4 conclusion that an ERP must ship Phase 1 and full Phase 2 for all tenants. | PH2-01, PH2-02, PH2-03, PH2-04, PH2-05, PH2-09, SUB-01, SUB-05, OPS-01<br>**P0** | REM-17, REM-16, REM-18, REM-19, REM-24, REM-23 |

| **R1-012** · Scope<br>No tax invoice (or e-invoice) is needed where a transaction consists solely of "Not subject to VAT" supplies; if such documents are generated on the same solution, there must be no gaps in the counter (ICV) and previous-hash chain.<br>*[DG-EN — Detailed E-Invoicing Guidelines v2, May 2023] May 2023 · FAQ p.62* | **PARTIALLY IMPLEMENTED** | Each posted invoice takes the next ICV and the PIH of the previous stored XML under a tenant row lock, backed by a unique (tenant_id, zatca_icv) index, so generated documents are gap-free on supported paths (ZatcaService.php:59-75; migration 000053:69). AWJ cannot mark a supply 'not subject to VAT' (no category O), so such transactions are issued as tax invoices at 0%.<br>*Evidence:* D PC-2 (PG: six concurrent posts gave ICV 1..6; a failed post rolls back); F PC-9 (8 concurrent posters, chain intact); VAT-03 (no S/Z/E/O category); PH1-09 (C Probe 1: model-level delete of a posted invoice succeeds). | Out-of-scope supplies cannot be identified, so category O is unavailable even on mixed invoices; chain continuity rests on service paths only, as a model-level delete of a posted invoice leaves an undetected counter gap.<br>*Risk:* Out-of-scope transactions are presented as zero-rated tax invoices, and a non-HTTP deletion could break the counter sequence unnoticed.<br>*Note:* Issuing a document for out-of-scope supplies is optional; once issued, ICV/PIH continuity is required, which AWJ meets on supported paths. | VAT-03, PH1-09<br>**P1** | REM-12, REM-07 |
| **R1-013** · Deemed supply<br>A tax invoice must be issued for a deemed (nominal) supply and retained for audit; it is not provided to the recipient, who cannot deduct.<br>*[TIG-EN — Tax Invoicing & Records Guideline v3, May 2026] v3 May 2026 · 4.1.2 Note 3, p.14* | **NOT IMPLEMENTED** | No deemed (nominal) supply concept: no document, flag or workflow issues and retains a tax invoice for own-use or free supplies, and the transaction code never sets the nominal position (ZatcaService.php:254).<br>*Evidence:* grep 'deemed\|nominal_supply\|own_use\|self_supply\|private_use' over app/*.php: none; CON-08 (lead grep; GM3 R1-141/R1-142); PH2-13. | Tenants cannot issue or retain the tax invoice required for a deemed supply, nor flag it as nominal once integrated.<br>*Risk:* Output VAT on deemed supplies goes undocumented and undeclared.<br>*Note:* The Nominal Supply flag (Annex 2 1.4) is an Integration-phase field; the TIG duty to issue and retain the invoice applies in every phase. | CON-08, PH2-13<br>**P2** | REM-32, REM-16 |
| **R1-014** · Exempt supplies<br>No tax invoice is required for domestic exempt supplies; if one invoice combines taxable and exempt items it must meet tax-invoice requirements.<br>*[TIG-EN — Tax Invoicing & Records Guideline v3, May 2026]; [DG-EN — Detailed E-Invoicing Guidelines v2, May 2023] · [TIG — Tax Invoicing & Records Guideline v3, May 2026] Note 4 p.15; [DG — Detailed E-Invoicing Guidelines v2, May 2023] 4.1.2 example p.16* | **PARTIALLY IMPLEMENTED** | Mixed invoices with 15% and 0% lines are supported and issued as tax invoices (per-line tax_rate, app/Models/InvoiceLine.php:26). | **R1-015** · Reverse charge<br>Where the customer self-accounts (RCM), the non-resident supplier does not issue a tax invoice and the customer is not required to self-invoice; transaction evidence must be retained. A non-resident supplier that *is* VAT-registered in KSA must issue a tax invoice showing the customer's VAT number.<br>*[TIG-EN — Tax Invoicing & Records Guideline v3, May 2026]; [IEG-EN — Imports & Exports Guideline 2nd ed., May 2026] 2nd ed. SEC-08 (F08: artefacts generated while the app is disabled); PH1-03. | none (Authority power; no system capability required).<br>*Risk:* none identified<br>*Note:* The register notes that no general SME exemption exists, which contradicts treating e-invoicing as an opt-in app (PH1-03, SEC-08). A ZATCA suspension for a specific tenant would need an explicit, audited setting rather than the app toggle. | PH1-03, SEC-08<br>**P1** | REM-20 |
| **R1-017** · Prohibited issuer<br>A person not registered for VAT must not issue a tax invoice (any document intended to show/collect VAT); fine up to SAR 100,000.<br>*[VATL — VAT Law (Royal Decree M/113, as amended)]; [TIG-EN — Tax Invoicing & Records Guideline v3, May 2026] · [VATL] Art. 44, p.13; [TIG — Tax Invoicing & Records Guideline v3, May 2026] 4.1.3 p.16* | **VERIFIED DEFECT** | The tenant VAT number is optional (UpdateCompanyRequest.php:18) and InvoiceService::post has no seller-registration precondition (InvoiceService.php:880-1062). An unregistered tenant posts invoices titled 'فاتورة ضريبية' or 'فاتورة ضريبية مبسطة' (PrintTemplateContract.php:158-161), with VAT at the default rate, a QR whose VAT tag is empty and the ZATCA compliance footer.<br>*Evidence:* PH1-04/PH2-16 (C Probe 1: no-VAT tenant, status posted, QR tag2 empty, XML CompanyID empty); PH1-13 (footer ar.json:2186 printed regardless); contrast DeliveryFinancialRoleGate.php:58-59, which blocks unregistered merchants in the delivery slice. | Nothing prevents a tenant without VAT registration from issuing documents titled and computed as tax invoices.<br>*Risk:* Unregistered tenants can issue tax invoices and collect VAT, incurring a fine of up to SAR 100,000 (VAT Law Art. 44).<br>*Note:* Fix: a tenant VAT-registration status that switches the title to a commercial invoice, suppresses VAT, QR and footer, and blocks VAT lines; any tolerance as a configurable policy (CLAUDE.md rule 6). | PH1-04, PH2-16, PH1-13<br>**P1** | REM-14, REM-34 |

### R1 2.B Phase 1 — Generation requirements (from 4 Dec 2021)

| Official requirement (source · section · version) | Status | AWJ implementation · evidence | Gap · risk | Findings · severity | Tasks |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| **R1-018** · Electronic generation<br>Invoices/notes must be generated by electronic means; manual/handwritten invoices or word-processor invoices are not compliant; a scanned/copied paper invoice is not an e-invoice.<br>*[REG — E-Invoicing Regulation, 4 Dec 2020]; [DG-EN — Detailed E-Invoicing Guidelines v2, May 2023]; [TIG-EN — Tax Invoicing & Records Guideline v3, May 2026]; [FAQ-21 — Phase-1 FAQ, 10 Jun 2021] · [REG] Art. 1(1)-(2); [DG — Detailed E-Invoicing Guidelines v2, May 2023] 4.1.1(b) p.14; [TIG — Tax …* | **VERIFIED IMPLEMENTED** | Sales invoices are created only by InvoiceService::create (InvoiceService.php:97) and issued only by post(), which derives totals from the lines and builds the QR/XML electronically (InvoiceService.php:894-916,1026). Credit notes and returns are likewise system-generated (CreditNoteService.php:50,124). No path imports or uploads a manual or scanned document as an issued sales invoice.<br>*Evidence:* grep 'Invoice::create\|new Invoice(' in app/: InvoiceService.php:97 only; C PC-3 (InvoiceTest::totals_are_derived_from_lines_even_if_the_header_is_tampered); C PC-10 (the Public API cannot post or set number, status or zatca fields). | none<br>*Risk:* none identified<br>*Note:* The content of system-generated notes is deficient (PH1-06); assessed under R1-006. | —<br>**—** | — |
| **R1-019** · Format<br>Phase 1: "No required format as long as the required data are present"; any electronic format; XML/PDF-A3 not mandated until Integration.<br>*[RES-EN — Implementation Resolution (EN), 19 May 2023] · Annex 1 p.10; [DG — Detailed E-Invoicing Guidelines v2, May 2023] 4.1.1(b)* | **VERIFIED IMPLEMENTED** | Phase-1 human-readable outputs are electronic: A4 print and client-side PDF (web/src/app/(app)/invoices/[id]/page.tsx:502,518), thermal receipt (web/src/modules/documents/templates/thermal-receipt.tsx), plus a stored XML per invoice (InvoiceService.php:1055).<br>*Evidence:* C PC-9/PC-13 (renderers read stored values; all ten tax-invoice templates); E Appendix C (print, PDF and share paths). | none (data completeness is assessed under R1-020).<br>*Risk:* none identified<br>*Note:* Because XML is not mandated in Phase 1, the stub XML (PH1-17) is not a Phase-1 format breach; its relevance is the PIH chain carried into Phase 2 (see GM1a_conflicts.md). | —<br>**—** | — |
| **R1-020** · Fields<br>Phase 1 documents must contain all Annex 2 fields marked "Enforced on 4 Dec 2021", comply with business rules for conditional fields and allowable values, and be time-stamped.<br>*[RES-EN — Implementation Resolution (EN), 19 May 2023] · Annex 1 p.10; Annex 2 pp.16-35* | **PARTIALLY IMPLEMENTED** | Prints carry title, number, date, seller name/VAT/CR/address, buyer name/VAT, lines and totals (web/src/modules/documents/builder/from-invoice.ts:146-195). Missing or wrong Phase-1 fields: issue time (draft created_at), supply date, buyer address, per-line VAT rate (label fixed '(15%)'), non-standard-rate narration, POS simplified title and the note reference; templates may drop quantity and unit price; seller VAT is optional.<br>*Evidence:* PH1-01 (C Probe 1); CON-07 (no supply_date); PH1-02/PH1-05 (buyer address never printed); CON-06 (PrintTemplateContract.php:72-83; ar.json:2184); VAT-03; PH1-11; PH1-06; PH1-04; PH1-18; GM2 R1-083/R1-095/R1-096/R1-099. | Several Annex 2 fields enforced from 4 Dec 2021 (1.1 on POS, 3.2, 3.3, 5.2, 7.3, 7.4, 7.8, 11.1, note reference) are missing, optional or wrong, and the time stamp is not the issue moment.<br>*Risk:* Issued documents lack mandatory Phase-1 fields, each a separate violation per missing field (R1-210).<br>*Note:* Phase-1 fields per Resolution Annex 2 (4 Dec 2021 column): 1.1, 1.2, 2.1, 3.1-3.3, 4.1-4.3, 5.1-5.3, 7.1, 7.3, 7.4, 7.6, 7.8, 7.10, 8.2, 8.4, 8.5, 11.1. | PH1-01, PH1-02, PH1-04, PH1-05, PH1-06, PH1-11, PH1-18, CON-06, CON-07, VAT-03<br>**P1** | REM-13, REM-14, REM-15, REM-18, REM-34, REM-29, REM-12 |
| **R1-021** · QR (Phase 1)<br>For simplified invoices/notes it is mandatory to generate and print a QR code containing: seller name; seller VAT number; timestamp (date+time); total with VAT; VAT total.<br>*[RES-EN — Implementation Resolution (EN), 19 May 2023]; [SEC — Security Features Standards v1.2, 19 May 2023] v1.2 · [RES — Implementation Resolution, 19 May 2023] Annex 1 p.11; [SEC] Table 3 p.26 (tags 1-5 "from 4th December 2021")* | **PARTIALLY IMPLEMENTED** | A 5-tag TLV/base64 QR (seller name, VAT number, timestamp, total, VAT) is built once at posting and stored (ZatcaService.php:156-168); POS receipts print it from the checkout response. Defects: tag 3 is the draft's created_at; tags 1-2 can be empty or overflow; A4/PDF omit the QR for post-cutover tenants; credit notes have no QR.<br>*Evidence:* C PC-1/PC-2 (ZatcaTest 10 passed); PH1-01, PH1-14 (Probe 1 tag 3); PH1-07/PH2-12 (Probe 1: 260-byte name gives length byte 4); PH1-04/PH2-16 (empty tag 2); PH1-03 (Probe 3: /zatca 403, DocQr empty); PH1-06; PH1-18 (amounts assumed SAR). | Simplified invoices printed on A4 may carry no QR, notes never do, and the QR timestamp and seller data can be wrong or unreadable.<br>*Risk:* Simplified invoices and notes are issued without a valid mandatory QR, defeating buyer and ZATCA verification.<br>*Note:* QR display alone is not evidence of compliance; the stored value must also be correct. | PH1-03, PH1-06, PH1-01, PH1-14, PH1-07, PH2-12, PH1-04, PH2-16, PH1-18<br>**P1** | REM-20, REM-18, REM-13, REM-17, REM-14 |
| **R1-022** · QR (tax invoices)<br>QR is optional on tax invoices in Phase 1 and mandatory from the Integration phase (for cleared tax invoices the QR string is produced by ZATCA at clearance and must be visualised by the taxpayer).<br>*[FAQ-21 — Phase-1 FAQ, 10 Jun 2021]; [RES-EN — Implementation Resolution (EN), 19 May 2023]; [DG-EN — Detailed E-Invoicing Guidelines v2, May 2023] · [FAQ] p.5-6; [RES — Implementation Resolution, 19 May 2023] Annex 2 2.4 p.18; [DG — Detailed E-Invoicing Guidelines v2, May 2023] 6.1 p.30, FAQ p.60* | **PARTIALLY IMPLEMENTED** | Phase 1: standard invoices also receive the 5-tag QR, which is optional (ZatcaService.php:82). Phase 2: AWJ shows its own locally built QR on standard invoices; the ZATCA-returned cleared XML is stored (ZatcaSubmissionDispatcher.php:51) but never read, and invoices are released before clearance.<br>*Evidence:* SUB-04 (grep zatca_cleared_xml in app/ and web/src: model fillable and dispatcher only; E probe P01: QR unchanged after clearance); SUB-01 (E Appendix C: no release path consults clearance). | Cleared tax invoices never show the ZATCA-generated QR, and standard invoices are shared before any ZATCA QR exists.<br>*Risk:* B2B invoices carry a non-ZATCA QR and are not valid cleared tax invoices.<br>*Note:* Phase-1 element met. Once Integration applies, PH1-03 (A4 QR suppressed for new tenants) also affects standard invoices. | SUB-04, SUB-01<br>**P0** | REM-24 |
| **R1-023** · Buyer VAT no.<br>Phase 1 tax invoices must show the buyer's VAT registration number when the buyer is VAT-registered.<br>*[FAQ-21 — Phase-1 FAQ, 10 Jun 2021]; [RES-EN — Implementation Resolution (EN), 19 May 2023] · [FAQ] p.3,5; [RES — Implementation Resolution, 19 May 2023] Annex 2 5.3 p.20* | **PARTIALLY IMPLEMENTED** | A4 and thermal prints show the buyer VAT number when the partner record has one (doc-parties.tsx:39-43,66; thermal-receipt.tsx:38-125), read live from the partner. Nothing requires it on a standard invoice to a registered buyer, and partner VAT is validated only as size:15 (StorePartnerRequest.php:22).<br>*Evidence:* C PC-13; PH1-05 (C Probe 1: explicit standard invoice posted without buyer VAT); PH1-04 (alphabetic VAT 'ABCDEFGHIJKLMNO' stored and classified simplified); PH1-02 (reprint reads the live partner, no snapshot); GM2 R1-092. | The buyer VAT number is not required, validated or frozen on tax invoices to registered buyers.<br>*Risk:* Tax invoices to registered buyers can omit or misstate the buyer VAT number, jeopardising the buyer's input-VAT recovery.<br>*Note:* Buyer VAT is conditional (register section 5 item 8); a configurable warn/block fits CLAUDE.md rule 6. | PH1-05, PH1-04, PH1-02<br>**P1** | REM-15, REM-14 |
| **R1-024** · Archival export<br>Solution must let the taxpayer export generated documents to offline/external archival; exported files named with VAT registration number + issuance date + issuance time + invoice reference number (IRN).<br>*[RES-EN — Implementation Resolution (EN), 19 May 2023]; [DG-EN — Detailed E-Invoicing Guidelines v2, May 2023] · [RES — Implementation Resolution, 19 May 2023] Annex 1 p.10; [DG — Detailed E-Invoicing Guidelines v2, May 2023] 5.4 p.26* | **PARTIALLY IMPLEMENTED** | Export is per invoice only: a client-side PDF — a raster of the on-screen document through `documentExporter`, named with the invoice number (invoices/[id]/page.tsx:502,518; modules/documents/services/export/index.ts:36) and the XML/QR via GET /invoices/{id}/zatca (routes/api.php:678), which returns 403 for tenants without the optional app. There is no bulk or offline archive, no XML for notes, and no VAT-number + date + time + IRN file naming.<br>*Evidence:* PH1-16 (single-invoice retrieval only); CON-09 (no archive format or standard naming); PH1-03 (C Probe 3: /zatca 403 for a fresh tenant); grep 'zatca.*export\|bulk.*xml\|xml.*download' in app/, routes/, web/src/app: none. | Taxpayers cannot export generated invoices and notes to an external archive, and exported files are not named as required.<br>*Risk:* Tenants cannot produce their e-invoice archive in the prescribed form, risking archiving violations (R1-218).<br>*Note:* Resolution Annex 1 lists this among the functionalities mandated from 4 Dec 2021; file name = VAT number + issuance date + issuance time + IRN. | PH1-16, CON-09, PH1-03<br>**P1** | REM-36, REM-24, REM-20 |
| **R1-025** · Tamper-resistance<br>Solution must be tamper-proof/tamper-resistant and include a mechanism that prevents tampering and reveals tampering attempts by the user or any third party.<br>*[REG — E-Invoicing Regulation, 4 Dec 2020]; [RES-EN — Implementation Resolution (EN), 19 May 2023] · [REG] Art. 5(A)(3), p.4; [RES — Implementation Resolution, 19 May 2023] Third(2)(C)(1), p.4* | **PARTIALLY IMPLEMENTED** | Posted invoices cannot be edited, deleted or re-posted through the service/API (InvoiceService.php:162-164,462-464,889-892), and each invoice XML is hashed into a PIH chain (ZatcaService.php:69-81). The model and database still allow updates and hard deletes, the revision log skips ZATCA fields and deletions, notes have no hash, and branch-restricted users can edit and issue other branches' invoices.<br>*Evidence:* C PC-4, B PC-3/PC-13, D PC-1/PC-3; PH1-09/SEC-05 (C Probe 1, F04: model update/delete succeed); ACC-05 (B probe); ACC-10 (RETENTION_DAYS 730); RecordsRevisions.php:32-50 (no deleted hook); PH1-06/PH2-05; SEC-01 (F05). | Tampering outside the service layer is neither prevented nor revealed (no integrity check, partial and prunable log); notes lack tamper evidence; branch/role boundaries can be bypassed for writes.<br>*Risk:* Issued e-invoices can be altered or destroyed undetected, which makes the solution non-compliant under the Regulation.<br>*Note:* DG 5.3/5.4 (Generation phase) require recording and detecting tampering attempts and list the anti-tampering mechanisms: counter, date, deletion/modification, uncontrolled access. | PH1-09, SEC-05, ACC-05, ACC-10, PH1-06, PH2-05, SEC-01<br>**P0** | REM-07, REM-33, REM-18, REM-03 |
| **R1-026** · Immutability<br>Solution must protect generated e-invoices/notes from alteration or deletion and allow saving/archiving in the Annex format without internet.<br>*[RES-EN — Implementation Resolution (EN), 19 May 2023]; [DG-EN — Detailed E-Invoicing Guidelines v2, May 2023] · [RES — Implementation Resolution, 19 May 2023] Third(2)(C)(2), p.4; [DG — Detailed E-Invoicing Guidelines v2, May 2023] 5.4 p.26 ("Once invoices are generated, they should not be deleted …* | **PARTIALLY IMPLEMENTED** | Service/API immutability for posted invoices (InvoiceService.php:162-164,462-464); credit notes and returns expose no update/delete routes (routes/api.php:722-725,1431-1434). Models remain editable/deletable, reprints read live party data, and archiving is limited to per-invoice PDF/XML downloads (notes have no XML) with no offline archive.<br>*Evidence:* C PC-4; InvoiceTest::a_posted_invoice_cannot_be_edited/_deleted; PH1-09/SEC-05/ACC-05 (probes: model update/delete succeed); PH1-02 (reprint changes after a tenant/partner edit); PH1-16, CON-09 (no archive or bulk export); PH2-05. | Protection holds only on HTTP/service paths; reprinted content can change; invoices and notes cannot be archived offline in the prescribed format.<br>*Risk:* Generated e-invoices can be altered or deleted and cannot be archived as prescribed (violations #12/#13, R1-217/R1-218).<br>*Note:* DG 5.4 (Generation phase) asks for archiving 'in XML format without Internet connection'; Resolution Annex 1 leaves the Phase-1 format open. | PH1-09, SEC-05, ACC-05, PH1-02, PH1-16, CON-09, PH2-05<br>**P0** | REM-07, REM-14, REM-36, REM-24, REM-18 |
| **R1-027** · Prohibited functions (P1)<br>Prohibited from 4 Dec 2021: anonymous access; operating with default password; absence of user session management; allowing alteration/deletion of generated documents; log modification/deletion; inaccurate timestamps; non-sequential log generation; invoice counter reset; ability to generate more than one invoice sequence at any time.<br>*[RES-EN — Implementation Resolution (EN), 19 May 2023]; [DG-EN — Detailed E-Invoicing Guidelines v2, May 2023] · [RES — Implementation Resolution, 19 May 2023] Annex 1 pp.14-15; [DG — Detailed E-Invoicing Guidelines v2, May 2023] 5.6 pp.27-28 (definitions, incl. password reset on first use, log all …* | **PARTIALLY IMPLEMENTED** | Met: no anonymous access (all tenant routes behind auth:sanctum), no factory password, no ICV reset feature, one tenant-wide chain. Not met: deactivated users keep their tokens; posted documents are alterable/deletable at model level; the revision log is prunable and skips ZATCA fields; back/future-dated invoices and draft-time QR/XML timestamps; notes outside the chain; no first-use reset of admin-set passwords; logins unlogged.<br>*Evidence:* F Appendix A, F PC-1; SEC-04 (F07); PH1-09/ACC-05/SEC-05; ACC-10 (DocumentRevision.php:56,66-69); PH1-01/PH2-08 (C Probe 3: draft dated 2020-01-01 posted); ACC-04 (B-lock.log: 2027 invoice accepted); PH2-05; UserController.php:143 and StoreUserRequest.php:23 (admin-set password, no must-change flag); AuthController.php:173-201 (token issued, no login record). | Several Annex 1 prohibited functions are enabled: alteration/deletion at model level, log deletion, inaccurate timestamps, and incomplete session management (no login trail, no forced first-use reset, deactivated sessions survive).<br>*Risk:* A solution with any enabled prohibited function is deemed non-compliant (violation #11, R1-216).<br>*Note:* Lead: the Detailed Guidelines §5.6 gaps found in this review — no login trail, no password change at first use for administrator-set passwords, deletions and outputs unlogged, mutable log rows — are raised as CON-13; log pruning is ACC-10. | SEC-04, PH1-09, ACC-05, SEC-05, ACC-10, PH1-01, PH2-08, ACC-04, PH2-05, CON-13<br>**P0** | REM-08, REM-07, REM-33, REM-13, REM-28, REM-18 |
| **R1-028** · Cancellation<br>"Invoices cannot be cancelled." Cancelling is only possible by issuing an associated credit note and re-issuing a new invoice.<br>*[DG-EN — Detailed E-Invoicing Guidelines v2, May 2023] · 5.6 p.27, 6.5 p.34, FAQ p.63* | **PARTIALLY IMPLEMENTED** | Posted invoices cannot be edited, deleted or cancelled through the API; 'cancelled' exists in the enum but no code sets it (B PC-13). Correction is by credit note or sales return, with duplicate() for re-issue. The note need not reference the invoice, can target a draft or another customer and is not an e-note; printed numbered drafts can still be deleted.<br>*Evidence:* C PC-4; B PC-13 (migration 000004:26); InvoiceTest::it_duplicates_a_posted_paid_invoice_into_a_clean_editable_draft; PH1-06/ACC-06 (notes with null reference, against a draft, for another partner); PH2-05; PH1-09 (model delete); PH1-10 (draft printed as 'فاتورة ضريبية', deletable, number reused). | The credit note is not required to be associated with the invoice it cancels, is not an e-note, and documents printed as tax invoices can disappear without any note.<br>*Risk:* De facto cancellations occur without the associated credit note and re-issue that ZATCA requires (R1-217).<br>*Note:* Partial anchor for PH1-10: a printed, numbered draft deleted afterwards is a cancellation outside the credit-note route (see GM1a_conflicts.md). | PH1-06, ACC-06, PH2-05, PH1-09, PH1-10<br>**P0** | REM-18, REM-02, REM-07, REM-34 |
| **R1-029** · Counter (ICV)<br>Solution must have a tamper-resistant invoice counter that cannot be reset or reformatted, increments for each e-invoice/note and is recorded in each document (Annex 2 field 2.5, enforced from Integration).<br>*[RES-EN — Implementation Resolution (EN), 19 May 2023]; [DG-EN — Detailed E-Invoicing Guidelines v2, May 2023] · [RES — Implementation Resolution, 19 May 2023] Third(2)(C)(7) p.5; Annex 2 2.5 p.18; [DG — Detailed E-Invoicing Guidelines v2, May 2023] FAQ p.62 ("Example 1,2,3,4,5...")* | **PARTIALLY IMPLEMENTED** | ICV = max(zatca_icv)+1 under a tenant row lock with a unique (tenant_id, zatca_icv) index, recorded in each invoice's XML (AdditionalDocumentReference ICV) and column (ZatcaService.php:59-75,274-277; migration 000053:69); no reset feature exists. Credit and debit notes never increment it, zatca_icv is mass-assignable, and deleting the latest invoice row would let the next invoice reuse its value.<br>*Evidence:* D PC-2 (PG six-process race gave 1..6); F PC-9; DocumentNumberingTest::the_zatca_counter_cannot_be_duplicated (PG); PH2-05/PH1-06 (D P2: max ICV unchanged after a credit note); PH1-09 (model delete); SEC-05 (Invoice.php:25-37 fillable zatca_icv). | The counter does not cover e-notes and is not tamper-resistant below the service layer, because it is derived from surviving rows.<br>*Risk:* Counter continuity required from Integration can be broken by notes outside the sequence or by row deletion.<br>*Note:* Annex 2 field 2.5 is an Integration-phase field; AWJ already emits it for invoices. | PH2-05, PH1-06, PH1-09, SEC-05<br>**P0** | REM-18, REM-07 |
| **R1-030** · Single sequence<br>A unit must not generate more than one sequence of e-invoices/notes; all documents of a unit are linked by the previous-invoice hash into a single chain.<br>*[RES-EN — Implementation Resolution (EN), 19 May 2023]; [DG-EN — Detailed E-Invoicing Guidelines v2, May 2023] · [RES — Implementation Resolution, 19 May 2023] Third(2)(E)(2) p.5 (definition of "Unit"); [DG — Detailed E-Invoicing Guidelines v2, May 2023] 5.6 p.28* | **PARTIALLY IMPLEMENTED** | One ICV/PIH chain per tenant; a per-branch chain is blocked until branches have their own VAT number and a branch-aware unique index (app/Support/ZatcaIcvScope.php:54-103). The chain excludes credit/debit notes, spans developer, simulation and production environments, and PIH is re-derived from the stored, mutable previous XML (ZatcaService.php:140-151).<br>*Evidence:* C PC-7 (DocumentNumberingTest::the_zatca_counter_stays_tenant_wide_while_numbers_split_by_branch, PG); F PC-9; PH2-05/PH1-06; PH2-10 (ZatcaService.php:69-74, no environment dimension); SUB-10; SEC-06; SEC-05 (F cross-stream note: an XML tamper rewrites the next PIH). | Not every document of the unit is in the chain (notes), and non-production documents become predecessors of production ones.<br>*Risk:* The production sequence starts mid-chain and omits notes, inviting chain-integrity findings once integrated.<br>*Note:* One EGS unit per tenant (one credential per environment, PH2-09); tenants needing several onboarded units cannot be modelled. | PH2-05, PH1-06, PH2-10, SUB-10, SEC-06, SEC-05<br>**P0** | REM-18, REM-25, REM-07 |
| **R1-031** · Connectivity<br>Solution must be able to connect to the internet and to external systems via API.<br>*[REG — E-Invoicing Regulation, 4 Dec 2020]; [RES-EN — Implementation Resolution (EN), 19 May 2023] · [REG] Art. 5(A)(1)-(2), p.4; [RES — Implementation Resolution, 19 May 2023] Third(2)(D), p.5* | **PARTIALLY IMPLEMENTED** | AWJ is an internet-hosted SaaS with a REST API, a key-authenticated public API (routes/api_public.php:93-95,137-138) and an outbound ZATCA transport pinned to the FATOORA endpoints (config/zatca.php:45-54; ZatcaHttpTransport.php:25-38). Dispatch is off by default (config/zatca.php:58) and the shipped deployment has no queue worker, so ZATCA's API is never called; the request also omits the SDK uuid field.<br>*Evidence:* E PC-1/PC-3 (probe P01, faked HTTP); F PC-15; OPS-01 (Dockerfile:47-53 QUEUE_CONNECTION=sync; deploy/entrypoint.sh:98 Apache only); SUB-03 (P01 body keys invoiceHash, invoice). | Integration through the Authority's API is not operational in the shipped deployment, and the request diverges from the SDK contract.<br>*Risk:* Once a tenant's integration date passes, no document can reach ZATCA through AWJ as deployed.<br>*Note:* Phase 1 does not require integration ([FAQ] p.5), but DG 5.3 asks Generation-phase solutions to be able to connect to the Authority's API; the gap bites from Integration. | OPS-01, SUB-03<br>**P1** | REM-23, REM-21 |
| **R1-032** · Cyber-security<br>Solution must observe all KSA data/information-security and cyber-security requirements (e.g. NCA controls may apply).<br>*[REG — E-Invoicing Regulation, 4 Dec 2020]; [DG-EN — Detailed E-Invoicing Guidelines v2, May 2023] · [REG] Art. 5(A)(4); [DG — Detailed E-Invoicing Guidelines v2, May 2023] 5.4 p.26* | **NOT VERIFIED** | Security controls verified in code: tenant isolation (F PC-1), encrypted credentials (F PC-4), TLS verification on (E PC-3), rate limits (F PC-14). The repository holds no NCA ECC/CCC control mapping, hosting attestation or penetration-test evidence.<br>*Evidence:* none (see reason); related verified defects: SEC-01 (F05 cross-branch writes), SEC-04 (F07 deactivated-user tokens); CON-01 (render.yaml:11,18 region frankfurt). | Compliance with the applicable KSA cybersecurity frameworks cannot be established; known access-control defects exist.<br>*Risk:* Non-conformity with NCA or data-protection rules may affect tenants and AWJ as their record custodian.<br>*Note:* Settled by an applicability ruling (which NCA framework binds a private SaaS ERP), a control mapping, production hosting/residency evidence and a penetration test. | SEC-01, SEC-04, CON-01<br>**P1** | REM-03, REM-08, REM-00A, REM-36 |

Authority access is possible only if a tenant creates a user with a custom read-only role (routes/api.php:1639-1642); no auditor-access feature or procedure exists.<br>*Evidence:* CON-01 (lead reading of render.yaml and deploy notes; production region NOT VERIFIED). | The production hosting location and an Authority access path are not evidenced.<br>*Risk:* If production follows the blueprint, tenants' records sit outside KSA without a documented Authority access path.<br>*Note:* Settled by the production evidence pack (region of database, application, storage and backups) and a documented auditor-access procedure. The KSA-location duty itself derives from IR Art. 66(3) (R1-191). | CON-01<br>**P1** | REM-00A, REM-36 |
| **R1-034** · Language<br>Human-readable invoice must be in Arabic (other languages may be added); Arabic (1234) or Hindi (١٢٣٤) numerals both count as Arabic; notes/remarks must also be in Arabic.<br>*[DG-EN — Detailed E-Invoicing Guidelines v2, May 2023]; [TIG-EN — Tax Invoicing & Records Guideline v3, May 2026] · [DG — Detailed E-Invoicing Guidelines v2, May 2023] 5.7 p.28, 6.6 p.36, FAQ pp.60, 63; [TIG — Tax Invoicing & Records Guideline v3, May 2026] 4.2.1 pp.16-17* | **VERIFIED DEFECT** | Document language ar (default), bilingual or en is frozen at posting (PrintTemplateContract.php:47,151; InvoiceService.php:1033). In 'en' mode v2 compositions print English labels only, and legacy compositions follow the UI locale, so an English-only tax invoice can be issued. Western numerals are used, which is acceptable. User-typed notes are not checked for Arabic.<br>*Evidence:* PH1-15 (modern-bilingual-label.tsx:35-36; use-document-label-mode.ts:16-17; doc-header.tsx:24); GM2 R1-082 VERIFIED DEFECT (code level, pinned by six Vitest/jsdom tests; not rendered in a real browser). | Tax invoices and notes can be printed without Arabic.<br>*Risk:* Invoices without Arabic are invalid under IR Art. 53(5) and 66(2).<br>*Note:* Arabic notes/remarks are a taxpayer content obligation; a warning on non-Arabic free text could support it. | PH1-15<br>**P2** | REM-34 |
| **R1-035** · Certification<br>No ZATCA/third-party certification of the solution is required (ZATCA "may enforce such requirement at a later stage"); compliance may be verified by the Authority, a third party, or self-certified.<br>*[FAQ-21 — Phase-1 FAQ, 10 Jun 2021]; [RES-EN — Implementation Resolution (EN), 19 May 2023] · [FAQ] p.11; [RES — Implementation Resolution, 19 May 2023] Third(1) p.3; Eighth(1) p.8* | **NOT APPLICABLE** | No certification is required. AWJ's landing copy nevertheless self-asserts compliance with both phases ('متوافقة مع المرحلتين', web/src/messages/ar.json:2425) and every document prints a ZATCA compliance footer.<br>*Evidence:* none (see reason); PH1-13 (web/src/app/page.tsx:34; ar.json:2186). | none (no certification obligation).<br>*Risk:* none identified<br>*Note:* Self-certification is permitted but verifiable by ZATCA; the current claim is contradicted by the audit evidence (PH1-13) and should be withdrawn before any self-certification. | PH1-13<br>**P2** | REM-34 |
| **R1-036** · Logging<br>All user activities tied to invoice generation must be logged from login onward; logs time-stamped and non-modifiable.<br>*[DG-EN — Detailed E-Invoicing Guidelines v2, May 2023] · 5.6 pp.27-28 (session management; log modification)* | **PARTIALLY IMPLEMENTED** | RecordsRevisions writes created/updated entries (including status, i.e. posting) with user_id and microsecond timestamps for invoices (RecordsRevisions.php:32-50,78-84). It skips ZATCA fields and invoice lines, has no deleted hook (draft deletion at InvoiceService.php:458-467 leaves no trace), its rows are not write-protected and are prunable after 730 days. Logins, print/PDF/share and submission re-dispatches are not logged.<br>*Evidence:* PH1-09 (RecordsRevisions.php:28-30); SEC-05 (InvoiceLine has no revisions); ACC-10 (DocumentRevision.php:38-45,56); OPS-02 (no Log:: calls in Zatca*, re-dispatch actor not recorded); AuthController.php:173-201 and grep 'last_login\|AuditLog\|Events.Login' in app/: nothing for tenant users. | User activity tied to invoice generation is not logged from login onward, deletions are invisible, and the log is neither complete nor immutable.<br>*Risk:* Tenants cannot evidence who did what in the invoicing process, which is an Annex 1 prohibited function (absence of session management, log deletion).<br>*Note:* Lead: the gaps found in this review (no login, logout or failed-login trail for tenant users; no deletion logging; revision rows not write-protected; client-side print/PDF/share/export unlogged) are raised as CON-13; log pruning is ACC-10. | PH1-09, SEC-05, ACC-10, OPS-02, CON-13<br>**P1** | REM-07, REM-33, REM-27 |
| **R1-037** · Phase transition<br>Taxpayers follow Phase 1 until their wave's integration date; Phase 2 may be adopted voluntarily earlier.<br>*[DG-EN — Detailed E-Invoicing Guidelines v2, May 2023] · FAQ p.58* | **PARTIALLY IMPLEMENTED** | Tenants stay in Phase-1 mode until any ZATCA credential row exists; from then on signing is mandatory and fails closed (ZatcaService.php:85-105). There is no explicit Phase-2 enablement, wave date, tenant-facing phase status or audited return to Phase 1 (no credential delete route, routes/api.php:1585-1588).<br>*Evidence:* PH2-07 (ZatcaSignedInvoicePostingTest: production credential with developer active blocks posting); SEC-03 (F06 matrix, no recovery path); PH1-19 (no tenant-facing mode). | The Phase-1 to Phase-2 transition is implicit, cannot be scheduled to the wave date and cannot be reverted.<br>*Risk:* Early or premature switching can halt all invoicing, while tenants cannot see which phase their documents follow.<br>*Note:* Phase-2 mode itself is not functional (R1-010). | PH2-07, SEC-03, PH1-19<br>**P1** | REM-26, REM-38 |

### R1 2.C Phase 2 — Integration (clearance/reporting, stamps, contingency)

| Official requirement (source · section · version) | Status | AWJ implementation · evidence | Gap · risk | Findings · severity | Tasks |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| **R1-038** · Format<br>From the Integration phase, documents must be generated in XML or PDF/A-3 with embedded XML; transmission to ZATCA is XML only.<br>*[RES-EN — Implementation Resolution (EN), 19 May 2023]; [DG-EN — Detailed E-Invoicing Guidelines v2, May 2023] · [RES — Implementation Resolution, 19 May 2023] Second(2) p.2; Third(2)(A)(1), (D)(1) pp.3,5; Seventh(10) p.7; [DG — Detailed E-Invoicing Guidelines v2, May 2023] 4.1.2(b), FAQ p.61* | **PARTIALLY IMPLEMENTED** | Every posted invoice gets a UBL XML (app/Services/Accounting/ZatcaService.php:234-315), stored in invoices.zatca_xml (app/Services/Accounting/InvoiceService.php:1055) and transmitted base64-encoded as XML only (app/Services/Accounting/ZatcaHttpTransport.php:35-38). The builder is self-described as not validated against the XSD (ZatcaService.php:232); credit/debit notes get no XML; no PDF/A-3 output exists.<br>*Evidence:* PH2-04 (SDK -validate S3: 18/18 FAILED at XSD/EN/KSA); PH1-17 (C Probe 1 content inventory); PH2-05 (CreditNoteService.php, ReturnService.php: 0 'zatca' references, own grep); CON-09 (own grep 'pdf/?a-?3\|pdfa\|EmbeddedFile\|AFRelationship' over app/ and web/src: only unrelated pdfAssignment/pdfAlignment identifiers); XML-only transmission: E PC-3, probe P01. | The XML does not conform to the XML Implementation Standard (XSD and business-rule failures); notes are not generated as e-documents; no PDF/A-3 with embedded XML exists, although AWJ shares plain PDFs.<br>*Risk:* Every invoice and note produced after a tenant's integration date is outside the mandated e-invoice format.<br>*Note:* XML-only transmission is correct. PDF/A-3 is optional only when the XML itself is shared and stored; once a PDF is used it must be PDF/A-3 with embedded XML (DG FAQ p.63), and AWJ's PDF output (a raster of the on-screen document) is not. | PH2-04, PH1-17, PH2-05, CON-09<br>**P0** | REM-16, REM-18, REM-24 |
| **R1-039** · Clearance<br>Tax invoices and their notes must be transmitted in real time and *cleared* by ZATCA (validated, cryptographically stamped, QR added) **before** being shared with the customer; an uncleared B2B document is not fully compliant.<br>*[RES-EN — Implementation Resolution (EN), 19 May 2023]; [DG-EN — Detailed E-Invoicing Guidelines v2, May 2023]; [DTG — Detailed Technical Guidelines v2, Nov 2022] · [RES — Implementation Resolution, 19 May 2023] Second(3)(A) pp.2-3; [DG — Detailed E-Invoicing Guidelines v2, May 2023] 2.15 p.12, 6.4 …* | **VERIFIED DEFECT** | Posting a standard invoice sets status posted and stores the locally built QR/XML in one transaction (app/Services/Accounting/InvoiceService.php:1026-1056); print, PDF, share, POS receipt and API expose them immediately. Clearance is a separate manual, flag-gated API call (routes/api.php:681-684; config/zatca.php:58 default off). The cleared XML is stored (app/Services/Accounting/ZatcaSubmissionDispatcher.php:51) but never read.<br>*Evidence:* SUB-01 (E Appendix C: no release path consults clearance; probe P01 'cleared xml exposed? NO'); SUB-04 (own grep zatca_cleared_xml: Invoice.php:34 fillable and dispatcher :51 only); SUB-05 (own grep: no web/mobile caller of zatca/submissions); OPS-01 (QUEUE_CONNECTION=sync, no worker); PH2-04, SUB-03 (submission would fail); PH2-05 (notes); SUB-07 (probe P10). | No clearance gate or posted-to-cleared lifecycle before sharing; buyers never receive the ZATCA-stamped document; notes are never cleared; clearance cannot succeed with the current XML and request; rejected invoices stay in circulation.<br>*Risk:* Every B2B invoice and note handed to buyers is uncleared and invalid from ZATCA's perspective, exposing tenants to penalties and buyers to disallowed input VAT.<br>*Note:* The DG contingency (R1-062) allows sharing an uncleared invoice only after clearance retries fail while ZATCA is down; AWJ shares every standard invoice uncleared by default. | SUB-01, SUB-04, SUB-05, OPS-01, PH2-04, SUB-03, PH2-05, SUB-07<br>**P0** | REM-24, REM-23, REM-16, REM-21, REM-18 |
| **R1-040** · Reporting<br>Simplified tax invoices and their notes must be *reported* to ZATCA within a period not exceeding 24 hours from generation.<br>*[RES-EN — Implementation Resolution (EN), 19 May 2023]; [DG-EN — Detailed E-Invoicing Guidelines v2, May 2023]; [DTG — Detailed Technical Guidelines v2, Nov 2022] · [RES — Implementation Resolution, 19 May 2023] Second(3)(B) p.3; [DG — Detailed E-Invoicing Guidelines v2, May 2023] 2.16 p.12, 6.4 …* | **NOT IMPLEMENTED** | Reporting exists only as a manual per-invoice API action (routes/api.php:681-684, throttle 10/min) behind a dispatch flag that defaults off (config/zatca.php:58). Nothing creates an attempt on posting or POS checkout, routes/console.php has no ZATCA schedule, and submission_mode 'automatic' is stored but never read (app/Support/Settings.php:243). No 24-hour deadline is tracked.<br>*Evidence:* SUB-05 (probe P08: attempt still pending after 3 days, no listing route); own greps: 'zatca/submissions' in web/src and mobile/ none, 'submission_mode' only Settings.php:243 and UpdateZatcaSettingsRequest.php:21; OPS-01, OPS-04 (probe P15); SUB-14 (10/min throttle); PH2-01/02/03/04 (SDK S3 rejects simplified outputs); SUB-03; PH2-05 (notes); PH2-08 (draft time). | No automatic or batch reporting, no 24-hour tracking or alert, no production worker; any report sent would be rejected; simplified credit/debit notes cannot be reported at all.<br>*Risk:* B2C/POS invoices and notes will not reach ZATCA within 24 hours, a recurring violation (R1-214) for every tenant in the integration phase.<br>*Note:* A transport skeleton exists (pinned endpoints, durable attempts, idempotency; E PC-1..PC-12), so the missing parts are orchestration and compliant content. PH2-08: IssueDate is the draft's created_at, so a late-posted draft starts outside the 24-hour window. | SUB-05, OPS-01, OPS-04, SUB-14, PH2-01, PH2-02, PH2-03, PH2-04, SUB-03, PH2-05, PH2-08<br>**P0** | REM-23, REM-27, REM-17, REM-16, REM-21, REM-18, REM-13 |
| **R1-041** · Simplified stamping<br>Simplified invoices must be stamped by the solution with its own CSID and carry the Phase-2 QR (9 tags) before being presented to the buyer; shared immediately as a printed copy (or electronically/other human-readable form by agreement).<br>*[RES-EN — Implementation Resolution (EN), 19 May 2023]; [DG-EN — Detailed E-Invoicing Guidelines v2, May 2023]; [XML — XML Implementation Standard v1.2, 19 May 2023] · [RES — Implementation Resolution, 19 May 2023] Third(2)(A)(3), (C)(4) pp.4; [DG — Detailed E-Invoicing Guidelines v2, May 2023] …* | **VERIFIED DEFECT** | Once any credential row exists, posting signs the invoice locally (XAdES) and builds a Phase-2 QR, adding tag 9 only for simplified documents (app/Services/Accounting/ZatcaService.php:89-105; ZatcaPhaseTwoQrEncoder.php:52-54); POS returns the receipt QR at checkout. Without a credential (the default) only the 5-tag Phase-1 QR exists. Notes are never stamped.<br>*Evidence:* PH2-01, PH2-02, PH2-03 (SDK S3 on signed simplified outputs: [SIGNATURE] 'wrong signature Value', [XSD] SigningCertificateV2, [QR] hashedXml/digital signature/publicKey); D PC-8 (tags 1,2,4,5,9 accepted); PH2-06 (mandatory policy element); PH2-08 (tag 3 source); PH2-09 (CSID not bound to tenant VAT); PH2-05; PH1-03 (C Probe 3: A4/PDF without QR when app disabled). | The solution's stamp and QR fail ZATCA's validator; no onboarding yields a genuine CSID; tag 3 uses the draft time; simplified notes have no stamp or QR; A4/PDF prints of new tenants lack the QR.<br>*Risk:* Every simplified invoice presented to a buyer carries an invalid stamp/QR and would be rejected on reporting.<br>*Note:* Immediate sharing as a printed copy is in place (POS receipt from the checkout response; C PC-11). Fail-closed posting once a credential exists (D PC-5) matches 'stamped before presented'. | PH2-01, PH2-02, PH2-03, PH2-06, PH2-08, PH2-09, PH2-05, PH1-03<br>**P0** | REM-17, REM-13, REM-19, REM-18, REM-20 |
| **R1-042** · Sharing B2B<br>Cleared tax invoices must be shared with customers in XML or PDF/A-3; XML alone suffices if the buyer agrees.<br>*[RES-EN — Implementation Resolution (EN), 19 May 2023]; [DG-EN — Detailed E-Invoicing Guidelines v2, May 2023] · [RES — Implementation Resolution, 19 May 2023] Third(2)(A)(2) p.3; [DG — Detailed E-Invoicing Guidelines v2, May 2023] 6.6 p.36, FAQ p.61* | **NOT IMPLEMENTED** | Buyers receive a browser print or a client-side PDF made by rasterising the on-screen document with html2canvas and jsPDF (web/src/modules/documents/services/export/index.ts:36, web/src/lib/pdf.ts; share action web/src/app/(app)/invoices/[id]/page.tsx:511-520), carrying the locally generated QR and released before clearance. XML-only sharing needs buyer agreement; moot while no XML hand-over exists. | CON-09, SUB-04, SUB-01<br>**P0** | REM-24 |
| **R1-043** · UUID<br>A UUID (128-bit) must be generated for each e-invoice/note in addition to the sequential IRN.<br>*[RES-EN — Implementation Resolution (EN), 19 May 2023] · Third(2)(C)(3) p.4; Annex 1 p.12; Annex 2 2.2 p.18* | **PARTIALLY IMPLEMENTED** | Each invoice gets a v4 UUID at posting (app/Services/Accounting/ZatcaService.php:43), emitted as cbc:UUID (:269) and stored in invoices.zatca_uuid (InvoiceService.php:1052), alongside the sequential number in cbc:ID (:268). | **R1-044** · Hash chain (PIH)<br>Each document carries a hash and embeds the previous document's hash; the chain must be maintained even for documents rejected by ZATCA (platform records rejected hashes).<br>*[RES-EN — Implementation Resolution (EN), 19 May 2023]; [DTG — Detailed Technical Guidelines v2, Nov 2022]; [XML — XML Implementation Standard v1.2, 19 May 2023] · [RES — Implementation Resolution, 19 May 2023] Third(2)(C)(5) pp.4-5; Annex 2 2.3; [DTG] p.48; [XML] BR-KSA-61 p.58* | **PARTIALLY IMPLEMENTED** | Each invoice stores a C14N-1.1 SHA-256 hash identical to the SDK's and embeds the previous hash, re-derived from the previous stored XML (app/Services/Accounting/ZatcaService.php:69-75,140-151) under a tenant-row lock with unique (tenant_id, zatca_icv) (migration 000053:69). Rejected invoices keep their hash and remain the PIH predecessor. Notes are outside the chain, which spans all ZATCA environments.<br>*Evidence:* D PC-1 (19/19 official samples hash-identical; S1/S2); D PC-2/PC-3 and F PC-9 (PG races: ICV 1..N, PIH verified link by link); E PC-9, probe P10 ('next PIH==hash(rejected xml)? true'); PH2-05; PH2-10, SUB-10, SEC-06 (one tenant chain across environments); PH2-11 (genesis encoding); SEC-05 (zatca_xml/zatca_hash updatable at model level); F06 (e) (null previous XML halts posting). | Notes never enter the chain; developer/simulation documents precede production ones in the same chain; genesis PIH differs from ZATCA's; stored-XML integrity is protected only at service level and a broken link has no repair path.<br>*Risk:* Chain discontinuities and unchained notes at go-live can cause rejected or investigated submissions.<br>*Note:* Continuing the chain through rejected documents is implemented as required (E PC-9). Platform enforcement of PIH continuity NOT VERIFIED (D section 4). | PH2-05, PH2-10, SUB-10, SEC-06, PH2-11, SEC-05, SEC-03, PH1-17<br>**P0** | REM-18, REM-25, REM-17, REM-07, REM-26, REM-16 |

Base64, up to 700 characters.<br>*[RES-EN — Implementation Resolution (EN), 19 May 2023]; [SEC — Security Features Standards v1.2, 19 May 2023] · [RES — Implementation Resolution, 19 May 2023] Annex 1 pp.12-13; [SEC] 4.1 pp.25-26 (tags 6-9 "from 1st January 2023")* | **VERIFIED DEFECT** | With a credential, ZatcaPhaseTwoQrEncoder packs tags 1-8 (+9 for simplified) under a 700-character cap (app/Services/Accounting/ZatcaPhaseTwoQrEncoder.php:40-71,95-103); without one, the 5-tag Phase-1 QR is used (ZatcaService.php:156-168). NO'); SUB-04 (no reader of zatca_cleared_xml, own grep); SUB-01 (E Appendix C); PH2-01, PH2-02 (EGS stamp fails SDK signature and XSD checks). | ZATCA's platform stamp on cleared invoices never reaches users or buyers; the EGS stamp on simplified documents is non-compliant.<br>*Risk:* B2B documents carry a non-ZATCA stamp/QR and simplified documents an invalid one, so neither validates.<br>*Note:* A seller's own stamp on standard documents is optional and ZATCA adds its own (R2-059), so stamping standard invoices locally is not itself a defect. | SUB-04, SUB-01, PH2-01, PH2-02<br>**P0** | REM-24, REM-17 |

| **R1-048** · Key protection<br>Stamping key marked non-exportable; disk encryption if stored in software; new key on CSID renewal; exporting the stamping key is a prohibited function; subject persons must preserve CSIDs and components safely.<br>*[RES-EN — Implementation Resolution (EN), 19 May 2023]; [DG-EN — Detailed E-Invoicing Guidelines v2, May 2023] · [RES — Implementation Resolution, 19 May 2023] Annex 1 p.12, p.15; Seventh(8) p.7; [DG — Detailed E-Invoicing Guidelines v2, May 2023] 5.4 p.26* | **PARTIALLY IMPLEMENTED** | Users paste the private key (app/Http/Requests/UpdateZatcaCredentialRequest.php:28); it is stored in an APP_KEY-encrypted hidden column (app/Models/ZatcaCredential.php:15,20; migration 000131:17-18), never returned by the API (ZatcaCredentialService.php:124-146) and used only in memory. AWJ generates no key and has no security module/KMS, non-exportable marking or new-key-on-renewal check.<br>*Evidence:* F PC-4 and F03SecretLeakProbeTest.log (89 assertions, no key or secret on 12 surfaces); D PC-4; PH2-09 (no server-side key generation); ZatcaCredentialService.php:61-73 (a CSID change requires a full set, not a different key); SEC-03 and F06 (f) (APP_KEY rotation: 'The MAC is invalid', posting blocked); deploy/entrypoint.sh:59-62 (temporary APP_KEY generated if unset). | The key is generated outside AWJ, so it cannot be non-exportable; protection relies on a single application key without HSM/KMS; renewal does not force a new key; host disk encryption is unverified.<br>*Risk:* A leaked database plus APP_KEY exposes every tenant's stamping key, and an unmanaged APP_KEY change makes all credentials unusable.<br>*Note:* new_gap: key custody (imported keys, single APP_KEY envelope, no HSM/KMS, no new-key-on-renewal check) is not the subject of any finding; D section 5 flagged it to the security stream without a finding. Production disk encryption NOT VERIFIED. | PH2-09, SEC-03, CON-11<br>**P1** | REM-19, REM-26 |
| **R1-049** · Prohibited functions (P2)<br>Additionally prohibited from the Integration date: option to export the cryptographic stamping key; software time changes / modification of timestamp during issuance.<br>*[RES-EN — Implementation Resolution (EN), 19 May 2023]; [DG-EN — Detailed E-Invoicing Guidelines v2, May 2023] · [RES — Implementation Resolution, 19 May 2023] Annex 1 p.15; [DG — Detailed E-Invoicing Guidelines v2, May 2023] 6.5 p.35* | **PARTIALLY IMPLEMENTED** | No option exports the stamping key: credential APIs return booleans/fingerprint only (app/Services/Accounting/ZatcaCredentialService.php:124-146). Timestamps come from the server clock, but the e-invoice IssueDate/IssueTime and QR tag 3 are the draft's created_at (ZatcaService.php:90,163,253,270-271), and the printed invoice_date is free user input without upper bound (app/Http/Requests/StoreInvoiceRequest.php:34).<br>*Evidence:* F PC-4, F03SecretLeakProbeTest.log (no key egress); PH2-08 (D P2 backdated case: invoice_date 2026-01-15, XML IssueDate 2026-10-10); PH1-01 (C-probe1.log: draft created 2026-10-01 and posted later, XML IssueDate 2026-10-01); ACC-04 (B-lock.log: 2027-dated invoice accepted). | The issuance timestamp is not the actual issuance moment: users shape it through draft timing and can set any visible issue date, including future dates.<br>*Risk:* This matches the prohibited 'modification of timestamp during issuance' function, which makes the solution non-compliant (violation #11, R1-216).<br>*Note:* The key-export prohibition is met. Production clock, APP_TIMEZONE and NTP NOT VERIFIED; readiness has no clock-skew check (OPS-03). | PH2-08, PH1-01, ACC-04<br>**P1** | REM-13, REM-28 |
| **R1-050** · Connectivity/queue<br>Encrypted authenticated connection (TLS); periodic batch upload for simplified documents; real-time submission with response for tax invoices; if offline, documents are queued, the solution remains operational and documents are reported after reconnection.<br>*[RES-EN — Implementation Resolution (EN), 19 May 2023] · Annex 1 pp.13-14* | **PARTIALLY IMPLEMENTED** | Submissions use pinned HTTPS FATOORA endpoints with Basic auth and default TLS verification (config/zatca.php:45-54; app/Services/Accounting/ZatcaHttpTransport.php:25-38). Stamping is local, so posting and POS continue while ZATCA is unreachable. There is no batch upload, no real-time clearance at issuance and no queue that reports documents after reconnection: attempts are manual, terminal on failure, and production has no worker.<br>*Evidence:* E PC-1/PC-3, F PC-6 (endpoint pinning, no withoutVerifying); SUB-05 (no automatic attempts; routes/console.php has no ZATCA command, own grep); OPS-01 (QUEUE_CONNECTION=sync, Apache-only entrypoint); OPS-04 (probe P15: failed job leaves attempt pending); SUB-06 (probes P04/P05: timeout terminal, TLS error non-retryable); SUB-01. | No periodic batch reporting, no real-time clearance at issuance, no durable offline queue with automatic catch-up after reconnection.<br>*Risk:* Documents issued during an outage are never reported unless an operator resubmits each one by hand.<br>*Note:* The encrypted, authenticated connection element is met. Live TLS/gateway behaviour NOT VERIFIED (no network). | SUB-05, OPS-01, OPS-04, SUB-06, SUB-01<br>**P0** | REM-23, REM-27, REM-22, REM-24 |
| **R1-051** · API auth<br>Integration through ZATCA APIs; OAuth 2 basic authentication where the client ID is the CSID certificate and a secret issued at onboarding; "the secret value is stored securely and not disclosed to third parties".<br>*[RES-EN — Implementation Resolution (EN), 19 May 2023]; [SEC — Security Features Standards v1.2, 19 May 2023] · [RES — Implementation Resolution, 19 May 2023] Third(2)(D)(2) p.5; [SEC] 5 p.27* | **VERIFIED IMPLEMENTED** | Reporting/clearance calls send Authorization: Basic base64(binary_security_token:secret) with accept-version v2 (app/Services/Accounting/ZatcaHttpTransport.php:25-31), using only a configured, production-stage, unexpired CSID of the active environment (ZatcaTransportCredentialResolver.php:27-53). The secret sits in the APP_KEY-encrypted hidden column (app/Models/ZatcaCredential.php:15,20) and is never returned (ZatcaCredentialService.php:124-146).<br>*Evidence:* E PC-2/PC-3 (matches DEVELOPER-PORTAL-MANUAL.txt:1371-1373); probe P01 'authorization scheme=Basic'; F PC-4/PC-6 and F03SecretLeakProbeTest.log (89 assertions: no secret in responses, attempt payloads, jobs, failed_jobs, notifications, logs); ZatcaTransportCredentialResolverTest 3/3 and ZatcaHttpTransportTest 5/5 (SQLite and PG); PH2-09 text: 'correct field mapping'. | none<br>*Risk:* none identified<br>*Note:* Live gateway acceptance NOT VERIFIED (network forbidden). Neighbouring issues, not gaps of this row: credentials are obtained outside AWJ (PH2-09); APP_KEY rotation makes the secret unusable (SEC-03); the request body lacks uuid (SUB-03). | —<br>**—** | — |
| **R1-052** · Incident notification<br>Subject persons must notify ZATCA (through the means it specifies) of any incident, technical error or emergency hindering generation or integration, notify again when it is resolved, and promptly resume generation and clearance/reporting of affected documents.<br>*[RES-EN — Implementation Resolution (EN), 19 May 2023]; [DG-EN — Detailed E-Invoicing Guidelines v2, May 2023] · [RES — Implementation Resolution, 19 May 2023] Seventh(5) p.7; [DG — Detailed E-Invoicing Guidelines v2, May 2023] 7.4 p.38, 10 pp.45-52 ("dedicated form on ZATCA's website")* | **NOT IMPLEMENTED** | AWJ keeps no incident or outage record, offers no support for ZATCA's failure-notification process and has no bulk catch-up of affected documents. Failures surface as one in-app alert per failed attempt (app/Services/Accounting/ZatcaNotificationBridge.php:57-109); recovery is a per-invoice manual re-dispatch throttled at 10/min (routes/api.php:681-684); generation halts with no recovery path on CSID expiry or chain gaps.<br>*Evidence:* CON-05 (lead search of app/, docs/, deploy/); own grep 'failure.?notification\|incident\|outage\|contingency\|manual invoice' over app/, deploy/, docs/: no ZATCA-related hit; OPS-02 (probe P08: no tenant-wide backlog); OPS-04 (probe P15); SUB-14; SEC-03 (F06 blast-radius matrix). | No incident log (start, resolution, affected documents) to support notifying ZATCA twice, and no prompt bulk resumption of clearance/reporting after the incident.<br>*Risk:* Tenants cannot evidence or remediate outages, so delayed documents become reporting violations plus the separate failure-to-notify violation (R1-219).<br>*Note:* Filing the notification on ZATCA's form is the taxpayer's act; AWJ's part is detection, an incident/evidence log and catch-up. The register note cites R1-218 for this penalty; the matching row is R1-219 (see conflicts). | CON-05, OPS-02, OPS-04, SUB-05, SUB-14, SEC-03<br>**P0** | REM-27, REM-23, REM-26 |

Whether one cloud EGS per tenant suffices is NOT VERIFIED. | PH2-09, PH2-10<br>**P1** | REM-19, REM-25 |

Trigger evidence: PH1-13 (copy cited above); D headline (SDK S3: 18/18 FAILED); E Appendix C (no clearance gate). | none (taxpayer obligation); AWJ is not yet a compliant Phase-2 solution (see findings).<br>*Risk:* none identified for AWJ directly; tenants relying on the compliance claim would be using a non-compliant solution.<br>*Note:* ZATCA's vendor list rests on vendor declarations or verification; any AWJ declaration should wait until SDK validation passes and clearance/reporting run end to end. | PH1-13, PH2-01, PH2-02, PH2-03, PH2-04, PH2-05, PH2-09, SUB-01, SUB-05<br>**P0** | REM-34, REM-17, REM-16, REM-18, REM-19, REM-24, REM-23 |
| **R1-055** · Waves<br>ZATCA determines target groups; each is notified of its integration procedures at least 6 months before the due date (via official e-mail and SMS registered with ZATCA).<br>*[RES-EN — Implementation Resolution (EN), 19 May 2023]; [DG-EN — Detailed E-Invoicing Guidelines v2, May 2023] · [RES — Implementation Resolution, 19 May 2023] Sixth(1) p.6; [DG — Detailed E-Invoicing Guidelines v2, May 2023] FAQ p.57* | **NOT APPLICABLE** | ZATCA selects target groups and notifies them at least 6 months ahead. | **R1-056** · Rejected documents<br>If clearance fails (HTTP 400) the taxpayer corrects and submits the XML "just like a new invoice having its own Invoice Counter Value and Hash"; the rejected document's hash/counter must not be changed or reused.<br>*[DG-EN — Detailed E-Invoicing Guidelines v2, May 2023] · FAQ p.61, p.63; p.53 (code table)* | **PARTIALLY IMPLEMENTED** | A rejected invoice keeps its ICV and hash: the posted XML is frozen and the next invoice's PIH is the hash of the rejected XML (app/Services/Accounting/ZatcaService.php:69-75,140-151); ICVs are unique per tenant (migration 000053:69). No correction flow exists: a new attempt re-sends the byte-identical XML (ZatcaSubmissionService.php:48-71, request_hash of the same zatca_xml), and the rejected original stays posted and printable.<br>*Evidence:* E PC-9 and probe P10 ('next PIH==hash(rejected xml)? true'; 'resubmission of rejected invoice resends identical XML: true => rejected'); SUB-07; SUB-01 (no release gate, E Appendix C); PH2-05 (no e-invoiced credit note to neutralise the original); SEC-05, PH1-09 (F04 and C Probe 1: zatca_xml/zatca_hash updatable at model level). | No guided correct-and-re-issue flow producing a new document with its own UUID/ICV/hash; the identical rejected XML can be resent; the rejected standard invoice is neither blocked nor marked; non-alteration of the rejected hash is enforced only at service level.<br>*Risk:* Rejected B2B invoices stay in circulation and corrections loop on identical rejections, so no valid tax invoice is issued for the supply.<br>*Note:* Workaround today: duplicate and post the invoice (new UUID/ICV/hash), leaving the original posted in the ledger. DTG FAQ p.77: the re-issued standard invoice gets an updated date; a re-issued simplified invoice keeps the transaction date. | SUB-07, SUB-01, PH2-05, SEC-05, PH1-09<br>**P0** | REM-24, REM-18, REM-07 |
| **R1-057** · Duplicates<br>The same document may not be submitted twice (not rejected at submission time, but not allowed).<br>*[DG-EN — Detailed E-Invoicing Guidelines v2, May 2023] · FAQ p.60* | **PARTIALLY IMPLEMENTED** | Submissions require an Idempotency-Key (unique per tenant), allow one open attempt per invoice and none after acceptance, and dispatch sends once under a row lock with no transport auto-retry (app/Services/Accounting/ZatcaSubmissionService.php:20-85; ZatcaSubmissionDispatcher.php:23-27; ZatcaHttpTransport.php:34). But a new attempt after 'rejected' resends the same document, and a retry after a timeout resends it without reconciliation.<br>*Evidence:* E PC-4/PC-5/PC-6 and F PC-8 (PG: 6 concurrent creators give 1 row; 4 dispatchers give 1 HTTP send); probe P10 (rejected document resent unchanged); SUB-06 (probe P04: identical body resent after timeout, no in-doubt state); SUB-09 (HTTP call inside the DB transaction, lost acceptance later re-sent); SUB-13 (PG scenario E: no DB constraint for one open attempt). | The same rejected document can be submitted again; after a timeout there is no in-doubt state or reconciliation; the single-open-attempt rule is not enforced in the schema.<br>*Risk:* Repeated submission of the same document, which ZATCA does not allow, mainly for rejected invoices.<br>*Note:* Re-sending after 429/5xx or a timeout is ZATCA's documented action and duplicates are counted once by UUID and hash (R2-057, R2-061; DTG FAQ p.77), so the clear breach is resending a 400-rejected document unchanged (SUB-07). | SUB-07, SUB-06, SUB-09, SUB-13<br>**P1** | REM-24, REM-22 |
