AWJ's own XML would trigger the same warnings on every invoice.<br>*Evidence:* E probe P03 ('202 accepted REPORTED', 0 notifications); OPS-02 (no tenant-wide view or logging, probe P08); SUB-15 (b) (response_message is a constant); PH2-04 (SDK S3 WARN on 18/18: BR-08, BR-KSA-08, BR-CO-10/13; standard shape: BR-KSA-10, BR-KSA-15, BR-KSA-81); CON-07 (no supply date); PH1-05 (buyer data absent); F PC-11 (platform plane has no invoice/ZATCA access). | Warnings are not surfaced, tracked or routed for correction, there is no channel to the solution provider, and the generator would produce the same warnings continuously.<br>*Risk:* Continuous warnings invite ZATCA investigation or audit of tenants and may become rejections as rules tighten.<br>*Note:* How the production platform treats each warning is NOT VERIFIED (SDK 2021-08-19 rule set only). | OPS-02, SUB-15, PH2-04, CON-07, PH1-05<br>**P0** | REM-27, REM-21, REM-16, REM-29, REM-15 |
| **R1-059** · Clearance off<br>HTTP 303 "Clearance switched off": tax invoices must be submitted via the Reporting API; no stamp/QR returned; document is reported instead.<br>*[DG-EN — Detailed E-Invoicing Guidelines v2, May 2023]; [DTG — Detailed Technical Guidelines v2, Nov 2022] · [DG — Detailed E-Invoicing Guidelines v2, May 2023] p.53; [DTG] 4.3.2 note p.51, FAQ p.70* | **VERIFIED DEFECT** | A 303 without a Location header is classified as a non-retryable rejection with a 'ZATCA rejected this invoice' notification (app/Services/Accounting/ZatcaHttpTransport.php:83-85; ZatcaNotificationBridge.php:80-82); with a Location header the HTTP client follows it. Submission type is fixed by document type and re-checked before sending (ZatcaSubmissionService.php:37-41; ZatcaSubmissionDispatcher.php:89-96), so a standard invoice cannot be rerouted to the Reporting API.<br>*Evidence:* SUB-11 (probe P03: every status other than 200/202/408/429/5xx becomes rejected with a notification; 303 follows the same code path); SUB-02 (probe P12: a 302 was followed and the result recorded as accepted); code read of the cited lines; GM6 R2-057 (303 neither rerouted nor distinguished). | No handling of 'clearance switched off': the invoice is mislabelled rejected (or a redirect is followed) instead of being reported through the Reporting API with the seller's own stamp and QR.<br>*Risk:* While ZATCA has clearance switched off, standard invoices are never reported and operators are told to correct valid invoices.<br>*Note:* When rerouted, the document needs the seller's stamp and QR; AWJ adds them to standard invoices only when a credential exists (ZatcaService.php:89). Whether ZATCA's 303 carries a Location header is NOT VERIFIED (E section 4). | SUB-11, SUB-02<br>**P2** | REM-21 |

Production PHP and web-server request-size limits NOT VERIFIED. | SUB-11<br>**P2** | REM-21 |
| **R1-061** · Contingency B2C<br>If ZATCA is unresponsive the business keeps issuing simplified invoices and keeps retrying reporting at regular intervals until successful, keeping evidence of attempts; if it cannot report within 24 h it must notify ZATCA via the dedicated web form and report once connectivity is restored.<br>*[DG-EN — Detailed E-Invoicing Guidelines v2, May 2023] · 10 pp.46, 48* | **PARTIALLY IMPLEMENTED** | Simplified invoices and POS sales continue while ZATCA is unreachable because stamping is local (POS creates and posts in one call, app/Services/Accounting/PosService.php:241-252). | **R1-065** · Advance payments<br>An invoice issued on receipt of an advance has type code 386; subsequent invoices (388) adjust it by PrepaidAmount (BT-113, VAT-inclusive) with references (IRN, issue date/time; UUID optional) and per-VAT-category prepayment lines (KSA-31..34); amounts rounded to 2 decimals; same currency as the invoice; if the adjustment makes "Amount due" negative and a refund is due, a credit note must be issued and cleared/reported. Arithmetic checks: KSA-32 …<br>*[DG-EN — Detailed E-Invoicing Guidelines v2, May 2023]; [XML — XML Implementation Standard v1.2, 19 May 2023] · [DG — Detailed E-Invoicing Guidelines v2, May 2023] 8 pp.40-43; [XML] 9.5* | **NOT IMPLEMENTED** | No prepayment document exists: InvoiceTypeCode is always 388 (app/Services/Accounting/ZatcaService.php:272) and the XML builder has no PrepaidAmount or prepayment lines (:234-315). Customer receipts post to accounts receivable only, with no VAT (PaymentService.php:350-371 per CON-03). A refund credit note could not be cleared or reported either.<br>*Evidence:* CON-03 (lead grep for 386/PrepaidAmount in app/: none); own grep 'PrepaidAmount\|386\|prepayment' over app/: none; D Appendix A (BT-113 PrepaidAmount not emitted); GM5 R2-031 NOT IMPLEMENTED; PH2-05 (credit notes not e-invoiced). | No 386 advance invoice, no PrepaidAmount/KSA-31..34 adjustment on the final invoice, no VAT at receipt of an advance, and no clearable/reportable credit note for refunds.<br>*Risk:* Tenants taking deposits cannot issue the mandatory advance invoice, and output VAT on advances is declared late.<br>*Note:* IR Art. 53(1)(a)(2) makes the advance invoice itself mandatory (R1-075). | CON-03, PH2-04, PH2-05<br>**P0** | REM-29, REM-16, REM-18 |
| **R1-066** · VAT groups<br>Members of a tax group must put the group's VAT registration number on all invoices; devices are onboarded under the group VAT number by the group representative.<br>*[RES-EN — Implementation Resolution (EN), 19 May 2023]; [TIG-EN — Tax Invoicing & Records Guideline v3, May 2026]; [DTG — Detailed Technical Guidelines v2, Nov 2022] · [RES — Implementation Resolution, 19 May 2023] Annex 2 4.3 p.19; [TIG — Tax Invoicing & Records Guideline v3, May 2026] FAQ 11 …* | **PARTIALLY IMPLEMENTED** | The seller VAT number on QR, XML and print is the single tenants.vat_number (app/Services/Accounting/ZatcaService.php:162,296; app/Models/Tenant.php:20), so a group member can enter the group's number. There is no VAT-group attribute (group number vs member TIN) and no onboarding, so units cannot be onboarded under the group number by the representative.<br>*Evidence:* Own grep 'vat_group\|tax_group\|group_vat\|vatgroup\|group_member' over app/, database/, web/src: none; Tenant.php:20 (single vat_number and cr_number); PH2-09 (no onboarding; CSID VAT not bound to tenant VAT); PH1-04 (VAT number not format-validated); PH1-02 (reprints read live master data). | Group membership is not modelled: nothing ensures the group number is used, onboarding under it is impossible, and changing the VAT number on joining a group alters reprints of earlier invoices.<br>*Risk:* Group members may invoice or onboard devices under the wrong VAT number, and earlier invoices may reprint with a number they were not issued under.<br>*Note:* Per R2-053, joining a VAT group revokes existing CSIDs and all units must be re-onboarded under the group TRN; AWJ has no onboarding flow (PH2-09). | PH2-09, PH1-04, PH1-02<br>**P1** | REM-19, REM-14 |
| **R1-067** · Self-billing (P2)<br>Self-billed tax invoices are submitted for clearance by the *buyer*; a self-billing agreement approved by ZATCA is mandatory; self-billing is only allowed where both parties are VAT-registered, is not allowed in simplified invoices, and not for export invoices.<br>*[DTG — Detailed Technical Guidelines v2, Nov 2022]; [RES-EN — Implementation Resolution (EN), 19 May 2023]; [XML — XML Implementation Standard v1.2, 19 May 2023] · [DTG] 4.3.2 p.50; [RES — Implementation Resolution, 19 May 2023] Annex 2 1.2 p.17; [XML] BR-KSA-07 p.48* | **NOT IMPLEMENTED** | AWJ has no self-billing mode: the transaction code is only 0100000/0200000 (app/Services/Accounting/ZatcaService.php:254), and there is no buyer-side clearance of self-billed invoices, no self-billing agreement record and no check that both parties are VAT-registered.<br>*Evidence:* PH2-13 (transaction flags unsupported); own grep 'self.?bill' over app/ and web/src: none; GM2 R1-087 NOT IMPLEMENTED (same gap at the legal level); GM5 R2-004 (self-billing limits cannot be breached). | Customers that self-bill their suppliers cannot issue and clear self-billed tax invoices through AWJ.<br>*Risk:* Tenants operating under a ZATCA-approved self-billing agreement must use another solution for those invoices.<br>*Note:* Becomes NOT APPLICABLE if self-billing is declared out of product scope, as PH2-13 recommends. The prohibitions (no simplified or export self-billing) cannot be breached today. | PH2-13<br>**P2** | REM-16 |
| **R1-068** · Document types<br>Allowed invoice type codes: 388 (tax invoice), 386 (prepayment invoice), 383 (debit note), 381 (credit note); subtype "01" standard / "02" simplified; transaction flags for third-party, nominal, export, summary, self-billed in the `name` attribute.<br>*[XML — XML Implementation Standard v1.2, 19 May 2023] · 11.2.1 pp.30-31; BR-KSA-06/07 p.48* | **PARTIALLY IMPLEMENTED** | Only type code 388 is produced (app/Services/Accounting/ZatcaService.php:272), with subtype 01 (standard) or 02 (simplified) from the stored document-type snapshot (:254). No 381 credit note, 383 debit note or 386 prepayment invoice exists, and the third-party, nominal, export, summary and self-billed flags are always 0.<br>*Evidence:* D PC-9 (subtype snapshot; SDK S3 passes the basic @name); D Appendix A rows InvoiceTypeCode and KSA-2; PH2-05, PH1-06 (no note e-documents); CON-03 (no 386); PH2-13 (flags); PH1-05 (01/02 choice advisory); GM5 R2-002/R2-003 PARTIALLY IMPLEMENTED. | Credit notes, debit notes and prepayment invoices cannot be issued as e-documents, special transactions are mis-flagged as ordinary, and the 01/02 subtype is not validated against buyer data.<br>*Risk:* Notes, advances, exports and other special supplies cannot be cleared or reported with the correct type and flags.<br>*Note:* Technical detail is carried by R2-002/R2-003 (GM5). | PH2-05, PH1-06, CON-03, PH2-13, PH1-05<br>**P0** | REM-18, REM-29, REM-16, REM-15 |

The builder states it is not XSD-validated (ZatcaService.php:232); CI runs only php artisan test (.github/workflows/ci.yml:207-212).<br>*Evidence:* D PC-1 (19/19 hash match); SDK S3 (18/18 FAILED: XSD, EN, KSA, QR, SIGNATURE) and the lead's run-lead calibration; PH2-01..04, PH2-06, PH2-11, PH2-13; PH2-08 (IssueDate semantics); PH1-17; PH2-05; PH2-14 (145 tests, none against official samples or the SDK); CON-10 (Security standard requirement 14). | The XML Implementation Standard, Security Features Standard and Data Dictionary are not met beyond the hash, and no conformance gate exists.<br>*Risk:* No AWJ e-invoice can pass compliance checks, so tenants cannot lawfully integrate using AWJ.<br>*Note:* Where D3 and the SDK diverge (policy identifier, certificate chain), this row makes the standards binding while the SDK is a verification tool (R1-071); see GM1b_conflicts.md and GM6 conflicts 1-2. | PH2-01, PH2-02, PH2-03, PH2-04, PH2-05, PH2-06, PH2-08, PH2-11, PH2-13, PH2-14, PH1-17, CON-10<br>**P0** | REM-17, REM-16, REM-18, REM-13, REM-11, REM-26 |
| **R1-070** · Storage format (P2)<br>Once in the Integration phase, e-invoices must be generated *and stored* in XML or PDF/A-3 (with embedded XML).<br>*[DG-EN — Detailed E-Invoicing Guidelines v2, May 2023] · FAQ p.61; 6.3 p.31* | **PARTIALLY IMPLEMENTED** | Generated XML is stored per invoice (invoices.zatca_xml; app/Services/Accounting/InvoiceService.php:1055) and ZATCA's cleared XML in zatca_cleared_xml (ZatcaSubmissionDispatcher.php:51); both are included in the owner's JSON export (app/Http/Controllers/Api/AccountSettingsController.php:117-174; Invoice has no hidden fields). The stored XML is non-compliant, notes have none, no PDF/A-3 is stored, and stored XML is protected only at service level.<br>*Evidence:* PH2-04, PH1-17 (SDK S3 failures); PH2-05; CON-09 (no PDF/A-3, own grep); SUB-04 (cleared XML stored, never served); SEC-05, PH1-09 (F04 and C Probe 1: model-level updates of zatca_xml/zatca_hash succeed); PH1-16 (no archive or bulk XML export); consistent with GM4 R1-195 and GM5 R2-037. | E-invoices are not stored in the prescribed XML format, notes are not stored at all, PDF/A-3 is absent, and stored XML can be altered outside the service layer.<br>*Risk:* Tenants cannot produce compliant stored e-invoices on request (archiving violation #13, R1-218).<br>*Note:* Storage location (KSA residency) is assessed under R1-191 (CON-01). | PH2-04, PH1-17, PH2-05, CON-09, SUB-04, SEC-05, PH1-09, PH1-16<br>**P0** | REM-16, REM-18, REM-24, REM-07, REM-36 |
| **R1-071** · Toolkit<br>ZATCA provides sandbox, SDK and web validator to verify that solutions generate compliant documents (means, not an obligation).<br>*[DG-EN — Detailed E-Invoicing Guidelines v2, May 2023]; [DTG — Detailed Technical Guidelines v2, Nov 2022] · [DG — Detailed E-Invoicing Guidelines v2, May 2023] 2.18 p.12; [DTG] 2.2* | **NOT APPLICABLE** | ZATCA-provided means, not an obligation. | **R1-072** · Fields rejected/warned<br>Examples: VAT rate must be 0.00-100.00 with max 2 decimals (no % sign); issue date ≤ current date; base quantity > 0; missing previous invoice hash → warning.<br>*[DG-EN — Detailed E-Invoicing Guidelines v2, May 2023] · 10 pp.54-55* | **PARTIALLY IMPLEMENTED** | Input validation keeps line VAT rates as integers 0-100 and quantities as integers 1-1,000,000 (app/Http/Requests/StoreInvoiceRequest.php:61,67); PIH is always emitted (app/Services/Accounting/ZatcaService.php:278-283); XML IssueDate is the draft creation date and so never in the future. BaseQuantity (BT-149) is optional and not emitted. | PH2-04, ACC-04, PH2-08, PH2-11<br>**P0** | REM-16, REM-28, REM-13, REM-17 |

### R1 2.D Tax invoice and simplified tax invoice — issuance rules and content (VAT IR Art. 53 + Resolution Annex 2)

| Official requirement (source · section · version) | Status | AWJ implementation · evidence | Gap · risk | Findings · severity | Tasks |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| **R1-075** · When tax invoice required<br>A tax invoice (≥ para-5 details) must be issued for: (1) taxable supplies to another taxable person, a non-taxable legal person, a sole establishment, or any other entity established in KSA; (2) receipt of any consideration from those persons *before* the supply (advances); (3) intra-GCC supplies or export of goods; (4) zero-rated services to a customer not resident in any GCC state.<br>*[IR — VAT Implementing Regulations, consolidated Apr 2025] · Art. 53(1)(a), p.49 (as amended by Res. 21-2-7, 9 Nov 2021)* | **PARTIALLY IMPLEMENTED** | Each invoice stores a standard/simplified type (zatca_document_type). Default is standard only when the partner has a 15-digit VAT number, else simplified (app/Services/Accounting/InvoiceService.php:444-446); any explicit value overrides it (app/Http/Requests/StoreInvoiceRequest.php:19). No advance-payment invoice, export/intra-GCC or zero-rated-service classification exists.<br>*Evidence:* PH1-05 (C Probe 1 'standard w/o buyer VAT' posted; ZatcaTest::an_explicit_document_type_overrides_the_customer_suggestion); CON-03 (receipts post to AR only); VAT-03 (grep VATEX\|tax_category\|exempt in app/: none); PH2-13 (ZatcaService.php:254 only 0100000/0200000); partners.entity_type exists (app/Models/Partner.php:31) but the classifier ignores it. | Classification is advisory and keyed only to a VAT-number regex, so a non-registered legal person defaults to simplified; no tax invoice on advances; exports, intra-GCC supplies and zero-rated services to non-GCC residents cannot be identified or invoiced as such.<br>*Risk:* B2B, legal-person, export and advance transactions can receive the wrong document type or none, undermining input-VAT recovery and zero-rating evidence.<br>*Note:* Refinement inside PH1-05 scope: entity_type='commercial' without VAT number is classified simplified although IR 53(1)(a)(1) requires a tax invoice for non-taxable legal persons. Settled by a classification rule on entity_type plus posting checks. | PH1-05, CON-03, VAT-03, PH2-13<br>**P1** | REM-15, REM-29, REM-12, REM-16 |
| **R1-076** · Deadline (tax invoice)<br>In all 53(1)(a) cases the tax invoice must be issued no later than the 15th day of the month following the month in which the supply occurred.<br>*[IR — VAT Implementing Regulations, consolidated Apr 2025]; [TIG-EN — Tax Invoicing & Records Guideline v3, May 2026] · [IR] Art. 53(1)(b), p.49; [TIG — Tax Invoicing & Records Guideline v3, May 2026] 4.5 p.25* | **NOT IMPLEMENTED** | Invoices have no supply-date field and no issue timestamp (app/Models/Invoice.php:25-37; no delivery/supply date column in invoice migrations); invoice_date is user-entered and may be back- or future-dated. Delivery notes record delivery_date but the invoice builder does not carry it (app/Services/Accounting/DeliveryNoteSalesInvoiceDraftBuilder.php:98,423-429).<br>*Evidence:* ACC-04 (B-lock.log: back-dated and 2027-dated invoices accepted); PH1-01 (issue moment not recorded; QR/XML use draft created_at); grep -rniE 'uninvoiced\|issue_deadline\|invoicing_deadline' app/: none. | No capture of the supply date and no warning or report for supplies (e.g. uninvoiced delivery notes) approaching the 15th-of-following-month deadline; back-dating can mask late issuance.<br>*Risk:* Late tax invoices go undetected and back-dated invoice dates can misstate the tax point, exposing the taxpayer to invoicing-violation penalties.<br>*Note:* New gap: supply-date capture and an issuance-deadline control for 53(1)(a) supplies (invoice analogue of CON-02). The deadline itself is a taxpayer obligation; the ERP support is missing. | ACC-04, PH1-01, CON-07<br>**P1** | REM-28, REM-13, REM-29 |
| **R1-077** · Simplified for B2B < 1,000<br>By exception, a simplified tax invoice may be issued for 53(1)(a) supplies whose value is less than SAR 1,000.<br>*[IR — VAT Implementing Regulations, consolidated Apr 2025]; [DG-EN — Detailed E-Invoicing Guidelines v2, May 2023] · [IR] Art. 53(1)(c), p.49; [DG — Detailed E-Invoicing Guidelines v2, May 2023] 4.2.1(b) p.17; [TIG — Tax Invoicing & Records Guideline v3, May 2026] 4.3.1 p.23* | **PARTIALLY IMPLEMENTED** | Simplified type can be chosen for any buyer by explicit override (app/Services/Accounting/InvoiceService.php:431-437; StoreInvoiceRequest.php:19). No value threshold exists anywhere in the classifier (InvoiceService.php:429-447); POS auto-classifies VAT-registered buyers as standard regardless of value.<br>*Evidence:* PH1-05; ZatcaTest::an_explicit_document_type_overrides_the_customer_suggestion (C-ZatcaTest.log, 10 passed); C Probe 3 '[C-POS] POS VAT-registered buyer: standard'. | The SAR 1,000 ceiling for simplified invoices to 53(1)(a) customers is neither enforced nor warned; B2B supplies of SAR 1,000 or more can be issued as simplified.<br>*Risk:* Business customers can receive simplified invoices above the threshold, which is non-compliant and blocks their input-VAT recovery.<br>*Note:* B2C simplified invoices have no cap, so absence of a cap is correct there. A configurable warn/block at the threshold fits CLAUDE.md rule 6. | PH1-05<br>**P1** | REM-15 |
| **R1-078** · When simplified required<br>A simplified tax invoice (≥ para-8 details) must be issued for taxable supplies to persons other than those in 53(1)(a) (i.e. consumers/natural persons not subject to tax) and for advances received from them.<br>*[IR — VAT Implementing Regulations, consolidated Apr 2025] · Art. 53(7)(a), pp.50-51* | **PARTIALLY IMPLEMENTED** | Partners without a 15-digit VAT number default to simplified (InvoiceService.php:444-446); POS walk-in sales are created and posted as simplified in one transaction (app/Services/Accounting/PosService.php:242-253). Advances received from consumers produce no invoice; receipts post to AR only.<br>*Evidence:* C Probe 3 '[C-POS] POS walk-in: simplified, posted, qr=true'; C PC-11 PosCheckoutIdempotencyTest 7 passed; CON-03; PH1-05 (type stays overridable). | No simplified invoice is issued on receipt of consumer advances; the type is advisory and can be overridden.<br>*Risk:* VAT on consumer deposits/advances is not invoiced or declared at the correct tax point. | CON-03, PH1-05<br>**P1** | REM-29, REM-15 |
| **R1-079** · Timing (simplified)<br>Simplified tax invoices must be issued on the date of supply or on receipt of consideration (in whole or part), whichever is earlier; exception: licensed banks/financial institutions/insurers (and others designated by Governor decision) may issue by the 15th of the following month if the invoice adds customer name/address and supply date.<br>*[IR — VAT Implementing Regulations, consolidated Apr 2025]; [TIG-EN — Tax Invoicing & Records Guideline v3, May 2026] · [IR] Art. 53(7)(b)-(c), p.51; [TIG — Tax Invoicing & Records Guideline v3, May 2026] 4.5 p.25* | **PARTIALLY IMPLEMENTED** | POS checkout creates and posts the simplified invoice at the sale (PosService.php:242-253). Back-office simplified invoices are drafts posted later with a user-chosen invoice_date, and the XML/QR issue time is the draft created_at (app/Services/Accounting/ZatcaService.php:163,253). No invoice is issued on receipt of advance consideration.<br>*Evidence:* C PC-11 (PosCheckoutIdempotencyTest 7 passed); PH1-01/PH2-08 (C Probe 1: draft 2026-10-01, invoice_date 2026-09-15, posted 2026-10-05, QR tag3 2026-10-01T09:00); ACC-04 (B-lock.log future/back-dated accepted); CON-03. | Outside POS nothing ties issuance to the supply or payment date; the recorded issue time is draft creation; advances are not invoiced.<br>*Risk:* Non-POS simplified invoices can be issued after the supply/receipt date with misleading timestamps, breaching IR 53(7)(b).<br>*Note:* Bank/insurer 15th-of-month exception not modelled (only relevant if financial institutions onboard). IEG 11.1 wording conflict is register-internal; IR prevails. | PH1-01, PH2-08, ACC-04, CON-03<br>**P1** | REM-13, REM-28, REM-29 |
| **R1-080** · Summary tax invoice<br>A taxable person may issue one summary tax invoice for several separate supplies to one customer within a period not exceeding one calendar month, containing at least the 53(5) details, by the 15th of the following month; separate invoices may not also be issued for the included supplies.<br>*[IR — VAT Implementing Regulations, consolidated Apr 2025]; [TIG-EN — Tax Invoicing & Records Guideline v3, May 2026] · [IR] Art. 53(4), p.50; [TIG — Tax Invoicing & Records Guideline v3, May 2026] 4.4 pp.24-25* | **PARTIALLY IMPLEMENTED** | Up to 50 confirmed delivery notes of one customer and warehouse can be consolidated into one invoice draft, and already-invoiced notes are refused (DeliveryNoteSalesInvoiceDraftBuilder.php:41,308-320,337). There is no summary flag, no billing-period dates, no one-calendar-month window, and the invoice date defaults to today (:423-429).<br>*Evidence:* Code read app/Services/Accounting/DeliveryNoteSalesInvoiceDraftBuilder.php as cited; PH2-13 (KSA-2 flags only 0100000/0200000, ZatcaService.php:254); PH2-04 (no Delivery/ActualDeliveryDate, BR-KSA-15 WARN). | Consolidation may span several months with no 1-month window or 15th-of-next-month deadline; the XML cannot carry the summary flag or the supply period (BR-KSA-71/72).<br>*Risk:* Consolidated invoices may be late or cover more than one month, and summary invoices cannot be reported with the required flag and period.<br>*Note:* New gap: period-window/deadline control on delivery-note consolidation is not covered by any finding. Positive: separate invoicing of already-included supplies is blocked (already_invoiced). | PH2-13, PH2-04, CON-07<br>**P0** | REM-16, REM-29 |
| **R1-081** · Summary simplified<br>Persons enjoying the 53(7)(c) exception may issue a summary simplified invoice for one customer per calendar month (with customer name/address and the period).<br>*[IR — VAT Implementing Regulations, consolidated Apr 2025] · Art. 53(7)(d), p.51* | **NOT APPLICABLE** | No summary-simplified mode; AWJ has no bank/insurer-specific invoicing regime.<br>*Evidence:* none (see reason); ZatcaService.php:254 emits only basic transaction codes (PH2-13). | none (permission reserved to persons enjoying the IR 53(7)(c) exception)<br>*Risk:* none identified<br>*Note:* Rule for a different actor (licensed banks, financial institutions, insurers, Governor-designated persons). If such tenants are onboarded, summary-flag support (PH2-13) plus customer/period fields would be needed. | —<br>**—** | — |
| **R1-082** · Language (legal)<br>The tax invoice must be in Arabic in addition to any other language it is issued in.<br>*[IR — VAT Implementing Regulations, consolidated Apr 2025] · Art. 53(5) chapeau, p.51; Art. 66(2), p.61* | **VERIFIED DEFECT** | Document language ar/en/bilingual is frozen at posting (app/Support/PrintTemplateContract.php:47; InvoiceService.php:379-386). ar and bilingual render Arabic; en renders English-only field labels in v2 compositions, and legacy compositions follow the UI locale.<br>*Evidence:* PH1-15 (modern-bilingual-label.tsx:35-36; use-document-label-mode.ts:16-17; doc-header.tsx:24); code-level evidence; six Vitest (jsdom) tests render the v2 compositions and pin the English-only output (`use-document-label-mode.test.tsx:38`, `document-modern.test.tsx:436`, `document-classic-v2.test.tsx:164`, `document-erp-v2.test.tsx:163`, `document-minimal-v2.test.tsx:174`, `document-retail-v2.test.tsx:178`); not rendered in a real browser (C §4 item 4). | An English-only tax invoice (no Arabic) can be issued and printed.<br>*Risk:* Invoices without Arabic are non-compliant with IR 53(5) chapeau/66(2), and their content is not in the conclusive language.<br>*Note:* Defaults ar/bilingual comply. PH1-15 cites IR Art. 53(1); the register places the rule in Art. 53(5) chapeau and 66(2) (see GM2_conflicts.md). | PH1-15<br>**P2** | REM-34 |
| **R1-083** · Tax invoice content<br>Mandatory details: (a) issue date; (b) sequential number identifying the invoice; (c) supplier TIN; (d) customer TIN if the customer self-accounts, with a statement to that effect; (e) names and addresses of supplier and customer; (f) quantity and nature of goods / scope and nature of services; (g) supply date if different from issue date; (h) taxable amount per rate or exemption, unit price excl. VAT, and any discounts/rebates not included in …<br>*[IR — VAT Implementing Regulations, consolidated Apr 2025] · Art. 53(5)(a)-(l), p.50* | **PARTIALLY IMPLEMENTED** | Print/PDF/thermal show number, invoice_date, seller name/VAT/CR/national address, buyer name/VAT/city, line description/qty/unit price/price-before-tax/line VAT/line total, and subtotal/discount/shipping/adjustment/VAT/'الإجمالي شامل الضريبة' (web/src/modules/documents/builder/from-invoice.ts:146-195; doc-items-table.tsx:211-244; doc-totals.tsx:103-134).<br>*Evidence:* Code read as cited; app/Support/PrintTemplateContract.php:72-83 (no rate/discount column; only description,total mandatory); web/src/messages/ar.json:2184 and presentation/visual-v2.ts:131 VAT label fixed '(15%)'; the invoice PDF is a raster of the on-screen document (`documentExporter`), so it carries the same label; PH1-01, PH1-02, PH1-04, PH1-05, PH1-18, VAT-03, PH1-17/PH2-04. | (a) XML/QR date differs from printed date; (c) seller VAT optional/unvalidated; (e) buyer address never printed; (g) no supply-date field; (h) no taxable amount per rate, line discounts not printed; (i) no per-line rate, label always 15%; (j) amounts follow tenant currency; (k) no treatment narration; (l) no margin statement; templates may hide qty/price/VAT columns.<br>*Risk:* Issued tax invoices omit or misstate IR 53(5) particulars, exposing the seller to invoice violations and buyers to input-VAT denial.<br>*Note:* New gap (print layer): no VAT-rate or line-discount column exists in the print contract, the VAT label is hard-coded '(15%)' even for 0%/mixed invoices, and only description/total columns are mandatory. Item (d) (self-accounting customer statement) rarely applies to resident suppliers. | PH1-01, PH1-02, PH1-04, PH1-05, PH1-18, VAT-03, PH1-17, PH2-04, CON-06<br>**P0** | REM-13, REM-14, REM-15, REM-12, REM-16, REM-34 |
| **R1-084** · Simplified content<br>Mandatory details: (a) issue date; (b) supplier name, address and TIN; (c) description of goods/services; (d) consideration payable; (e) VAT payable **or** a statement that the consideration includes VAT.<br>*[IR — VAT Implementing Regulations, consolidated Apr 2025]; [TIG-EN — Tax Invoicing & Records Guideline v3, May 2026] · [IR] Art. 53(8), p.51; [TIG — Tax Invoicing & Records Guideline v3, May 2026] 4.3.2 pp.23-24* | **PARTIALLY IMPLEMENTED** | The thermal/A4 simplified invoice prints seller name, VAT, CR and national address when set, number, date, item description, qty x price, line totals, VAT amount and 'الإجمالي شامل الضريبة' (web/src/modules/documents/templates/thermal-receipt.tsx:38-63,98-117; from-invoice.ts:146-157).<br>*Evidence:* Code read as cited; PH1-04 (C Probe 1: tenant without VAT posts with empty VAT; Probe 3: '12345' accepted); PH1-02 (seller data read live on reprint; no snapshot columns). | Seller VAT/address are optional, unvalidated and not frozen at issue, so a simplified invoice can be issued or reprinted without a valid TIN or address; the VAT label always states 15%.<br>*Risk:* Simplified invoices lacking a valid supplier TIN or address breach IR 53(8)(b).<br>*Note:* Wrong title on POS receipts is assessed under R1-101 (PH1-11). Items (c)-(e) are met for tenants with complete master data. | PH1-04, PH1-02<br>**P1** | REM-14 |
| **R1-085** · Governor's additions<br>The Governor may amend the required details and add details for e-invoicing purposes → Annex 2 fields are legally binding additions.<br>*[IR — VAT Implementing Regulations, consolidated Apr 2025] · Art. 53(9), p.51* | **PARTIALLY IMPLEMENTED** | Posting generates a UBL XML with UUID, ICV, PIH and QR and stores its hash (ZatcaService.php:38-108), but the XML omits most Annex-2 fields (tax breakdown, categories, seller address/CRN, buyer address/VAT/ID, supply date, payment means, allowances) and notes are not generated.<br>*Evidence:* PH2-04 (SDK 238-R3.4.8: 18/18 GLOBAL FAILED; D Appendix A matrix); PH1-17 (C Probe 1 XML inventory); PH2-05; VAT-03; PH2-13; D PC-1 (hash identical to SDK on 19/19 samples). | The Annex-2 data set is largely absent from the e-invoice; special invoice types and notes are unsupported.<br>*Risk:* E-invoices lack legally binding Annex-2 details, so none can pass ZATCA validation or evidence compliance.<br>*Note:* Meta row; field-level gaps are detailed in R1-092, R1-095 to R1-101, R1-113, R1-115 and R1-116. | PH2-04, PH1-17, PH2-05, VAT-03, PH2-13<br>**P0** | REM-16, REM-18, REM-12 |
| **R1-086** · Electronic issuance (legal)<br>Tax invoices must be issued automatically/electronically in the cases determined by a regulation of the Minister/Board that is in force at the time of supply (→ E-Invoicing Regulation).<br>*[IR — VAT Implementing Regulations, consolidated Apr 2025]; [TIG-EN — Tax Invoicing & Records Guideline v3, May 2026] · [IR] Art. 53(6), p.50; [TIG — Tax Invoicing & Records Guideline v3, May 2026] 6.3 p.36* | **PARTIALLY IMPLEMENTED** | Posting an invoice always generates an electronic artefact set (UUID, ICV, PIH, XML, hash, 5-tag QR) in the same transaction (ZatcaService.php:38-108), but compliance.zatca is an optional application disabled for new tenants, and credit notes/returns generate nothing.<br>*Evidence:* PH1-19 (behaviour); PH1-03 (ApplicationCatalog.php:91-96 mandatory=false; C Probe 3: GET /invoices/{id}/zatca 403, A4 printed without QR); PH1-06/PH2-05 (credit_notes has no zatca columns); PH1-17; SEC-08. | Electronic issuance is not mandatory per tenant, notes are outside the e-invoice pipeline, and the generated XML is a non-validated stub.<br>*Risk:* Tax invoices and notes are issued without the compliant electronic form required by the E-Invoicing Regulation. | PH1-03, PH1-06, PH2-05, PH1-17, SEC-08<br>**P0** | REM-20, REM-18, REM-16 |
| **R1-087** · Self-billing (legal)<br>A taxable customer may issue tax invoices on the supplier's behalf only with ZATCA's approval, a prior agreement, an indication on the invoice that it is issued by the customer on behalf of the supplier, both parties registered; the invoice is treated as the supplier's; the agreement must set acceptance procedures and the supplier's undertaking not to issue invoices for those supplies; both parties notify ZATCA.<br>*[IR — VAT Implementing Regulations, consolidated Apr 2025]; [TIG-EN — Tax Invoicing & Records Guideline v3, May 2026] · [IR] Art. 53(2), p.49; [TIG — Tax Invoicing & Records Guideline v3, May 2026] 5.1 pp.27-29 (wording "Self-Billing Tax Invoice / Issued on Behalf of the Supplier")* | **NOT IMPLEMENTED** | No self-billing mode: no 'issued by the customer on behalf of the supplier' indicator, no self-billing agreement record and no self-billed transaction flag. The purchase-side 'إشعار مدين' is a buyer note, not a self-billed tax invoice.<br>*Evidence:* PH2-13 (ZatcaService.php:254 only 0100000/0200000); grep -rniE 'self[-_ ]?bill\|on[-_ ]behalf' app/: only delivery-platform constants; database/migrations/2025_01_01_000039_add_type_to_credit_notes.php:28 (type sales\|purchase). | Customers cannot issue compliant self-billed invoices (statement, agreement, flag) through AWJ.<br>*Risk:* Tenants that self-bill suppliers would issue documents lacking the mandatory self-billing statement and flag.<br>*Note:* Needed only where a tenant has a ZATCA-approved self-billing arrangement; declare as unsupported scope until built. | PH2-13<br>**P2** | REM-16 |
| **R1-088** · Third-party billing<br>A third party may issue tax invoices on the supplier's behalf after ZATCA approval; all obligations must be met; the supplier remains responsible for accuracy and output tax; the invoice carries the supplier's details/VAT number (not the third party's) and states it is issued on behalf of the supplier; ZATCA notified.<br>*[IR — VAT Implementing Regulations, consolidated Apr 2025]; [TIG-EN — Tax Invoicing & Records Guideline v3, May 2026] · [IR] Art. 53(3), p.49; [TIG — Tax Invoicing & Records Guideline v3, May 2026] 5.2 pp.30-31* | **NOT IMPLEMENTED** | No third-party billing mode (flag, on-behalf statement). For delivery platforms, profiles whose invoice responsibility is 'platform_on_behalf' or 'platform_as_supplier' are blocked from posting (app/Services/DeliveryFinancialRoleGate.php:42-47; app/Models/DeliveryPlatformProfileVersion.php:48-50).<br>*Evidence:* PH2-13 (transaction flags absent, ZatcaService.php:254); code read DeliveryFinancialRoleGate.php:40-66 (fail-closed decision). | A tenant acting as, or relying on, a third-party issuer cannot produce the third-party flag and on-behalf statement.<br>*Risk:* Third-party-issued invoices would lack the required on-behalf statement and flag.<br>*Note:* Positive: AWJ fails closed instead of issuing platform-on-behalf invoices it cannot model. | PH2-13<br>**P2** | REM-16 |
| **R1-089** · TIN on invoices<br>The taxable person must show its tax identification number on tax invoices and in all VAT correspondence with ZATCA.<br>*[IR — VAT Implementing Regulations, consolidated Apr 2025] · Art. 57, p.54* | **PARTIALLY IMPLEMENTED** | Seller VAT number is printed when present (from-invoice.ts:148; thermal-receipt.tsx:39-41), placed in QR tag 2 and XML CompanyID (ZatcaService.php:296), but it is nullable and unvalidated (app/Http/Requests/UpdateCompanyRequest.php:18) and posting has no precondition on it.<br>*Evidence:* PH1-04 (C Probe 1: vat_number=null, invoice posted, QR tag2 empty; Probe 3: '12345' accepted); PH2-16; PH1-02 (printed VAT read live, may differ from stored QR). | Invoices can be issued with an empty or malformed TIN; reprints may show a TIN different from the stored QR.<br>*Risk:* Invoices without a valid TIN breach IR Art. 57/53(5)(c) and block buyers' input-VAT recovery.<br>*Note:* VAT correspondence with ZATCA is outside AWJ (no filing function). | PH1-04, PH2-16, PH1-02<br>**P1** | REM-14 |
| **R1-090** · Head office name<br>Invoices are issued in the name of the head office as on the registration certificate; the branch name may be added; where several CRs exist, the seller's additional ID must be the CR of the branch issuing the invoice.<br>*[TIG-EN — Tax Invoicing & Records Guideline v3, May 2026]; [RES-EN — Implementation Resolution (EN), 19 May 2023] · [TIG — Tax Invoicing & Records Guideline v3, May 2026] FAQ 15 p.57; [RES — Implementation Resolution, 19 May 2023] Annex 2 4.4 note p.20* | **PARTIALLY IMPLEMENTED** | Invoices are issued in the tenant's single name, VAT and CR (from-invoice.ts:146-150; ZatcaService.php:296-297); branches hold name/address but no CR or VAT (app/Models/Branch.php:22-26) and are not printed.<br>*Evidence:* PH1-02 (Tenant.php:19-23: no legal/trade-name distinction, no snapshot); PH2-04 (no seller PartyIdentification/CRN, BR-KSA-08 WARN 18/18); Branch.php:22-26 code read. | Nothing ensures the name equals the certificate legal name; for multi-CR taxpayers the issuing branch CR cannot be recorded or emitted as seller additional ID.<br>*Risk:* Branch invoices may carry the wrong legal name or CR, a common audit finding for multi-branch taxpayers.<br>*Note:* New gap: branch-level CR (seller additional ID per issuing branch) is not modelled. Adding the branch name is optional. | PH1-02, PH2-04<br>**P0** | REM-14, REM-16 |
| **R1-091** · Seller master data<br>Seller name = legal name on the VAT certificate; address = principal place of business or relevant establishment per the National Address; the address must be in KSA; VAT number as on certificate (or group number).<br>*[TIG-EN — Tax Invoicing & Records Guideline v3, May 2026] · 4.2.3 p.19* | **PARTIALLY IMPLEMENTED** | Company profile stores name, vat_number, cr_number, national-address parts (building, street, additional no., district, city, postal code, short address) and country (app/Support/CompanyProfile.php:18-36), all nullable free text (UpdateCompanyRequest.php:17-30); A4 prints the national address (from-invoice.ts:150).<br>*Evidence:* Code read as cited; PH1-04 (VAT unvalidated, C Probe 3); PH1-02 (read live, not frozen); PH2-04 (seller PostalAddress absent in XML, BR-08/BR-KSA-37 WARN); PH2-16. | No legal-name field, no VAT-format, building/postal-format or KSA-country validation; seller address absent from XML and not frozen at issue.<br>*Risk:* Invoices may carry non-certificate names, foreign or malformed addresses and invalid VAT numbers.<br>*Note:* New gap: national-address format and KSA-country validation of seller master data is not covered by any finding (address element rules are R1-227 in another slice). | PH1-04, PH1-02, PH2-04, PH2-16<br>**P0** | REM-14, REM-16 |
| **R1-092** · Buyer identification<br>Tax invoices must carry buyer name and address (mandatory from Phase 1), buyer VAT number if registered (conditional), and—if the buyer is not VAT-registered—one additional ID in this priority order: TIN, CR, MOMRA licence, MLSD licence, 700 number, SAGIA/MISA licence, National ID, GCC ID, Iqama, Passport (Phase 2, conditional). A missing additional ID → accepted with warnings; master data should be completed.<br>*[RES-EN — Implementation Resolution (EN), 19 May 2023]; [TIG-EN — Tax Invoicing & Records Guideline v3, May 2026]; [DG-EN — Detailed E-Invoicing Guidelines v2, May 2023] · [RES — Implementation Resolution, 19 May 2023] Annex 2 5.1-5.4 pp.20-21; [TIG — Tax Invoicing & Records Guideline v3, May 2026] …* | **PARTIALLY IMPLEMENTED** | Partners store name, vat_number(15), cr_number, address parts, country and entity_type (database/migrations/2025_01_01_000003_create_partners_and_products.php:17-33; Partner.php:30-34). Print shows buyer name, VAT and city only (from-invoice.ts:160-164); XML carries buyer RegistrationName only (ZatcaService.php:300-304).<br>*Evidence:* PH1-05 (C Probe 1: 'XML contains buyer VAT?: false'); PH1-02 (buyer address never printed); PH2-04 (BT-46/48/50-55 absent; BR-KSA-10/81 WARN); grep -rniE 'national_id\|iqama\|passport' database/migrations: none. | Buyer address is never printed or emitted, buyer VAT is absent from the XML, other-ID types (national ID, Iqama, passport, 700, MISA) are not modelled, and standard invoices post without them.<br>*Risk:* Standard invoices lack mandatory buyer identification, producing warnings/rejections and weak audit evidence.<br>*Note:* Per R1-092 a missing additional ID is accepted with warnings; buyer name and address are mandatory. | PH1-05, PH1-02, PH2-04<br>**P0** | REM-15, REM-14, REM-16 |
| **R1-093** · Export invoices<br>Buyer VAT number is not mandatory for export invoices and must not exist in an export invoice; a foreign address is permitted for a KSA-VAT-registered buyer.<br>*[RES-EN — Implementation Resolution (EN), 19 May 2023]; [XML — XML Implementation Standard v1.2, 19 May 2023]; [DG-EN — Detailed E-Invoicing Guidelines v2, May 2023] · [RES — Implementation Resolution, 19 May 2023] Annex 2 5.3 p.20; [XML] BR-KSA-46 p.57; [DG — Detailed E-Invoicing Guidelines v2, …* | **NOT IMPLEMENTED** | No export or intra-GCC invoice classification, no zero-rate reason and only basic transaction codes (ZatcaService.php:254); partners.country is stored (migration 000003:33) but not used by any VAT or invoice rule.<br>*Evidence:* VAT-03 (grep VATEX\|tax_category\|exempt in app/: none; probe S9); PH2-13; grep -rniE 'is_export\|zero_rated\|VATEX' app/Models app/Services/Accounting app/Http/Requests: none. | Export invoices cannot be marked, buyer VAT cannot be suppressed by rule, and the export zero-rating reason cannot be recorded.<br>*Risk:* Exports are invoiced as ordinary 0% or 15% lines without export treatment, risking loss of zero-rating.<br>*Note:* AWJ never emits buyer VAT in XML today (accidental compliance with 'must not exist'); the print still shows it if stored. | VAT-03, PH2-13<br>**P1** | REM-12, REM-16 |
| **R1-094** · Citizens EDU/HEA<br>On simplified invoices for private education or private healthcare to Saudi citizens, the buyer name and buyer National ID are mandatory (the State bears the VAT; treated as zero-rated with VATEX-SA-EDU/HEA).<br>*[RES-EN — Implementation Resolution (EN), 19 May 2023]; [XML — XML Implementation Standard v1.2, 19 May 2023]; [DG-EN — Detailed E-Invoicing Guidelines v2, May 2023] · [RES — Implementation Resolution, 19 May 2023] Annex 2 (simplified) 5.1, 5.3 p.28; [XML] BR-KSA-49 p.57; [DG — Detailed E-Invoicing …* | **NOT IMPLEMENTED** | No VAT category/VATEX model (no VATEX-SA-EDU/HEA), and partners have no national-ID field; simplified XML carries the buyer name only (ZatcaService.php:300-304).<br>*Evidence:* VAT-03 (no category/reason code anywhere in app/); PH2-04 (buyer ID BT-46 absent); grep -rniE 'national_id\|iqama\|passport' database/migrations: none; Partner.php:30-34. | Citizen private education/healthcare supplies cannot carry the buyer National ID and VATEX-SA-EDU/HEA zero-rating.<br>*Risk:* Private schools and clinics would charge 15% or zero-rate without the mandatory citizen identification.<br>*Note:* Relevant only to education and healthcare tenants. | VAT-03, PH2-04<br>**P0** | REM-12, REM-16 |
| **R1-095** · Line-item fields<br>Tax invoice lines: description (P1); item code (optional); unit price (P1); quantity (P1); line discount % (conditional, P2) and amount (conditional, P1); subtotal excl. VAT (P2); VAT rate (conditional—mandatory unless out-of-scope/exempt; P1); VAT category code (P2); VAT amount (P1); subtotal incl. VAT (P2); line charge (conditional); prepayment subtotal/VAT/rate (conditional).<br>*[RES-EN — Implementation Resolution (EN), 19 May 2023] · Annex 2 7.1-7.15 pp.21-22* | **PARTIALLY IMPLEMENTED** | invoice_lines store description, qty, unit_price, tax_rate, line_discount, line_tax, line_total (migration 000004:52-59). Print shows description, qty, unit price, price-before-tax, line VAT and line total (doc-items-table.tsx:211-244). XML lines carry only quantity, pre-discount LineExtensionAmount, name and (possibly gross) price (ZatcaService.php:240-249).<br>*Evidence:* Code read as cited; PrintTemplateContract.php:72-83 (no rate/discount column; only description,total mandatory); VAT-03; PH2-04 (BR-CO-04, BR-KSA-52/53 ERROR); VAT-06 (A-invoice-engine.log S3c/S15 XML); VAT-07 (line_tax pre-discount); CON-03. | No VAT category code; print omits line VAT rate and line discount and lets templates hide qty, price and VAT; XML lacks line VAT, category, net price, allowances, VAT-inclusive subtotal and prepayment lines.<br>*Risk:* Mandatory line particulars are missing on the printed and electronic invoice, causing rejection and invalid invoices.<br>*Note:* New gap (print): Annex-2 7.6 and 7.8 visible fields (line discount amount, VAT rate) cannot be printed; REQUIRED_ITEM_COLUMNS = description,total only (PrintTemplateContract.php:83). | VAT-03, PH2-04, VAT-06, VAT-07, PH1-17, CON-03, CON-06<br>**P0** | REM-12, REM-16, REM-10, REM-29, REM-34 |
| **R1-096** · Totals<br>Invoice-level discount % (P2 cond.) and amount (P1 cond.); taxable amount per rate/exemption (P2); VAT total "must be in SAR" (P1); gross total incl. VAT with statement "Amount includes VAT" (P1); document charges; advance-payment adjustment; rounding amount (optional, "required if populated"); amount due for payment (mandatory when prepayment or rounding used).<br>*[RES-EN — Implementation Resolution (EN), 19 May 2023] · Annex 2 8.1-8.9 pp.22-23 (tax invoice), pp.29-30 (simplified)* | **PARTIALLY IMPLEMENTED** | Header stores subtotal, discount, shipping, adjustment (rounding), tax_amount and total (migration 000004:27-32). A4 prints these with 'الإجمالي شامل الضريبة' (doc-totals.tsx:103-134; ar.json:2185); thermal prints subtotal, VAT and grand total only (thermal-receipt.tsx:110-116).<br>*Evidence:* Code read as cited; VAT-06 (S4/S15: TaxExclusive + Tax differs from TaxInclusive, BR-CO-15 ERROR); VAT-03 (no per-category breakdown); PH2-04 (no TaxSubtotal/AllowanceTotal/PayableRounding); PH1-18 (non-SAR currency); CON-03; VAT-07 (header VAT truncated). | No taxable amount per rate/exemption on print or XML; VAT total not forced to SAR; no advance-payment adjustment; XML omits allowance/charge/rounding totals; thermal omits discount, shipping and adjustment rows.<br>*Risk:* Invoice and XML totals do not reconcile under Annex 2 and BR-CO rules, triggering rejections and audit queries.<br>*Note:* New gap (print): per-rate taxable amount (Annex-2 8.3, visible) absent; thermal receipts drop discount/shipping/adjustment, so the printed subtotal plus VAT does not equal the total when they exist. | VAT-06, VAT-03, PH2-04, PH1-18, CON-03, VAT-07, PH1-17, CON-06<br>**P0** | REM-16, REM-12, REM-14, REM-29, REM-10, REM-34 |
| **R1-097** · Payment means<br>Payment means (cash, card, transfer, credit, other) is mandatory on tax invoices from Phase 2 (not required to be visible); optional on simplified invoices; payment terms and supplier bank details optional.<br>*[RES-EN — Implementation Resolution (EN), 19 May 2023]; [XML — XML Implementation Standard v1.2, 19 May 2023] · [RES — Implementation Resolution, 19 May 2023] Annex 2 9.1-9.3 pp.23-24, 30-31; [XML] 11.2.5 (UNCL4461)* | **NOT IMPLEMENTED** | Invoices record payment_type (cash/credit) and payment_method (migration 000004:21; 000055:48), but the XML has no cac:PaymentMeans (ZatcaService.php:234-315). R2-021/Data Dictionary mark BT-81 optional (BR-KSA-16 warning) while RES Annex 2 9.1 (p.23) makes it mandatory for tax invoices; see GM2_conflicts.md. | PH2-04, PH1-17<br>**P0** | REM-16 |
| **R1-098** · Special treatment narration<br>Where tax is not charged at the standard rate, a narration "tax treatment applied to the supply" is required (conditional, Phase 1, visible on tax invoices).<br>*[RES-EN — Implementation Resolution (EN), 19 May 2023]; [IR — VAT Implementing Regulations, consolidated Apr 2025] · [RES — Implementation Resolution, 19 May 2023] Annex 2 11.1 p.24; [IR] 53(5)(k)* | **NOT IMPLEMENTED** | Lines carry only an integer tax_rate; no category, exemption reason or treatment narration exists, and the printed VAT label is fixed 'ضريبة القيمة المضافة (15%)' / 'VAT (15%)' whatever the line rates (web/src/messages/ar.json:2184; presentation/visual-v2.ts:131; used at doc-totals.tsx:127, thermal-receipt.tsx:112).<br>*Evidence:* VAT-03 (grep VATEX\|tax_category\|exempt in app/: none; probe S9 TaxCategory ABSENT); PH2-04 (no TaxExemptionReason in XML); code read as cited. | Zero-rated, exempt or out-of-scope supplies carry no tax-treatment statement and are labelled 15%.<br>*Risk:* Invoices with non-standard treatment misstate the rate and lack the IR 53(5)(k) narration.<br>*Note:* New gap: the hard-coded '(15%)' VAT label on A4 and thermal prints (an affirmative misstatement on 0%/mixed invoices) is not covered by any finding. | VAT-03, PH2-04, CON-06<br>**P0** | REM-12, REM-16, REM-34 |
| **R1-099** · Issue time<br>Invoice issue time HH:mm:ss (AST, or hh:mm:ssZ UTC) is mandatory from Phase 1 (not required on the printout).<br>*[RES-EN — Implementation Resolution (EN), 19 May 2023]; [XML — XML Implementation Standard v1.2, 19 May 2023] · [RES — Implementation Resolution, 19 May 2023] Annex 2 3.2 p.19; [XML] BR-KSA-70 p.58* | **VERIFIED DEFECT** | XML IssueTime is the draft created_at formatted H:i:s in the app time zone with no 'Z' (ZatcaService.php:253,271); the Phase-1 QR tag 3 uses created_at->toIso8601String() (:163); no issue timestamp is stored at posting.<br>*Evidence:* PH1-01 (C Probe 1: draft 09:00 UTC posted days later, QR/XML show 2026-10-01T09:00); PH2-08 (D P2 manifest issue_in_xml == created_at; SDK timeStamp WARN); PH1-14 (offset follows server zone). | Issue time is not the issuance moment and, under a UTC app zone, is neither AST nor marked 'Z' as BR-KSA-70 requires.<br>*Risk:* Every e-invoice carries a wrong issue time, hashed into the PIH chain and uncorrectable afterwards.<br>*Note:* Issue time is not required on the printout (Annex 2 3.2), so missing time on POS receipts (PH1-11) is not a breach of this row. Production APP_TIMEZONE NOT VERIFIED. | PH1-01, PH2-08, PH1-14<br>**P1** | REM-13 |
| **R1-100** · Dates format<br>Issue date YYYY-MM-DD; supply date (YYYY-MM-DD) required when different from issue date; human-readable dates may be shown as day/month/year.<br>*[RES-EN — Implementation Resolution (EN), 19 May 2023]; [TIG-EN — Tax Invoicing & Records Guideline v3, May 2026] · [RES — Implementation Resolution, 19 May 2023] Annex 2 3.1, 3.3 p.19; [TIG — Tax Invoicing & Records Guideline v3, May 2026] 4.2.1 p.17, 4.2.3 p.18* | **PARTIALLY IMPLEMENTED** | XML IssueDate uses YYYY-MM-DD (ZatcaService.php:270) but from created_at, not invoice_date; the printed date is invoice_date (from-invoice.ts:167). Invoices have no supply-date field (Invoice.php:25-37) and the XML has no ActualDeliveryDate.<br>*Evidence:* PH1-01/PH2-08 (D P2 backdated case: invoice_date 2026-01-15 vs XML IssueDate 2026-10-10); PH2-04 (BR-KSA-15 WARN on standard); PH1-17 ('ActualDeliveryDate=false'); grep ActualDeliveryDate in app/: none. | The XML/QR issue date differs from the printed and ledger date; a supply date different from the issue date cannot be recorded or shown.<br>*Risk:* Dates on the e-invoice contradict the human-readable invoice and the tax point.<br>*Note:* Human-readable date format is permissive (day/month/year allowed). | PH1-01, PH2-08, PH2-04, PH1-17<br>**P0** | REM-13, REM-16 |
| **R1-101** · Document title<br>Title must state the type: "Tax Invoice" / "Simplified Tax Invoice" / "Credit Note" / "Debit Note" (enumerated value; visible).<br>*[RES-EN — Implementation Resolution (EN), 19 May 2023]; [TIG-EN — Tax Invoicing & Records Guideline v3, May 2026] · [RES — Implementation Resolution, 19 May 2023] Annex 2 1.1 pp.17, 25, 32, 34; [TIG — Tax Invoicing & Records Guideline v3, May 2026] 4.2.3 p.18* | **PARTIALLY IMPLEMENTED** | A4/PDF titles follow the stored type: 'فاتورة ضريبية' or 'فاتورة ضريبية مبسطة' (PrintTemplateContract.php:158-161); notes print 'إشعار دائن'/'إشعار مدين' (from-credit-note.ts:45) and returns 'إشعار دائن (مرتجع مبيعات)'. POS thermal receipts default to 'فاتورة ضريبية' for simplified sales (from-invoice.ts:142).<br>*Evidence:* C PC-5 (PrintTemplateTest::invoice_document_type_maps_zatca_simplified_independently passed); PH1-11 (pos/page.tsx:1464-1470 passes no type); PH1-10 (drafts print titled 'فاتورة ضريبية'). | Every POS simplified receipt is titled as a standard tax invoice; drafts are printable under the tax-invoice title.<br>*Risk:* Consumers receive documents bearing the wrong legal title, a visible invoicing violation.<br>*Note:* AWJ's 'إشعار مدين' is a purchase-side buyer note (credit_notes.type=purchase), not a supplier debit note. | PH1-11, PH1-10<br>**P2** | REM-34 |
| **R1-102** · Single issuance<br>"A tax invoice shall be issued only once, and the issuance date may not be amended"; the invoice must also be delivered to the customer within the same period.<br>*[TIG-EN — Tax Invoicing & Records Guideline v3, May 2026] · 4.5 p.25* | **PARTIALLY IMPLEMENTED** | Posted invoices cannot be edited, deleted or re-posted via service/API (InvoiceService.php:163-165,462-464,889-892), and reprints reuse the stored number and invoice_date. The model still allows direct updates/deletes, and the XML/QR issue date differs from the printed date.<br>*Evidence:* C PC-4 (InvoiceTest posted cannot be edited/deleted; Probe 3 PUT/DELETE/post-again 422); PH1-09/ACC-05 (B-imm.log/C Probe 1: Eloquent update of total/number/qr succeeded; delete removed a posted invoice); SEC-05; PH1-10; PH1-01; PH1-02. | Single issuance relies on service convention only; issue date is inconsistent between XML/QR and print; numbered drafts can circulate; reprinted party data can change.<br>*Risk:* Issued invoices can be altered or duplicated outside the API, undermining the issued-once, date-unchangeable rule.<br>*Note:* Delivery to the customer within the same period is a taxpayer obligation. | PH1-09, ACC-05, SEC-05, PH1-10, PH1-01, PH1-02<br>**P1** | REM-07, REM-34, REM-13, REM-14 |
| **R1-103** · Sequential number<br>The invoice must carry a sequential number that uniquely identifies it; numerals or numerals+letters allowed; different sequences per branch are acceptable "provided that the Tax Invoice is clearly and distinctly defined and follows a sequence".<br>*[IR — VAT Implementing Regulations, consolidated Apr 2025]; [TIG-EN — Tax Invoicing & Records Guideline v3, May 2026]; [DG-EN — Detailed E-Invoicing Guidelines v2, May 2023] · [IR] 53(5)(b); [TIG — Tax Invoicing & Records Guideline v3, May 2026] 4.2.1 p.17, 4.2.3 p.18; [DG — Detailed E-Invoicing …* | **PARTIALLY IMPLEMENTED** | Invoice numbers are DB-unique per tenant/branch with serialized max()+1 allocation and alphanumeric prefix/suffix (migration 000053; app/Support/GeneratesDocumentNumbers.php:95-107,153-166); per-branch series exist. Numbers are allocated at draft and can be reused or gapped by draft deletion; services accept caller-supplied numbers.<br>*Evidence:* C PC-6 (DocumentNumberingTest 11 passed on PG incl. concurrency); PH1-12 (C-probe2-pg.log: gap 00001,00003; last draft number reused; MANUAL-7 accepted at service level); PH1-10 (numbered drafts printable). | A printed draft and a later issued invoice can share a number; non-HTTP callers can inject numbers.<br>*Risk:* Two documents bearing one number weaken unique identification at audit.<br>*Note:* Per DG 2.14, per-branch sequences are acceptable and no gap-free rule applies to the IRN (it applies to the ICV, which is tenant-wide and gap-free, C PC-7); PH1-12 is partly overstated (see GM2_conflicts.md). | PH1-12, PH1-10<br>**P2** | REM-35, REM-34 |
| **R1-104** · Certificate display<br>A resident taxable person must display its VAT registration certificate at its main place of business, all branches and e-commerce stores.<br>*[TIG-EN — Tax Invoicing & Records Guideline v3, May 2026] · 10.3 p.49* | **PARTIALLY IMPLEMENTED** | The storefront footer shows the merchant's legal name, CR and VAT number when provided (storefront/src/components/layout/Footer.tsx:168-170,205,320-324); there is no field or block for displaying the VAT registration certificate itself.<br>*Evidence:* Code read as cited; grep -rniE 'certificate' storefront/src: only the e-commerce authentication stamp text (storefront/src/components/customizer/messages.ts:47); TIG 10.3 text (R1-work vat_tax_invoicing_records_guideline_EN.txt:1817-1820) requires the certificate to be visible. | AWJ-hosted e-commerce stores cannot display the VAT registration certificate.<br>*Risk:* Tenants selling online through AWJ cannot meet the certificate-display obligation and face the prescribed penalty.<br>*Note:* New gap (storefront). Display at physical premises and branches is a taxpayer obligation. Whether a custom Store Customizer section could host a certificate image is NOT VERIFIED. | **new gap**<br>**—** | — |
| **R1-105** · VAT shown to consumers<br>The invoice must include the VAT value even if the customer is not taxable (simplified: VAT amount or inclusive statement).<br>*[TIG-EN — Tax Invoicing & Records Guideline v3, May 2026] · FAQ 7 p.55; 4.3.2 p.24* | **VERIFIED IMPLEMENTED** | Every invoice print path shows the VAT amount and a tax-inclusive total regardless of buyer status: A4 VAT and 'الإجمالي شامل الضريبة' rows (doc-totals.tsx:125-134), thermal VAT row (thermal-receipt.tsx:112-115), PDF = raster of the on-screen document (`documentExporter`, web/src/lib/pdf.ts), with the same VAT row as the page.<br>*Evidence:* Code read as cited; A §2 (header tax_amount = QR tag 5 = ledger 2120, probes S4/S14/S15); rendering verified by source reading and by jsdom render tests of the A4 compositions (Vitest), not in a real browser (C §4 item 4). | none<br>*Risk:* none identified<br>*Note:* The printed VAT label states 15% regardless of the actual rate; tracked under R1-083/R1-098. | —<br>**—** | — |
| **R1-106** · VAT after discount<br>VAT is calculated on the actual value after discounts/reductions; the invoice shows value after discount, discount value, tax amount and total incl. tax; tax per item when items carry different rates (may be on the total when all are 15%).<br>*[TIG-EN — Tax Invoicing & Records Guideline v3, May 2026] · FAQ 8-9 p.55* | **PARTIALLY IMPLEMENTED** | Line discounts reduce the taxable base before VAT (InvoiceService.php:564-586) and invoice discounts are allocated pro-rata across mixed-rate lines (:1230-1260); A4 prints the discount, VAT and grand total, and per-line VAT.<br>*Evidence:* A §2 probes S4/S5 (A-invoice-engine.log); VAT-07 (S15/S16: header-discount VAT truncated; line_tax stays pre-discount); VAT-06/PH2-04 (XML drops allowances, BR-CO-15); doc-items-table.tsx:211-244 (no discount column); thermal-receipt.tsx:110-116 (no discount row). | Header-discount VAT is truncated (1 halala low); printed line VAT stays pre-discount so line VAT does not sum to the VAT total; line discounts and value after discount per rate are not printed; thermal omits the discount; XML drops discounts.<br>*Risk:* Discounted invoices show inconsistent VAT and understate VAT by up to one halala each.<br>*Note:* New gap (print): line-discount values are never printed (no column in PrintTemplateContract.php:72-82) and thermal receipts omit the discount row. | VAT-07, VAT-06, PH2-04, CON-06<br>**P0** | REM-10, REM-16, REM-34 |
| **R1-107** · Profit-margin invoices<br>Under the margin scheme the tax must not be shown separately; the invoice states that VAT was charged on the profit margin (purchase from non-taxable person requires a purchase document that is not a tax invoice).<br>*[IR — VAT Implementing Regulations, consolidated Apr 2025]; [TIG-EN — Tax Invoicing & Records Guideline v3, May 2026] · [IR] 53(5)(l); [TIG — Tax Invoicing & Records Guideline v3, May 2026] 5.4 pp.33-34* | **NOT IMPLEMENTED** | No margin-scheme capability: VAT is always computed and shown separately; Product.profit_margin is a pricing markup, not the VAT margin scheme (app/Models/Product.php:38,47).<br>*Evidence:* grep -rniE 'margin_scheme\|profit_margin\|هامش الربح' app/ database/migrations/: only pricing/cost-sensitivity uses (Product.php, SensitiveCostPolicy.php). | Margin-scheme invoices (no separate VAT, margin statement) cannot be issued.<br>*Risk:* Approved margin-scheme dealers using AWJ would show VAT separately, contrary to IR 53(5)(l).<br>*Note:* New gap / scope declaration: the margin scheme (IR Art. 48, ZATCA approval) is unsupported; VAT-03 (no treatment model) is adjacent. Relevant to used-goods dealers only. | CON-08<br>**P2** | REM-32 |
| **R1-108** · Retail price display<br>In retail, displayed prices must include VAT; VAT = tax-inclusive price × 15/115; where a price was agreed exclusive of VAT, 15% is added.<br>*[RSG-EN — Retail Sector Guideline issue 3, May 2026] Issue 3 May 2026 · 4.1 p.15* | **PARTIALLY IMPLEMENTED** | Inclusive extraction uses the exact 15/115 fraction and exclusive prices get the rate added (InvoiceService.php:1179-1196). Whether catalog/POS prices are inclusive is a client-supplied per-request flag derived from unvalidated sales-config JSON; storefront orders carry no VAT.<br>*Evidence:* A §2 probe S3 (100.00 -> 13.04/86.96; 999.99 -> 130.43); VAT-09 (A-pos.log S12b: same price, VAT 0.43 vs 0.50 by flag); VAT-15; VAT-16 (CommerceCheckoutService.php:822; storefront markets.ts:43 tax_inclusive true). | No server-side rule that displayed retail prices are VAT-inclusive; storefront prices have no VAT basis.<br>*Risk:* Retail prices may be treated as exclusive, collecting VAT inconsistently across terminals.<br>*Note:* Stream A cites RSG §4.3 for this rule; it is RSG §4.1 (see GM2_conflicts.md). | VAT-09, VAT-15, VAT-16<br>**P2** | REM-10, REM-12, REM-30 |
| **R1-109** · Mixed-language fields<br>Proper names may be in Latin script; currency symbols/codes allowed; invoice numbers may mix numerals and letters.<br>*[TIG-EN — Tax Invoicing & Records Guideline v3, May 2026] · 4.2.1 p.17* | **VERIFIED IMPLEMENTED** | Invoice numbers combine letters and digits via configurable prefix/suffix (e.g. INV-2026-00001, FAT-2026-00001-JED); partner names accept Latin script (name, name_en); currency codes are rendered via getCurrency (from-invoice.ts:133).<br>*Evidence:* C-probe2-pg.log [C-NUM] lines; NumberingSettingsTest 17 passed on PG; Partner.php:31 (name, name_en). | none<br>*Risk:* none identified<br>*Note:* Permissive rule; Arabic labels remain required (R1-082/PH1-15). | —<br>**—** | — |

### R1 2.E Credit and debit notes (IR Art. 54 and Art. 40)

| Official requirement (source · section · version) | Status | AWJ implementation · evidence | Gap · risk | Findings · severity | Tasks |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| **R1-110** · Credit note trigger<br>If an Art. 40(1) event occurs after a tax invoice was issued and the VAT shown exceeds the VAT due, the taxable person must provide the customer with a credit note.<br>*[IR — VAT Implementing Regulations, consolidated Apr 2025] · Art. 54(1), p.52* | **PARTIALLY IMPLEMENTED** | Credit notes (CreditNoteService) and sales returns (ReturnService/PosReturnService) reduce output VAT via LedgerService and print as 'إشعار دائن'. Non-POS returns and credit notes recompute VAT from entered prices (15% exclusive default) with no cap, no mandatory invoice link and no e-invoice.<br>*Evidence:* B PC-5 (2120 net = invoice VAT minus CN and return VAT); VAT-01 (A-returns.log R2/R3); VAT-02 (R4: 264.50 credited vs 115.00 sold); VAT-13 (R1: VAT 0.14 vs 0.11); ACC-06 (B-imm.log: CN 10x invoice, against draft); ACC-02; PH1-06; PH2-05; SUB-04. | VAT reduced by notes is neither derived from nor bounded by the VAT shown on the invoice; notes lack reference, QR and XML.<br>*Risk:* Output VAT can be over-reversed, and the notes are invisible to ZATCA.<br>*Note:* Positive: POS returns allocate exactly from the source line (A probes R5/R6). | VAT-01, VAT-02, VAT-13, ACC-06, ACC-02, PH1-06, PH2-05, SUB-04<br>**P0** | REM-01, REM-02, REM-06, REM-18, REM-24 |
| **R1-111** · Debit note trigger<br>If the VAT shown is less than the VAT due after an Art. 40(1) event, a debit note must be provided.<br>*[IR — VAT Implementing Regulations, consolidated Apr 2025] · Art. 54(2), p.52* | **NOT IMPLEMENTED** | No supplier-side (sales) debit note exists: credit_notes.type is 'sales' (credit to customer) or 'purchase' (buyer note to a supplier) (migration 000039:28; from-credit-note.ts:45). Upward adjustments can only be made with a new, unlinked invoice.<br>*Evidence:* PH2-05 ('No DebitNote model exists for sales'); code read database/migrations/2025_01_01_000039_add_type_to_credit_notes.php:13-28. | AWJ cannot issue an IR 54(2) debit note referencing the original invoice when the VAT shown is too low.<br>*Risk:* Increases in consideration are invoiced without the required debit-note linkage or e-note (383). | PH2-05<br>**P0** | REM-18 |
| **R1-112** · Data-error correction<br>Outside Art. 40(1) events, errors in the supplier's or customer's data on an issued invoice are corrected by a credit or debit note; both parties keep invoice and note.<br>*[IR — VAT Implementing Regulations, consolidated Apr 2025]; [TIG-EN — Tax Invoicing & Records Guideline v3, May 2026] · [IR] Art. 54(3), p.52 (added 2021); [TIG — Tax Invoicing & Records Guideline v3, May 2026] 7.1 item 5 p.37* | **PARTIALLY IMPLEMENTED** | Posted invoices cannot be edited via service/API, so corrections need a credit note (full reversal; zero-value notes cannot post) and a new invoice. Printed seller/buyer data are read live, so editing master data changes reprints of issued invoices.<br>*Evidence:* C PC-4; PH1-02 (C Probe 1: stored QR unchanged, qrFor(live) differs, no snapshot columns); B PC-13 (no void/edit path); PH1-06/ACC-06 (note reference optional); PH2-05; PH1-09. | A de facto edit-and-reprint path exists through master-data edits; correcting notes lack a mandatory reference and an e-note.<br>*Risk:* Issued invoices can change silently after issuance and corrections are not evidenced to ZATCA.<br>*Note:* LedgerService rejects zero-value entries (B PC-1), so data-only corrections require full credit plus re-issue. | PH1-02, PH1-06, PH2-05, ACC-06, PH1-09<br>**P0** | REM-14, REM-18, REM-02, REM-07 |
| **R1-113** · Note content<br>Notes must contain all Art. 53 details according to the nature of the related invoice and must clearly reference the previously issued invoice(s) "as determined by the Authority".<br>*[IR — VAT Implementing Regulations, consolidated Apr 2025]; [RES-EN — Implementation Resolution (EN), 19 May 2023]; [XML — XML Implementation Standard v1.2, 19 May 2023] · [IR] Art. 54(4)-(5), pp.52-53; [RES — Implementation Resolution, 19 May 2023] Annex 2 13.1 p.33 (reference—conditional, …* | **PARTIALLY IMPLEMENTED** | The credit-note print shows title, number, date, seller name/VAT/CR (no address), buyer name/VAT/city, lines and totals, with the reason as a note (from-credit-note.ts:45-78). original_invoice_id is optional and never printed; return prints show no source invoice; no QR or XML.<br>*Evidence:* PH1-06 (C Probe 1: CN posted with original_invoice_id and reason null; from-credit-note.ts:62,76,78); ACC-06 (reference may be a draft); PH2-05/SUB-04 (no BillingReference); web/src/app/(app)/returns/[id]/page.tsx (no source reference); PH1-02. | Notes need not reference an issued invoice, never print it, omit the seller address and rates, and have no e-note.<br>*Risk:* Notes breach IR 54(4)-(5); ZATCA treats unreferenced notes as violating Art. 54.<br>*Note:* New gap: the credit-note builder drops the seller national address (from-credit-note.ts:48-56 vs from-invoice.ts:150) and no rate column exists. | PH1-06, ACC-06, PH2-05, SUB-04, PH1-02, CON-06<br>**P0** | REM-18, REM-02, REM-24, REM-14, REM-34 |
| **R1-114** · Note deadline<br>Credit/debit notes must be issued no later than the 15th day of the month following the date of the triggering event.<br>*[IR — VAT Implementing Regulations, consolidated Apr 2025]; [TIG-EN — Tax Invoicing & Records Guideline v3, May 2026] · [IR] Art. 54(6), p.53 (added by Res. 24-06-01, 19 Nov 2024); [TIG — Tax Invoicing & Records Guideline v3, May 2026] 7.2 p.39* | **NOT IMPLEMENTED** | note_date defaults to today and may be any date (CreditNoteService.php:57); no triggering-event date is stored (migration 000018) and no deadline check or report exists.<br>*Evidence:* CON-02; ACC-04 (B-lock.log: back-dated CN changed a 'filed' month); grep deadline in CreditNoteService.php/ReturnService.php: only the commercial return-window policy (ReturnService.php:275-303). | No control or alert that notes are issued by the 15th of the month after the triggering event.<br>*Risk:* Late notes go undetected, exposing the taxpayer to the IR 54(6) violation added in 2024.<br>*Note:* ReturnService's return window (days from the source document) is a customer-return policy, not the IR 54(6) deadline. | CON-02, ACC-04<br>**P1** | REM-31, REM-28 |
| **R1-115** · Note type follows invoice<br>The note's fields "should correspond exactly to the type of Electronic Invoice for which the Credit/Debit Note is issued"; a note to a simplified invoice is a simplified note (reported), to a tax invoice a standard note (cleared).<br>*[RES-EN — Implementation Resolution (EN), 19 May 2023]; [DG-EN — Detailed E-Invoicing Guidelines v2, May 2023] · [RES — Implementation Resolution, 19 May 2023] Annex 2 notes p.32; [DG — Detailed E-Invoicing Guidelines v2, May 2023] 2.5-2.6 p.9, 4.3 p.19* | **NOT IMPLEMENTED** | Credit notes and returns have no standard/simplified type or ZATCA fields (migrations 000018, 000039; C Probe 1 'credit_notes zatca/qr columns: []'); they are never cleared or reported.<br>*Evidence:* PH2-05 (D P2: posted CN carries no zatca attributes, ICV unchanged); PH1-06; SUB-04 (requestManual accepts Invoice only, ZatcaSubmissionService.php:20). | Notes cannot inherit the original invoice's type, profile or clearance/reporting route.<br>*Risk:* Notes to simplified invoices are never reported and notes to tax invoices are never cleared. | PH2-05, PH1-06, SUB-04<br>**P0** | REM-18, REM-24 |
| **R1-116** · Reason<br>Reason for issuing the note "as per the VAT Implementing Regulation" is mandatory (Phase 2, visible).<br>*[RES-EN — Implementation Resolution (EN), 19 May 2023] · Annex 2 13.2 pp.33, 35* | **PARTIALLY IMPLEMENTED** | credit_notes.reason is optional free text printed as the document note (migration 000018:29; CreditNoteService.php:67; from-credit-note.ts:78); return documents have no reason field, only notes (app/Models/ReturnDocument.php:25-32); no InstructionNote in any XML.<br>*Evidence:* PH1-06 (C Probe 1: CN posted with reason null); PH2-05 (KSA-10/BR-KSA-17 unsatisfiable); grep InstructionNote in app/: none. | The reason is optional, not tied to IR adjustment grounds, absent on returns and absent from the e-note.<br>*Risk:* Phase-2 notes would fail BR-KSA-17 and lack the visible reason.<br>*Note:* Annex 2 13.2 makes the reason mandatory from Phase 2. | PH1-06, PH2-05<br>**P0** | REM-18 |
| **R1-117** · Multi-invoice notes<br>One note may adjust several invoices of the same customer; it must reference each invoice (a range of IRNs is acceptable when many).<br>*[DG-EN — Detailed E-Invoicing Guidelines v2, May 2023]; [TIG-EN — Tax Invoicing & Records Guideline v3, May 2026] · [DG — Detailed E-Invoicing Guidelines v2, May 2023] 4.3 p.19, FAQ p.63; [TIG — Tax Invoicing & Records Guideline v3, May 2026] 7.2.1 p.39* | **NOT IMPLEMENTED** | A credit note can reference at most one invoice (single original_invoice_id, migration 000018:30) and a return one source (ReturnDocument original_type/original_id); there is no multi-invoice reference or IRN range.<br>*Evidence:* Schema read as cited; PH1-06 (reference optional, not printed); PH2-05 (no BillingReference). | A note adjusting several invoices of one customer cannot reference each invoice.<br>*Risk:* Volume rebates or multi-invoice corrections would be issued without the required references.<br>*Note:* New gap: making original_invoice_id mandatory (PH1-06/ACC-06 recommendations) would still not support multi-invoice notes; a note-to-invoice link table is needed. | PH1-06, PH2-05<br>**P0** | REM-18 |
| **R1-118** · No note without supply<br>A credit/debit note may not be issued for a payment or account adjustment unrelated to a prior supply (e.g. goodwill credits).<br>*[TIG-EN — Tax Invoicing & Records Guideline v3, May 2026] · 7.1 Example 19 p.38* | **VERIFIED DEFECT** | Credit notes can be created and posted with no original invoice and no reason (original_invoice_id nullable, CreditNoteService.php:68), so notes unrelated to a prior supply are accepted; credit notes have no 'require source' setting.<br>*Evidence:* PH1-06 (C Probe 1: CN-2026-00001 posted with original_invoice_id=null, reason=null); ACC-06 (B-imm.log: CN against a draft invoice and with another partner accepted). | No control prevents goodwill or account-adjustment credit notes unrelated to a supply.<br>*Risk:* Output VAT can be reduced by notes with no underlying supply.<br>*Note:* Returns have an opt-in source requirement (app/Support/Settings.php:76 sales.require_return_source=false). | PH1-06, ACC-06<br>**P1** | REM-18, REM-02 |
| **R1-119** · Adjustment events<br>Supply value is adjusted when: (a) the supply is cancelled/suspended after occurring; (b) a material change in nature changes the tax due; (c) the agreed value is changed for any reason incl. an additional discount after sale; (d) goods/services are returned and accepted.<br>*[IR — VAT Implementing Regulations, consolidated Apr 2025] · Art. 40(1), p.31* | **PARTIALLY IMPLEMENTED** | Cancellations and value changes are handled by credit notes and returns (no invoice void/edit path); returns support restock and POS returns allocate exactly from source lines. Non-POS returns and credit notes compute VAT from entered prices; there is no debit note for increases.<br>*Evidence:* B PC-13 (cancelled status never set; corrections via CN/returns); A §2 POS returns R5/R6 exact; VAT-01 (R2/R3); VAT-02 (R4); VAT-13 (R1); ACC-06; PH2-05 (no sales debit note). | Adjustments for returns or discounts on inclusive or discounted invoices reverse the wrong VAT; upward adjustments lack a debit note.<br>*Risk:* Adjusted taxable value and VAT diverge from the actual adjustment event. | VAT-01, VAT-02, VAT-13, ACC-06, PH2-05<br>**P0** | REM-01, REM-02, REM-18 |
| **R1-120** · Output-tax timing<br>Increases are adjusted in the return for the period in which the event occurred; decreases in the period of the event **or** the period in which the credit note was issued, whichever is later; the taxable customer corrects input tax in the period the note was issued.<br>*[IR — VAT Implementing Regulations, consolidated Apr 2025]; [TIG-EN — Tax Invoicing & Records Guideline v3, May 2026] · [IR] Art. 40(4)-(6), pp.31-32; [TIG — Tax Invoicing & Records Guideline v3, May 2026] 7.1.1 table p.38* | **PARTIALLY IMPLEMENTED** | Note and return journals are dated note_date/return_date (B PC-8), so decreases land in the note's period when it is dated on issue; the date is user-editable and there is no event date, VAT period or 'Adjustments' classification.<br>*Evidence:* B PC-8; ACC-04 (B-lock.log: CN dated into a filed month changed January output VAT 15,000 to 0); ACC-03 (no return boxes/adjustments field); CON-02. | No rule enforces the later of event period and note period; back-dating moves decreases into earlier or filed periods; increases have no debit note; no adjustments field.<br>*Risk:* Output-tax decreases may be declared in the wrong, earlier period.<br>*Note:* Register §5.2: RSG 'whichever earlier' conflicts with IR 40(5) 'whichever later'; the IR prevails. Customer-side input-tax corrections use purchase notes dated note_date. | CON-02, ACC-04, ACC-03<br>**P1** | REM-31, REM-28 |
| **R1-121** · Bad-debt relief<br>Output tax may be reduced for unpaid consideration only if: tax was declared and paid; supply to an unrelated person; ≥12 months since supply; CPA write-off certificate (or other evidence if no auditor required); for amounts > SAR 100,000, formal legal action; not available under cash accounting; if later paid, tax is re-accounted and a new tax invoice issued.<br>*[IR — VAT Implementing Regulations, consolidated Apr 2025] · Art. 40(7)-(9), pp.32-33* | **NOT IMPLEMENTED** | No bad-debt relief mechanism (statutory conditions, write-off evidence, output-tax reduction, re-accounting on recovery).<br>*Evidence:* CON-04; grep -rniE 'bad_debt\|badDebt\|write_?off' app/Services app/Models: none. | Bad-debt relief cannot be computed, evidenced or reversed under the IR 40(7)-(9) conditions.<br>*Risk:* Relief is either unclaimed or claimed manually without control of the statutory conditions.<br>*Note:* A manual journal can reduce 2120 without classification (ACC-11), i.e. an uncontrolled workaround. | CON-04<br>**P2** | REM-32 |
| **R1-122** · Input-tax reversal<br>A customer who deducted input tax but has not paid in full within 12 months after the month following the supply must reverse the deduction for the unpaid part (exception: financing contracts with periodic payments); re-deduct when paid.<br>*[IR — VAT Implementing Regulations, consolidated Apr 2025] · Art. 40(10)-(11), pp.32-33 (amended 24-06-01)* | **NOT IMPLEMENTED** | No 12-month unpaid-purchase input-VAT reversal or re-deduction; payables ageing exists only as a report (app/Services/Reporting/ReportService.php:740).<br>*Evidence:* CON-04; grep for 12-month/input-VAT reversal logic in app/Services/Accounting: none. | Input VAT on supplier invoices unpaid beyond the statutory window is never flagged or reversed.<br>*Risk:* Input tax stays over-deducted, exposing the taxpayer to assessments and penalties. | CON-04<br>**P2** | REM-32 |
| **R1-123** · Retrospective discounts<br>Discounts granted after invoicing require a credit note.<br>*[RSG-EN — Retail Sector Guideline issue 3, May 2026]; [TIG-EN — Tax Invoicing & Records Guideline v3, May 2026] · [RSG — Retail Sector Guideline issue 3, May 2026] 6.1 p.32; [TIG — Tax Invoicing & Records Guideline v3, May 2026] 7.1* | **PARTIALLY IMPLEMENTED** | A post-sale discount can only be granted through a credit note: posted invoices are immutable and payments have no discount or write-off option. The credit note computes VAT at a default 15% exclusive, needs no invoice reference and is not e-invoiced; manual journals could bypass it.<br>*Evidence:* C PC-4; grep -niE 'discount\|write_?off' app/Services/Accounting/PaymentService.php: none; VAT-02 (A-returns.log R4); PH1-06; PH2-05; ACC-06; ACC-11 (manual VAT journals unclassified). | The note for a retrospective discount does not inherit the invoice's rate/basis, need not reference it, and is invisible to ZATCA.<br>*Risk:* Retrospective discounts reduce VAT incorrectly or without a compliant note.<br>*Note:* RSG 6.1 'whichever earlier' timing conflicts with IR 40(5) (register §5.2); the IR prevails. | VAT-02, PH1-06, PH2-05, ACC-06, ACC-11<br>**P0** | REM-02, REM-18, REM-28 |

### R1 2.F Time of supply / tax point

| Official requirement (source · section · version) | Status | AWJ implementation · evidence | Gap · risk | Findings · severity | Tasks |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| **R1-125** · General tax point<br>Tax becomes due on the earliest of: date of supply; date of issue of the tax invoice; date of receipt of consideration (in whole or part, to the extent received).<br>*Unified VAT Agreement Art. 23(1) as quoted in [TIG-EN — Tax Invoicing & Records Guideline v3, May 2026] 4.5.1 p.26 and [RSG-EN — Retail Sector Guideline issue 3, May 2026] 5.1 p.24 · —* | **PARTIALLY IMPLEMENTED** | VAT is recognised only at the document date: journal entry_date = invoice_date (InvoiceService.php:1005), defaulting to today (:83).
