POS creates and posts the invoice at checkout, so sale, invoice and payment coincide (PosService.php:241-252). Customer receipts post Dr cash / Cr AR only (PaymentService.php:350-358). The earliest-event rule is documented in OD-DG-3 (architecture only, nothing implemented).<br>*Evidence:* B PC-8 (March-dated invoice posted in October lands in March); B-lock.log:13 'future-dated invoice (2027-06-01) post: ACCEPTED'; StoreInvoiceRequest.php:34 (invoice_date nullable\|date, no bound); PaymentService.php:350-358; ZatcaService.php:90,163,253 (e-invoice issue date = created_at); CommercePaymentIntentService.php:24 (markCollected, no accounting effect); docs/plans/pos/DELIVERY-DECISION-PASS-7-DG-3-TAX-POINT-REPORT.md:12-15,619-641; grep advance_payment\|prepayment\|386 in app/ -> none. | No tax point at receipt of consideration (advances, collected storefront orders); no supply date, so delivery before invoicing never advances the tax point (see R1-126); invoice_date is free (back/future-dated) and differs from the e-invoice IssueDate (created_at), so the 'date of issue' is not one reliable fact.<br>*Risk:* Output VAT is declared in a later period than due for advances, online collections and supplies invoiced after delivery, exposing tenants to late-payment and incorrect-return penalties.<br>*Note:* POS/cash sales are aligned by construction. The supply-date element is flagged as new_gap on R1-126. OD-DG-3 (accepted) records the correct rule but authorises no implementation. | CON-03, ACC-04, ACC-08, VAT-16, PH1-01, PH2-08, CON-07<br>**P1** | REM-29, REM-28, REM-30, REM-13 |
| **R1-126** · Date of supply (one-off)<br>Goods without transport: when placed at the customer's disposal; with transport: when transport commences; installed/assembled: on completion; services: on completion of performance.<br>*Agreement Art. 23(2) as quoted in [RSG-EN — Retail Sector Guideline issue 3, May 2026] 5.1 p.25 · —* | **NOT IMPLEMENTED** | Invoices carry only invoice_date and due_date (migration 2025_01_01_000004:22-23); no supply/delivery date. Delivery notes record delivery_date but have no ledger or VAT effect (DeliveryNoteService.php:19-24), and the invoice drafted from them defaults to today, not the delivery date (DeliveryNoteSalesInvoiceDraftBuilder.php:423-429). products.type good\|service (migration 000003:69) is unused by tax logic.<br>*Evidence:* grep supply_date\|tax_point\|ActualDeliveryDate in app/ and database/migrations -> none (delivery_date only on delivery notes/commerce schedules); D Appendix A: KSA-5 not emitted, BR-KSA-15 WARN on standard shape (SDK S3); R2-020; DeliveryNoteService.php:19-24; DeliveryNoteSalesInvoiceDraftBuilder.php:423-429; B ACC-03 matrix ('tax-point basis -> document date, not configurable'). | Date of supply (goods placed at disposal, transport start, installation completion, service completion) is neither captured nor used; VAT follows invoice_date even when a delivery note shows earlier delivery; XML lacks KSA-5.<br>*Risk:* Supplies delivered in one period and invoiced in the next are declared late, and standard invoices omit the supply date expected by BR-KSA-15.<br>*Note:* NEW GAP: no finding covers the tax-point consequence - no supply-date capture and no earliest-of(supply, invoice, payment) derivation; built delivery notes precede invoices with no VAT recognition. PH2-04 covers only the missing XML element; ACC-03 only the report basis. OD-DG-3 lists it as a future gate. | PH2-04, ACC-03, CON-07<br>**P0** | REM-16, REM-28, REM-29 |
| **R1-127** · Continuous supplies by instalment<br>Services supplied successively with payments due on set dates: supply occurs on the earliest of instalment due date, actual payment, or invoice issue — and at least once every 12 consecutive months.<br>*[IR — VAT Implementing Regulations, consolidated Apr 2025] · Art. 20(1), pp.15-16* | **NOT IMPLEMENTED** | No instalment/continuous-supply model: sales.installments, operations.rentals and operations.leases are coming_soon (ApplicationCatalog.php:74-79,215-226). Recurring templates only create a draft invoice dated today when a user calls generate() (RecurringInvoiceService.php:106-145, :129; RecurringInvoiceController.php:49); no scheduler. Payments create no VAT (PaymentService.php:350-358).<br>*Evidence:* ApplicationCatalog.php:74-79,215-226; RecurringInvoiceService.php:129 (invoice_date = now()); grep 'recurring' routes/console.php -> none; grep installment in app/ -> catalog key only; D Appendix A / PH2-13: @name only 0100000/0200000 (no continuous-supply flag C, R2-003). | No tax point at instalment due date or payment, no 12-consecutive-month backstop, no continuous-supply transaction flag; VAT arises only when a user issues and posts an invoice.<br>*Risk:* Rental, subscription and instalment VAT is declared late or missed when invoices lag due dates or payments.<br>*Note:* NEW GAP: due-date trigger and 12-month deemed-supply backstop are not covered by any finding (CON-03 covers only the payment trigger; PH2-13 only the XML flag). Relevance grows when rentals/leases/installments ship. | CON-03, PH2-13, CON-07<br>**P2** | REM-29, REM-16 |
| **R1-128** · Other continuous supplies<br>Each invoice or payment is a separate supply taxed at the earlier of invoice or payment, to the extent of the amount; if neither occurs, the supply is deemed to occur 12 months after the later of start of supply / previous supply event.<br>*[IR — VAT Implementing Regulations, consolidated Apr 2025] · Art. 20(2)-(3), p.16* | **PARTIALLY IMPLEMENTED** | Each recurring or ordinary invoice is taxed at its invoice_date (InvoiceService.php:1005); a payment received before an invoice creates no VAT (PaymentService.php:350-358); nothing deems a supply 12 months after the last event.<br>*Evidence:* RecurringInvoiceService.php:106-145; PaymentService.php:350-358; grep advance_payment\|prepayment in app/ -> none; ZatcaService.php:254 (no continuous-supply flag, PH2-13). | Earlier-of(invoice, payment) not implemented (payment leg missing); no 12-month deemed-supply rule.<br>*Risk:* Payments received ahead of invoicing on continuous contracts escape VAT in the period of receipt.<br>*Note:* The 12-month backstop gap is flagged as new_gap on R1-127 (same root). | CON-03, PH2-13, CON-07<br>**P2** | REM-29, REM-16 |
| **R1-129** · Utilities<br>Oil/gas/water/electricity via a distribution network (non-continuous basis): earlier of supplier's invoice or receipt of payment.<br>*[IR — VAT Implementing Regulations, consolidated Apr 2025] · Art. 20(4), p.16* | **PARTIALLY IMPLEMENTED** | The invoice-date tax point exists (InvoiceService.php:1005); the payment leg does not (receipts Dr cash / Cr AR only, PaymentService.php:350-358). No AWJ module targets network utilities (fuel_stations sells at the pump).<br>*Evidence:* PaymentService.php:350-358; InvoiceService.php:1005; ApplicationCatalog.php (no utilities capability); grep advance_payment\|prepayment in app/ -> none. | Receipt of payment before the supplier's invoice does not trigger VAT.<br>*Risk:* For a tenant distributing utilities through a network, prepaid consumption would be declared late.<br>*Note:* Applicability limited to tenants supplying oil/gas/water/electricity via a distribution network. | CON-03<br>**P2** | REM-29 |
| **R1-130** · Government contracts<br>Supplies to government entities under the Government Tenders & Procurement Law: date of the payment order issued to the supplier or receipt of (part of) the consideration, whichever earlier, to the amount received.<br>*[IR — VAT Implementing Regulations, consolidated Apr 2025] · Art. 20(5), p.16* | **NOT IMPLEMENTED** | No government-customer type or payment-order date exists; VAT is always recognised at invoice_date (InvoiceService.php:1005). The XML emits only @name 0100000/0200000, so the B2G flag and contract ID are absent.<br>*Evidence:* grep government\|payment_order in app/ and database/migrations -> no tax-relevant hit; InvoiceService.php:1005; ZatcaService.php:254; D PH2-13; R2-005 (B2G flag, BR-KSA-92 contract ID); B ACC-03 matrix (no box 1.2 or classification). | Tax point for government-procurement supplies (earlier of payment order or receipt) cannot be applied; no box 1.2 split; no B2G flag/contract reference.<br>*Risk:* Government contractors declare VAT in the wrong (earlier) period and cannot fill return box 1.2, misstating returns and burdening cash flow.<br>*Note:* NEW GAP: the payment-order-date tax point and a government-entity customer classification are not covered by any finding (CON-03 covers receipt of consideration only). | CON-03, ACC-03, PH2-13, CON-07<br>**P1** | REM-29, REM-28, REM-16 |
| **R1-131** · Deemed supply on cessation<br>Deemed supply from cessation/deregistration occurs at the earlier of cessation or deregistration date.<br>*[IR — VAT Implementing Regulations, consolidated Apr 2025] · Art. 20(6), p.16 (added 24-06-01)* | **NOT APPLICABLE** | AWJ has no VAT registration, deregistration or cessation event (grep deregist\|registration_date in app/ and migrations -> none). A deemed-supply output VAT could only be booked through a manual journal to 2120, which carries no tax classification (ACC-11).<br>*Evidence:* none (see reason): one-off taxpayer lifecycle event; grep scope app/, database/migrations. | none (taxpayer obligation; supporting capability limited to unclassified manual journals)<br>*Risk:* none identified<br>*Note:* Taxpayer determines cessation/deregistration date. Supporting capability: ManualJournalService (unclassified, ACC-11); avg_cost/asset values available for valuation (R1-141). A future deregistration workflow should compute deemed-supply VAT on retained goods/assets. | —<br>**—** | — |
| **R1-132** · Invoice/advance before supply<br>If the tax invoice is issued before supply, tax is declared in the period of issuance; an advance received creates a supply to the extent paid and a tax invoice must be issued within 15 days after the end of the month of receipt.<br>*[TIG-EN — Tax Invoicing & Records Guideline v3, May 2026] · 4.5.1 p.26* | **PARTIALLY IMPLEMENTED** | An invoice posted before delivery puts its VAT in the invoice_date period (InvoiceService.php:1005), as required. Advances are not modelled: receipts post Dr cash / Cr AR with no VAT (PaymentService.php:350-358); there is no advance (386) invoice, no prepaid-amount deduction and no 15-day issuance control.<br>*Evidence:* B PC-8; PaymentService.php:350-358; grep advance_payment\|prepayment\|prepaid\|386 in app/ -> none; B section 4 note on advance receipts; DELIVERY-DECISION-PASS-7-DG-3-TAX-POINT-REPORT.md:184-204,627-631 (386 lifecycle documented as future gate); StoreInvoiceRequest.php:34; R2-031 (prepayment adjustment). | No VAT at receipt of an advance, no advance tax invoice within 15 days after month-end, no later deduction of the advance; invoice_date not bound to the actual issue moment (back/future dating accepted; e-invoice IssueDate = created_at).<br>*Risk:* Output VAT on deposits is declared late, or charged twice when a manual deposit invoice is followed by a full final invoice.<br>*Note:* Workaround (ordinary invoice for the deposit) double-charges VAT at the final invoice unless a credit note is issued. | CON-03, ACC-04, PH1-01, PH2-08, CON-07<br>**P1** | REM-29, REM-28, REM-13 |
| **R1-133** · Applicable rate<br>The rate due is the rate applicable at the date of supply or date of import.<br>*[VATL — VAT Law (Royal Decree M/113, as amended)] · Art. 18, p.6* | **PARTIALLY IMPLEMENTED** | Rate is a per-line integer snapshot defaulting to sales/purchases default_tax_rate = 15 (Settings.php:54,163; InvoiceService.php:504) with no effective-dated rate table; issued lines keep their rate after a default change. Credit notes, returns, expenses, assets and recurring templates fall back to a literal 15 (CreditNoteService.php:77; ReturnService.php:154).<br>*Evidence:* A probe S18 (A-invoice-engine.log:52 'old invoice line rate=15, new invoice line rate=5'); Settings.php:54,163; CreditNoteService.php:77,84-85; ReturnService.php:154; A-returns.log:12 (R4 CN rate defaulted to 15); VAT-11 code refs. | Rate is not resolved from the date of supply/import; documents dated before 1 Jul 2020 or after a future change get the current default; credit notes do not inherit the original invoice's rate.<br>*Risk:* At any rate change, back-dated invoices and credit notes against old invoices would charge or reverse VAT at the wrong rate.<br>*Note:* Low exposure today (15% stable since 1 Jul 2020). Statutory basis for VAT-11 is VATL Art. 18 (see GM3_conflicts.md). | VAT-11, VAT-02, VAT-10<br>**P1** | REM-12, REM-02, REM-10 |
| **R1-134** · Cash accounting<br>Taxable persons with taxable supplies ≤ SAR 5,000,000 (prior and current year) may, with ZATCA approval, account for output and input tax only to the extent paid; invoices are still issued per the general rules; ZATCA notifies approval and effective date.<br>*[IR — VAT Implementing Regulations, consolidated Apr 2025]; [RSG-EN — Retail Sector Guideline issue 3, May 2026] · [IR] Art. 46, pp.36-38; [RSG — Retail Sector Guideline issue 3, May 2026] 5.3 pp.27-31* | **NOT IMPLEMENTED** | No VAT accounting-basis setting: output and input VAT are always recognised on the document date (B PC-8). OD-DG-3 lists an effective-dated accrual/cash_accounting basis only as an unresolved future design gate.<br>*Evidence:* grep cash_basis\|cash_accounting\|accounting_basis\|vat_basis in app/ and database/migrations -> none; ReportService.php:475-487 (GL-only report); B ACC-03 matrix ('no payment-basis'); DELIVERY-DECISION-PASS-7-DG-3-TAX-POINT-REPORT.md:161-182,624-626. | An approved small taxpayer cannot report output/input VAT to the extent paid; no effective-date election and no payment-allocated VAT projection.<br>*Risk:* Tenants approved for cash accounting must prepare returns outside AWJ or over-declare VAT on unpaid invoices.<br>*Note:* Optional scheme (ZATCA approval, supplies <= SAR 5M); per CLAUDE.md rule 6 it should be a configurable policy defaulting to accrual. | ACC-03, CON-03<br>**P1** | REM-28, REM-29 |

### R1 2.G Taxable amount, discounts, rounding, currency

| Official requirement (source · section · version) | Status | AWJ implementation · evidence | Gap · risk | Findings · severity | Tasks |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| **R1-135** · Net tax<br>Net tax for a period = total output tax on all taxable supplies − total deductible input tax (incl. import VAT) — "invoice basis".<br>*[IR — VAT Implementing Regulations, consolidated Apr 2025] · Art. 45(1), p.36* | **PARTIALLY IMPLEMENTED** | ReportService::taxReport returns output (2120) minus input (1150) net movements for a free date range (ReportService.php:475-487); balances reconcile to document VAT on SQLite and PostgreSQL. All purchase/expense/asset VAT is treated as deductible; import VAT does not exist.<br>*Evidence:* B PC-5 (B-mix.log:18-20 'Σ docs output 33800 / input 11250; taxReport March net 22550'); ReportService.php:475-487; B-route.log:3-12 (tax_output->4110 accepted, report output 0; tax_input->1110 accepted); grep deductib\|import_vat in app/ -> none. | Net tax is not limited to deductible input tax, import VAT is absent, VAT can be routed out of 2120/1150 or moved by unclassified manual journals, and no period/return structure exists.<br>*Risk:* Net VAT payable is understated (over-claimed input) or silently misstated after an account remap.<br>*Note:* The three figures are arithmetically correct for posted documents (PC-5); defects are in scope and classification. | ACC-03, ACC-01, ACC-02, CON-04, ACC-11<br>**P1** | REM-28, REM-05, REM-06, REM-32 |
| **R1-136** · Inclusive-price formula<br>Tax on a VAT-inclusive consideration = consideration × rate ÷ (100% + rate).<br>*[IR — VAT Implementing Regulations, consolidated Apr 2025] · Art. 45(2), p.36* | **PARTIALLY IMPLEMENTED** | Sales invoices, POS, quotes and purchases extract VAT as round(incl x rate / (100+rate)) in integer halalas (InvoiceService.php:1188-1196; ComputesLineTax.php:26-34). Generic returns and credit notes ignore inclusive mode and add VAT on top; expenses/assets are exclusive-only; shipping is taxed as exclusive inside inclusive invoices.<br>*Evidence:* A PC + probe S3 (100.00 -> 13.04/86.96; 999.99 -> 130.43; A-invoice-engine.log:6-13); A-purchase.log:1 (P1 869/130/999); failures: A-returns.log:3-8 (R2 115.00 inclusive returned as 115.00 + 17.25), :12 (R4); A-purchase.log:13-15 (E1); A-pos.log:4-5 (S12b client flag decides); A-invoice-engine.log:48-50 (S16). | Formula not applied on returns (ReturnService.php:133,179), credit notes (CreditNoteService.php:77-85), expenses/assets, or inclusive shipping; POS inclusive mode is client-controlled.<br>*Risk:* Output VAT is over-reversed on returns/credit notes and input VAT over-claimed on receipts entered gross.<br>*Note:* Fuel sales resolve inclusive mode server-side and fail closed (FuelStationSettingsService.php:80-88) - reusable pattern. | VAT-01, VAT-02, VAT-14, VAT-08, VAT-09, VAT-07<br>**P0** | REM-01, REM-02, REM-10 |
| **R1-137** · Currency<br>Invoice values may be in any currency, but the VAT amount must be stated in SAR; non-SAR amounts are converted at the SAMA (Saudi Central Bank) daily rate on the date the tax becomes due.<br>*[IR — VAT Implementing Regulations, consolidated Apr 2025]; [TIG-EN — Tax Invoicing & Records Guideline v3, May 2026] · [IR] Art. 53(5)(j), Art. 61 p.56; [TIG — Tax Invoicing & Records Guideline v3, May 2026] 4.2.2 p.17 + annex example (USD invoice with "TOTAL VAT – SAR")* | **VERIFIED DEFECT** | Tenant currency is a free 3-letter field (Tenant.php:20; UpdateCompanyRequest.php:20) shown on prints, but the e-invoice hard-codes SAR in DocumentCurrencyCode and every currencyID (ZatcaService.php:245,247,273,306-311), with no TaxCurrencyCode and no exchange-rate data anywhere.<br>*Evidence:* grep exchange_rate\|fx_rate\|SAMA\|TaxCurrencyCode in app/ -> none; ZatcaService.php:273; D Appendix A: BR-KSA-68 ERROR on 18/18 AWJ outputs (SDK S3; lead run-lead logs); C PH1-18 (print shows tenant currency while QR/XML label the same numbers SAR); R2-009. | Foreign-currency invoices cannot state VAT in SAR at the SAMA rate on the tax-due date; non-SAR tenants get foreign amounts labelled SAR; TaxCurrencyCode (BT-6) is missing from every XML.<br>*Risk:* Non-SAR invoices misstate VAT in SAR, and every e-invoice fails BR-KSA-68 so none can be cleared or reported.<br>*Note:* SAR-only tenants state VAT in SAR correctly in ledger/QR; for them the defect is limited to the missing TaxCurrencyCode. | PH1-18, PH2-13, PH2-04, PH1-17<br>**P0** | REM-14, REM-16 |
| **R1-138** · Rounding (VAT)<br>VAT is shown in riyals and halalas; the unit price may be stated without rounding, but if the VAT amount or total taxable amount contains fractions of a halala the VAT amount is rounded to the nearest halala; the rounded figures are the ones used in the VAT return.<br>*[TIG-EN — Tax Invoicing & Records Guideline v3, May 2026]; [RSG-EN — Retail Sector Guideline issue 3, May 2026] · [TIG — Tax Invoicing & Records Guideline v3, May 2026] 4.2.4 p.21; [RSG — Retail Sector Guideline issue 3, May 2026] 4.1 p.15 (Example 2: 172.173 → 172.17)* | **PARTIALLY IMPLEMENTED** | Money is integer halalas end-to-end; line VAT is half-up intdiv(base x rate + 50, 100) (InvoiceService.php:1179-1182), and the stored rounded tax_amount is what posts to 2120/1150 and feeds the report (B PC-5). Header-discount VAT is truncated (InvoiceService.php:1213; PurchaseService.php:245,449) and document VAT is the sum of rounded line VAT.<br>*Evidence:* A PC + probe S13 (A-invoice-engine.log:33-35); S15 exact 1293.75 -> stored 1293 (:47); S16 1327.5 -> 1327 (:50); A-purchase.log:9 (P3 134 vs 135); S2 0.20 vs document-level 0.15 (:2-4); A-returns.log:1-2 (R1 Σ 0.14 vs 0.11); B PC-5. | Truncation instead of nearest halala on discounted documents; invoice VAT depends on how lines are split (Σ rounded lines ≠ rounded total); partial returns accumulate rounding beyond the original VAT.<br>*Risk:* Systematic 1-halala under-statement on discounted documents and halala-level over-reversal on partial returns.<br>*Note:* VAT-04 is validator-tolerant (lead re-rated P3); RSG 4.1 text speaks of rounding the invoice's total tax amount to the nearest halala. | VAT-07, VAT-04, VAT-13<br>**P2** | REM-10, REM-16, REM-01 |
| **R1-139** · Rounding (XML)<br>Amounts carry up to 2 decimals; rounding is "half-up" on the third decimal; unit price, quantity and percentage have no decimal restriction; allowance/charge amounts rounded separately.<br>*[XML — XML Implementation Standard v1.2, 19 May 2023] · ch.7 semantic data types p.12; ch.10 Rounding p.29; 13.2.8 (BR-DEC) p.45, 13.3.2 (BR-KSA-DEC) p.62* | **PARTIALLY IMPLEMENTED** | XML/QR amounts are formatted from integer halalas to 2 decimals without floats (ZatcaService.php:190-195); line VAT is half-up. Category VAT is not rounded at document level, header-discount VAT is truncated, and allowances, charges and line RoundingAmount are not emitted, so their separate rounding does not exist.<br>*Evidence:* C PC-2 (ZatcaTest amount formatting); A probe S13; D Appendix A (no AllowanceCharge, no line TaxTotal/RoundingAmount; BR-KSA-52/53 ERROR on standard shape); lead rounding experiment (R2-029: SDK tolerance 0.01 x lines); A-invoice-engine.log:42-50 (S15/S16). | Document-level rounding of BT-110/BT-117 (XML std ch.10) not applied; truncation on discounts; allowance/charge and KSA-12 rounding absent from XML.<br>*Risk:* XML arithmetic warnings/errors once TaxSubtotal and allowances are emitted; 1-halala deviations on discounted documents.<br>*Note:* Two-decimal unit prices and integer tax rates are a permissible AWJ policy (the standard allows, not requires, more decimals). Detailed BR-DEC checks are R2 scope. | VAT-04, VAT-07, VAT-06, PH2-04<br>**P0** | REM-16, REM-10 |
| **R1-140** · Discounts<br>Discounts and price reductions granted to the customer reduce the value of the supply; VAT is due on the discounted consideration; post-sale discounts require a credit note.<br>*[RSG-EN — Retail Sector Guideline issue 3, May 2026]; [TIG-EN — Tax Invoicing & Records Guideline v3, May 2026] · [RSG — Retail Sector Guideline issue 3, May 2026] 4.2.2 pp.17-18 (quoting Agreement), 6.1 p.32; [TIG — Tax Invoicing & Records Guideline v3, May 2026] FAQ 8* | **PARTIALLY IMPLEMENTED** | Line discounts reduce the taxable base before VAT and header discounts are allocated pro-rata across mixed-rate lines with VAT following (InvoiceService.php:1205-1260); ledger and QR carry the discounted VAT. Post-sale reductions use credit notes, which are exclusive-only, default to 15%, are uncapped against the invoice and are not e-invoiced.<br>*Evidence:* A PC + probes S5 (999-70 -> VAT 139) and S4 (10% off mixed -> VAT 13.50; ledger 2120 1350; A-invoice-engine.log:17-21); A-returns.log:9-12 (R3 return ignores header discount; R4); B-imm probe (CN 10x invoice posted); D PH2-05 (no 381 XML). | Header-discount VAT truncated; XML omits allowances so the discounted VAT is misrepresented (BR-CO-15 ERROR); returns reverse undiscounted VAT; credit notes unbounded, not inheriting the invoice basis, without QR/XML/reference/reason.<br>*Risk:* Over-reversal of output VAT through returns/credit notes and rejection of discounted invoices at clearance/reporting.<br>*Note:* The core rule (VAT on the discounted consideration at invoicing) is met in ledger and QR. | VAT-07, VAT-06, VAT-01, VAT-02, ACC-06, PH2-05, PH1-06<br>**P0** | REM-10, REM-16, REM-01, REM-02, REM-18 |
| **R1-141** · Deemed-supply value<br>Value of a deemed supply = purchase price or cost; if not determinable, fair market value at the date of the deemed supply.<br>*[IR — VAT Implementing Regulations, consolidated Apr 2025] · Art. 39(1), pp.30-31 (amended 24-06-01)* | **NOT IMPLEMENTED** | No deemed-supply concept. Stock-issue permits for damage, internal consumption and samples post Dr inventory_manual_adjustment (5180) / Cr 1140 at average cost, with a free-text reason that is explicitly not an accounting classification, and never compute output VAT (StockPermitService.php:19-31,295-304).<br>*Evidence:* StockPermitService.php:19-31,295-304; StoreStockPermitRequest.php:21 (reason nullable, max:255); grep deemed\|nominal supply\|own_use\|personal_use\|internal_consumption in app/ -> none; RSG Issue 3 Example 18 (vat_retail_guideline_EN.txt:1361-1366: gift -> output VAT on purchase price). | Goods given away or used outside the business after input-VAT deduction are neither valued (cost/FMV) nor charged output VAT; no deemed-supply document or return mapping.<br>*Risk:* Output VAT on nominal supplies (gifts, samples above limits, own use) is omitted, under-declaring VAT.<br>*Note:* NEW GAP (no finding covers deemed/nominal supplies). Cost basis (avg_cost) exists, so valuation could be derived. Same root as R1-142. | CON-08<br>**P2** | REM-32 |
| **R1-142** · Gifts & samples<br>Free gifts/samples (and goods to employees) are not deemed supplies up to SAR 200 (excl. VAT) per recipient per calendar year, with an annual cap of SAR 50,000 at FMV; same for free services.<br>*[IR — VAT Implementing Regulations, consolidated Apr 2025] · Art. 15(2)-(6), pp.11-12 (amended 24-06-01)* | **NOT IMPLEMENTED** | Samples and gifts leave stock via issue permits with an optional free-text reason (StoreStockPermitRequest.php:21); no recipient, per-recipient SAR 200 annual value or SAR 50,000 annual cap is tracked, and no output VAT is computed on the excess (StockPermitService.php:19-31).<br>*Evidence:* StockPermitService.php:19-31; StoreStockPermitRequest.php:21; migration 2025_01_01_000042_create_stock_permits.php:45; grep gift\|sample in tax paths -> none (CommerceOrderGift is gift-wrapping metadata); RSG Issue 3 text (vat_retail_guideline_EN.txt:1370-1384). | Threshold tests (<= SAR 200 per recipient per calendar year; <= SAR 50,000 per year at FMV) cannot be applied; excess gifts/samples are not taxed.<br>*Risk:* Retail and distribution tenants under-declare nominal-supply VAT on promotional giveaways.<br>*Note:* NEW GAP, same root as R1-141 (deemed supplies not modelled). | CON-08<br>**P2** | REM-32 |
| **R1-143** · Government payments<br>Amounts paid by a government entity to a supplier are not a subsidy where they are consideration for a supply to that entity.<br>*[IR — VAT Implementing Regulations, consolidated Apr 2025] · Art. 39(2), p.31* | **NOT APPLICABLE** | AWJ treats every invoiced amount as consideration for a taxable supply regardless of customer type; there is no subsidy/grant classification that could exclude government payments from VAT.<br>*Evidence:* none (see reason): legal characterisation rule; grep government\|subsid in app/ -> no tax-relevant hit. | none (legal characterisation applied by the taxpayer)<br>*Risk:* none identified<br>*Note:* If a tenant records government consideration as a bare receipt (PaymentService.php:350-358), no VAT arises - a usage error, not a system rule. | —<br>**—** | — |

### R1 2.H Tax categories and the legal grounds behind VATEX codes

| Official requirement (source · section · version) | Status | AWJ implementation · evidence | Gap · risk | Findings · severity | Tasks |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| **R1-144** · Standard rate<br>Tax is imposed at the basic rate of 15% of the value of the supply or import unless the Law/IR provide an exemption or zero rate for that supply.<br>*[VATL — VAT Law (Royal Decree M/113, as amended)] · Art. 2(2), p.3 (as amended by Royal Order A/638)* | **PARTIALLY IMPLEMENTED** | 15% is the default for sales and purchases (Settings.php:54,163; product/line columns default(15)) and is computed correctly in integer halalas on invoices, POS (server-authoritative product rate), quotes and purchases. Shipping is always taxed at a hard-coded 15% (InvoiceService.php:48,691,908), even on zero-rated-only invoices; import VAT is not modelled.<br>*Evidence:* A PC (probes S1/S3/S13/S17 match Fraction expectations; identical on PG, A-invoice-engine-pgsql.log); A-pos.log:3 (client 0% ignored -> 15%); A-invoice-engine.log:53-54 (S18 shipping VAT 1.50 on 0%-only invoice); grep import_vat\|customs in app/ -> none. | No S category code anywhere (XML BR-CO-04); exceptions rely on a bare 0% rate; shipping ignores zero-rating/exemption; imports outside the engine.<br>*Risk:* 15% is charged on delivery of zero-rated or exempt supplies, and import VAT is not captured by the system.<br>*Note:* Basic-rate arithmetic itself is verified; gaps are at the edges (shipping, imports, category coding). | VAT-08, CON-04, VAT-03<br>**P1** | REM-10, REM-32, REM-12 |
| **R1-145** · Exempt — financial services<br>Exempt: financial services listed (money/securities dealings, credit, account operation, derivatives, Islamic equivalents, implicit-margin interest/charges, brokerage with implicit spread, issue/transfer of debt or equity securities), **except** where the consideration is an explicit fee, commission or commercial discount; life insurance/reinsurance exempt even if fee-based.<br>*[IR — VAT Implementing Regulations, consolidated Apr 2025] · Art. 29(1)-(8), pp.21-22* | **NOT IMPLEMENTED** | No exempt category exists. A user can type tax_rate 0 on a line, stored identically to zero-rated or out-of-scope lines (probe S9); the 'Tax Free' preset is a UI-only label with rate 0 (SalesConfigController.php:35-39). No VATEX-SA-29/29-7 and no exempt box.<br>*Evidence:* grep VATEX\|tax_category\|exemption_reason\|zero_rated\|out_of_scope in app/ and database/migrations -> only free-text fuel_products.tax_category (FuelStationMasterDataRequest.php:70, unused by the engine); A-invoice-engine.log:30-32 (S9 'TaxCategory=ABSENT'); D Appendix A (no TaxSubtotal/TaxCategory). | Exempt supplies cannot be recorded as exempt (category E + VATEX-SA-29/29-7), shown on the invoice, reported in box 5, or separated from explicit-fee services.<br>*Risk:* Exempt financial-service income is indistinguishable from zero-rated sales, misreporting returns and e-invoices.<br>*Note:* A 0% rate is not an exemption (exempt supplies also restrict input-tax deduction, R1-166). Exempt vs explicit-fee classification is a taxpayer decision. | VAT-03, ACC-03, PH2-04<br>**P0** | REM-12, REM-28, REM-16 |
| **R1-146** · Exempt — real estate<br>Exempt: transfer of ownership/disposal of real estate (residential, commercial, agricultural, bare land) and lease/licence of residential real estate; hotels, guest houses, serviced accommodation for visitors are not residential.<br>*[IR — VAT Implementing Regulations, consolidated Apr 2025] · Art. 30, pp.22-23* | **NOT IMPLEMENTED** | Same as R1-145: no exempt category or VATEX-SA-30; residential-lease or real-estate-disposal lines can only be entered at 0%, indistinguishable from zero-rated or out-of-scope lines. Rental/lease modules are coming_soon (ApplicationCatalog.php:215-226).<br>*Evidence:* Same grep as R1-145 (none); A probe S9; SalesConfigController.php:35-39; ApplicationCatalog.php:215-226; B ACC-03 matrix ('exempt -> missing'). | No E category/VATEX-SA-30 on lines, invoice or XML; no exempt-sales box; no distinction between residential (exempt) and hotel/serviced (taxable) accommodation.<br>*Risk:* Exempt real-estate income is misreported in returns and e-invoices while related input VAT is still claimed in full.<br>*Note:* RETT (5%) on disposals is outside VAT and outside this audit. | VAT-03, ACC-03, PH2-04<br>**P0** | REM-12, REM-28, REM-16 |
| **R1-147** · Zero-rate priority<br>A supply that is both exempt (ch.5) and zero-rated (ch.6) is treated as zero-rated.<br>*[IR — VAT Implementing Regulations, consolidated Apr 2025] · Art. 31(2), p.23* | **NOT IMPLEMENTED** | AWJ has no Z/E categories, so the precedence of zero-rating over exemption cannot be expressed; any such supply is just a 0% line.<br>*Evidence:* Same category grep as R1-145 (none); A probe S9; InvoiceLine.php:26 / migration 000004:55 (tax_rate is the only tax attribute). | The classification outcome (Z with reason, return box 3/4 rather than E/box 5) cannot be recorded.<br>*Risk:* Supplies that should be zero-rated may be reported as exempt, wrongly restricting input-VAT recovery.<br>*Note:* Precedence is a taxpayer classification rule; the system prerequisite is a category model (VAT-03 remediation). | VAT-03, ACC-03<br>**P1** | REM-12, REM-28 |
| **R1-148** · Export of goods<br>Zero-rated if the supplier holds, within 90 days of supply, evidence that the goods left GCC territory (customs export documents + commercial documents identifying customer and delivery place + transport documents); otherwise not treated as export until evidence is obtained.<br>*[IR — VAT Implementing Regulations, consolidated Apr 2025] · Art. 32(1)-(6), pp.23-24* | **NOT IMPLEMENTED** | Exports can only be entered as 0% lines: no zero-rated/export category, no VATEX-SA-32, no export transaction flag (@name fixed 0100000/0200000, ZatcaService.php:254), no export box and no export-evidence tracking. partners.country exists (migration 000003:33) but no tax logic reads it.<br>*Evidence:* Same category grep as R1-145 (none); D PH2-13 (no export flag) and Appendix A; A probe S9; grep country in app/Services/Accounting and app/Services/Reporting -> none; B ACC-03 matrix ('exports -> missing'). | No Z/VATEX-SA-32 and export flag; no capture of customs/commercial/transport evidence or of the 90-day deadline; no reversion to 15% when evidence is missing.<br>*Risk:* Exports are misreported in returns/e-invoices and unsupported zero-rating after 90 days goes undetected.<br>*Note:* NEW GAP (low): no 90-day export-evidence control or automatic re-rating is covered by any finding (VAT-03 covers only the category). Holding the evidence is the taxpayer's obligation. | VAT-03, ACC-03, PH2-13, PH2-04, CON-08<br>**P0** | REM-12, REM-28, REM-16, REM-32 |
| **R1-149** · Customs suspension / special zones<br>Zero rate for supplies into/within customs duty-suspension arrangements and qualifying special zones; re-export of temporarily imported goods after repair/processing.<br>*[IR — VAT Implementing Regulations, consolidated Apr 2025] · Art. 32 bis, pp.24-25 (added 24-06-01)* | **NOT IMPLEMENTED** | No category model; supplies into customs-suspension arrangements or special zones can only be typed as 0% lines without a reason code.<br>*Evidence:* Same category grep as R1-145 (none); R2-026 and R2 section 4 table: SDK accepts VATEX-SA-32(bis) 'Supply under Customs Suspension Arrangement' (not in XML std v1.2). | No Z category with VATEX-SA-32(bis); no zero-rated box mapping.<br>*Risk:* Zero-rated special-zone supplies are misreported in returns and e-invoices.<br>*Note:* R1 flagged the missing VATEX code; R2 shows the SDK list includes VATEX-SA-32(bis), resolving the encoding question for implementation. | VAT-03, ACC-03, PH2-04<br>**P0** | REM-12, REM-28, REM-16 |
| **R1-150** · Services to non-GCC residents<br>Zero-rated services to a customer not resident in any GCC state, except: place-of-supply special cases (Agreement Arts 17-21); customer resident in a GCC state; customer/related person benefits directly while in a GCC state and cannot fully recover input tax; services physically performed on tangible goods located in a GCC state; tourist-refund provider services zero-rated.<br>*[IR — VAT Implementing Regulations, consolidated Apr 2025] · Art. 33, pp.25-26 (amended 2019, 2022, 2024)* | **NOT IMPLEMENTED** | No zero-rated category or VATEX-SA-33; customer residence (partners.country) and product type (good\|service) are stored but unused by tax logic; services to non-residents can only be entered as 0% lines.<br>*Evidence:* migration 2025_01_01_000003_create_partners_and_products.php:33 (country), :69 (type); grep country\|'service' in app/Services/Accounting and Reporting -> none; same category grep as R1-145; B ACC-03 matrix. | Cannot record VATEX-SA-33 zero-rating, apply the residency/benefit exceptions, or report in the exports box.<br>*Risk:* Export-of-services sales are misreported and the GCC-benefit exceptions are uncontrolled.<br>*Note:* Residency and benefit tests are taxpayer judgements; the system needs a category plus customer residence. | VAT-03, ACC-03, PH2-04<br>**P0** | REM-12, REM-28, REM-16 |
| **R1-151** · International transport<br>Zero-rated: international transport of goods and of passengers; services directly connected/incidental to passenger transport; supply of qualifying means of transport (customer certificate retained by supplier); maintenance/repair/modification of qualifying means of transport (incl. parts) with customer certificate; related services (Art. 25 definition) when ancillary. Qualifying = main purpose international transport, ≥10 persons or commercial …<br>*[IR — VAT Implementing Regulations, consolidated Apr 2025] · Art. 34(1)-(10), pp.26-28* | **NOT IMPLEMENTED** | No zero-rated category or VATEX-SA-34-x. Shipping charged on invoices is always taxed at a hard-coded 15% (InvoiceService.php:48,691,908), even when the goods lines are zero-rated. logistics.fleet/shipping are coming_soon (ApplicationCatalog.php:253-262).<br>*Evidence:* Same category grep as R1-145 (none); A-invoice-engine.log:53-54 (S18 shipping VAT on 0%-only invoice); VAT-08 refs; ApplicationCatalog.php:253-262. | International transport and qualifying means-of-transport supplies cannot be zero-rated with VATEX-SA-34-1..5; delivery charges on exported goods are taxed at 15%; no customer-certificate tracking.<br>*Risk:* 15% VAT is charged on zero-rated transport/delivery elements and returns are misreported.<br>*Note:* Customer certificates for qualifying transport are taxpayer-held evidence. | VAT-03, ACC-03, VAT-08, PH2-04<br>**P0** | REM-12, REM-28, REM-10, REM-16 |
| **R1-152** · Medicines & medical equipment<br>Zero-rated supplies of qualifying medicines and medical goods per classifications issued by the Ministry of Health or competent authority (and GCC health ministers' controls).<br>*[IR — VAT Implementing Regulations, consolidated Apr 2025] · Art. 35, p.28* | **NOT IMPLEMENTED** | Products carry only an integer tax_rate (migration 2025_01_01_000003:73); qualifying medicines can be set to 0% but without a zero-rated category, VATEX-SA-35 or box 3 mapping.<br>*Evidence:* migration 2025_01_01_000003_create_partners_and_products.php:73; same category grep as R1-145 (none); A probe S9; B ACC-03 matrix ('zero-rated domestic -> missing'). | No Z category/VATEX-SA-35 on lines or XML; zero-rated domestic sales cannot be reported in box 3.<br>*Risk:* Pharmacy/medical tenants' zero-rated sales are indistinguishable from exempt ones, misreporting returns and e-invoices.<br>*Note:* Which goods qualify (MoH classification) is a taxpayer master-data decision. | VAT-03, ACC-03, PH2-04<br>**P0** | REM-12, REM-28, REM-16 |
| **R1-153** · Investment metals<br>Zero-rated: first supply of qualifying metals (gold, silver, platinum ≥99% purity, tradable on global bullion markets) by the producer or refiner, and grants/assignments of rights thereto.<br>*[IR — VAT Implementing Regulations, consolidated Apr 2025] · Art. 36, p.28* | **NOT IMPLEMENTED** | As R1-152: 0% possible per line, but no Z category/VATEX-SA-36 and no purity or first-supply attributes.<br>*Evidence:* Same category grep as R1-145 (none); VAT-03 code refs; B ACC-03 matrix. | No VATEX-SA-36 zero-rating and no box 3 reporting.<br>*Risk:* Investment-metal supplies are misreported.<br>*Note:* Narrow applicability (producers/refiners). | VAT-03, ACC-03, PH2-04<br>**P0** | REM-12, REM-28, REM-16 |
| **R1-154** · Military goods<br>Zero-rated supply of qualifying locally-manufactured military goods to the armed forces/internal security by a registered supplier licensed by GAMI holding a GAMI certificate.<br>*[IR — VAT Implementing Regulations, consolidated Apr 2025] · Art. 36 bis, pp.28-29 (added 29 Nov 2022)* | **NOT IMPLEMENTED** | No Z category/VATEX-SA-MLTRY and no GAMI licence/certificate data; such supplies can only be 0% lines.<br>*Evidence:* Same category grep as R1-145 (none); VAT-03 code refs. | Cannot zero-rate with VATEX-SA-MLTRY or retain the GAMI certificate reference.<br>*Risk:* Qualifying military supplies are misreported.<br>*Note:* Narrow applicability. | VAT-03, ACC-03, PH2-04<br>**P0** | REM-12, REM-28, REM-16 |
| **R1-155** · Diplomatic missions<br>Zero-rated supplies to diplomatic missions by qualified suppliers per Governor decision.<br>*[IR — VAT Implementing Regulations, consolidated Apr 2025] · Art. 36 bis (2), p.29* | **NOT IMPLEMENTED** | No category model; supplies to diplomatic missions can only be 0% lines without a reason code.<br>*Evidence:* Same category grep as R1-145 (none); R2-026 / R2 section 4: SDK list includes VATEX-SA-DIPLOMAT (no official text in XML std v1.2). | No Z category with VATEX-SA-DIPLOMAT; no qualified-supplier record.<br>*Risk:* Zero-rated diplomatic supplies are misreported.<br>*Note:* Encoding exists only in the SDK list; official wording NOT VERIFIED (R1 section 5 item 10). | VAT-03, ACC-03, PH2-04<br>**P0** | REM-12, REM-28, REM-16 |
| **R1-156** · Citizens' education/health<br>Private education and private healthcare supplied to Saudi citizens: "the Saudi government will cover the VAT on behalf of the citizen"; invoice treated as zero-rated with VATEX-SA-EDU / VATEX-SA-HEA; return box 2 "Sales where the State bears the VAT".<br>*[DG-EN — Detailed E-Invoicing Guidelines v2, May 2023]; [XML — XML Implementation Standard v1.2, 19 May 2023]; [RSG-EN — Retail Sector Guideline issue 3, May 2026]; [SFG — Simplified VAT filing guide] · [DG — Detailed E-Invoicing Guidelines v2, May 2023] 2.3 fn.1 p.8, 4.2.2 example p.19; [XML] …* | **NOT IMPLEMENTED** | No VATEX-SA-EDU/HEA, no state-borne VAT box, and partners have no national-ID field (migration 000003:17-34 holds vat_number, cr_number and address fields only); such sales can only be entered at 0% or 15%.<br>*Evidence:* Same category grep as R1-145 (none); migration 2025_01_01_000003_create_partners_and_products.php:17-34; R2-018 (BR-KSA-25/49: buyer name + NAT id required); B ACC-03 matrix. | Cannot issue VATEX-SA-EDU/HEA zero-rated invoices with buyer name and national ID, nor report box 2 'sales where the State bears the VAT'.<br>*Risk:* Private schools and clinics serving citizens cannot produce compliant invoices or returns; VAT may be wrongly charged to citizens.<br>*Note:* Royal Order basis is RECALLED-UNVERIFIED in R1; the ZATCA treatment is verified. | VAT-03, ACC-03, PH1-05, PH2-04<br>**P0** | REM-12, REM-28, REM-15, REM-16 |
| **R1-157** · Out of scope<br>Not supplies / outside scope: transfer of an economic activity as a going concern meeting Art. 17 conditions (incl. written agreement and notification to ZATCA by end of the following month); supplies by a legal person to itself; intra-tax-group supplies; issue/supply of vouchers (except premium over face value); compensation unrelated to a supply; supplies by a branch outside GCC.<br>*[IR — VAT Implementing Regulations, consolidated Apr 2025]; [TIG-EN — Tax Invoicing & Records Guideline v3, May 2026] · [IR] Art. 17 pp.13-14, Art. 18 p.15, Art. 19 p.15; [TIG — Tax Invoicing & Records Guideline v3, May 2026] Note 6 p.15* | **NOT IMPLEMENTED** | No out-of-scope (O) category or VATEX-SA-OOS reason; non-supplies can only be recorded as 0% invoice lines or plain journals. The invoice 'adjustment' is a non-taxable rounding amount posted to 5170 (InvoiceService.php:694-696), not an O-category line.<br>*Evidence:* Same category grep as R1-145 (none); InvoiceService.php:694-696; migration 2025_01_01_000004:30; A probe S9; D Appendix A. | Cannot record category O with a free-text reason on invoices/XML, nor exclude such amounts from taxable totals in a return.<br>*Risk:* Out-of-scope receipts are either taxed or reported as zero-rated/exempt, misstating returns.<br>*Note:* Going-concern transfers also require notification to ZATCA (taxpayer obligation). | VAT-03, ACC-03, PH2-04<br>**P0** | REM-12, REM-28, REM-16 |
| **R1-158** · Category codes<br>VAT category codes are a subset of UNCL5305: E (exempt), S (standard), Z (zero-rated), O (not subject to VAT); each E/Z/O breakdown carries a Saudi exemption reason code (VATEX-SA-29, 29-7, 30, 32, 33, 34-1..5, 35, 36, EDU, HEA, MLTRY, OOS) with Arabic/English reason text; zero-rated breakdowns must carry a reason code.<br>*[XML — XML Implementation Standard v1.2, 19 May 2023] · 11.2.4 pp.32-33; BR-KSA-69 p.58* | **NOT IMPLEMENTED** | No VAT category (S/Z/E/O) or exemption-reason code on any line, product, partner or setting; buildXml emits no TaxSubtotal, TaxCategory, ClassifiedTaxCategory or TaxExemptionReasonCode (ZatcaService.php:232-315). The 'Zero Rated' and 'Tax Free' presets are both rate 0 and UI-only (SalesConfigController.php:35-39).<br>*Evidence:* grep VATEX\|tax_category\|exemption_reason in app/ and database/migrations -> only free-text fuel_products.tax_category; A-invoice-engine.log:32 (S9 TaxCategory=ABSENT); D Appendix A + SDK S3: BR-CO-04 and BR-CO-18 ERROR on 18/18; lead run-lead validate logs. | Category codes, VATEX reason codes/texts and per-category breakdowns are entirely absent; zero-rated breakdowns cannot carry reasons (BR-KSA-69/83).<br>*Risk:* No e-invoice can pass validation, and returns cannot separate zero-rated, exempt and out-of-scope supplies.<br>*Note:* SDK adds codes absent from XML std v1.2 (VATEX-SA-32(bis), DIPLOMAT, DUTYFREE, ROYALDECREE; R2-026) - the remediation code list should follow the SDK. | VAT-03, PH2-04, PH1-17, VAT-15, ACC-03<br>**P0** | REM-12, REM-16, REM-28 |
| **R1-159** · Out-of-scope lines<br>Lines/allowances/charges with category O must have a VAT rate of 0 if a rate is present.<br>*[XML — XML Implementation Standard v1.2, 19 May 2023] · BR-KSA-11/12/13 p.50* | **NOT IMPLEMENTED** | No O category exists. Lines typed at 0% satisfy 'rate 0' only incidentally, while the shipping charge is always taxed at 15% (InvoiceService.php:48,691,908), so an out-of-scope charge could never carry 0%. No AllowanceCharge/TaxCategory is emitted.<br>*Evidence:* Same category grep as R1-145 (none); A-invoice-engine.log:53-54 (S18); D Appendix A (no document AllowanceCharge with TaxCategory); R2 BR-KSA-11 error rule. | BR-KSA-11/12/13 cannot be honoured: no O category on lines, allowances or charges; charges forced to 15%.<br>*Risk:* Out-of-scope charges are taxed at 15% and XML category rules are unenforceable. | VAT-03, VAT-08, PH2-04<br>**P0** | REM-12, REM-10, REM-16 |
| **R1-160** · Taxable supplies defined<br>Tax is imposed on all taxable supplies of goods/services by a taxable person in KSA in the course of economic activity, on supplies received under the reverse charge, and on imports.<br>*[IR — VAT Implementing Regulations, consolidated Apr 2025] · Art. 14(1), p.10* | **PARTIALLY IMPLEMENTED** | Domestic supplies invoiced through InvoiceService/POS are taxed (A PC). Supplies received under reverse charge and imports are not modelled, and storefront/commerce orders carry no VAT and never reach an invoice (CommerceCheckoutService.php:813-827; no order-to-invoice path), even after payment is marked collected.<br>*Evidence:* A PC (S1/S3/S12); grep reverse_charge\|import_vat\|customs in app/ -> none; A VAT-16 (CommerceCheckoutService.php:822 line_total = price x qty); B ACC-08 and PC-14 (no CommerceOrder -> InvoiceService call); CommercePaymentIntentService.php:24 (markCollected has no accounting effect). | Reverse-charge and import taxation are absent; online sales, even when collected, stay untaxed until someone invoices manually.<br>*Risk:* Output VAT on imported services/goods and on storefront sales is under-declared.<br>*Note:* The Commerce contract (CLAUDE.md) separates orders from invoices by design; the VAT consequence still needs the ADR required by CLAUDE.md Commerce item 9. | CON-04, ACC-08, VAT-16<br>**P2** | REM-32, REM-30 |
| **R1-161** · Mixed invoices<br>One invoice may combine taxable and exempt items (e.g. bank commission + exempt profit element) provided it meets tax-invoice requirements.<br>*[DG-EN — Detailed E-Invoicing Guidelines v2, May 2023] · 4.1.2 example p.16* | **PARTIALLY IMPLEMENTED** | One invoice may mix 15% and 0% lines; arithmetic and header-discount allocation across rates are correct (probes S4/S9; InvoiceService.php:1230-1260). An exempt line cannot be marked exempt; the print shows one VAT total and per-line VAT amounts without rate or category; the XML has no per-category TaxSubtotal.<br>*Evidence:* A-invoice-engine.log:17-21 (S4), :30-32 (S9 30000/1500/31500, TaxCategory=ABSENT); web/src/modules/documents/components/sections/doc-totals.tsx:127-128 (single VAT row); doc-items-table.tsx:233-245 (tax amount column, no rate); D Appendix A (BR-CO-18 ERROR). | Exempt vs taxable items are not identified on the invoice or XML; no per-rate/category breakdown; shipping on mixed invoices forced to 15%.<br>*Risk:* Mixed invoices do not meet the tax-invoice breakdown requirements and fail XML validation.<br>*Note:* Per-rate taxable amount on the human-readable invoice is assessed under R1-083 (other slice). | VAT-03, PH2-04, VAT-08, CON-06<br>**P0** | REM-12, REM-16, REM-10, REM-34 |

### R1 2.I Input tax, reverse charge, imports

| Official requirement (source · section · version) | Status | AWJ implementation · evidence | Gap · risk | Findings · severity | Tasks |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| **R1-162** · Deduction scope<br>Input tax on goods/services received in the course of economic activity is deductible to the extent used for taxable supplies (incl. zero-rated), intra-GCC supplies, and supplies that would be taxable if made in KSA.<br>*[IR — VAT Implementing Regulations, consolidated Apr 2025] · Art. 49(1), p.42* | **PARTIALLY IMPLEMENTED** | Input VAT on purchases, expenses and assets is posted to 1150 (tax_input role or hard-coded 1150) and netted in the report (B PC-5; ExpenseService.php:32; AssetService.php:34). Every input VAT amount is treated as fully deductible; there is no use/attribution attribute.<br>*Evidence:* B PC-5 (B-mix.log:19 Σ docs input 11250 = report); ExpenseService.php:32; AssetService.php:34; grep deductib\|non_deduct\|apportion in app/ -> only AccountingRoles.php:112 description; B-route.log:12 (tax_input->1110 cash accepted); A-purchase.log:13-15 (E1 gross receipt taxed on top). | No limitation of deduction to taxable use; input VAT can be routed to non-asset accounts; receipts entered gross over-claim VAT.<br>*Risk:* Input VAT is over-claimed (non-deductible or exempt-use), understating net VAT payable.<br>*Note:* Fully taxable tenants are affected mainly by VAT-14 entry errors and routing misconfiguration. | CON-04, ACC-03, VAT-14, ACC-01, ACC-02<br>**P1** | REM-32, REM-28, REM-10, REM-05, REM-06 |
| **R1-163** · Evidence<br>Deduction requires evidence of input tax paid/payable per Agreement Art. 48; alternatives at ZATCA's discretion: a correctly issued simplified tax invoice; commercial document for intra-GCC transfers; other commercial documents proving the charge and payment.<br>*[IR — VAT Implementing Regulations, consolidated Apr 2025]; [TIG-EN — Tax Invoicing & Records Guideline v3, May 2026] · [IR] Art. 49(7), p.43; [TIG — Tax Invoicing & Records Guideline v3, May 2026] 9.5 pp.46-47 (incl. non-Arabic invoices, discretionary)* | **PARTIALLY IMPLEMENTED** | Purchases store an optional supplier_invoice_no (migration 000009:23; StorePurchaseRequest.php:26); expenses keep vendor_name and attachments (migration 000069:36-55); Document Center files link only to purchases/expenses (DocumentTransactionLink.php:89-90). Input VAT posts whether or not a supplier invoice number, supplier VAT number or document exists (PurchaseService.php:74-97,502).<br>*Evidence:* migrations 2025_01_01_000009_create_purchases.php:23 and 2025_01_01_000069_create_expense_categories_and_attachments.php:36-55; StorePurchaseRequest.php:26; grep vat_number in PurchaseService.php -> none; DocumentTransactionLink.php:89-90; C PH1-16 (unlinked received documents purgeable after 365 days; local disk storage). | No control that claimed input VAT is backed by a valid tax invoice (supplier VAT number, invoice number, document); evidence durability depends on retention/storage configuration.<br>*Risk:* Input VAT unsupported by tax invoices can be claimed and later disallowed on audit.<br>*Note:* NEW GAP: no evidence requirement/validation for input VAT (supplier invoice no. optional, supplier VAT no. unchecked) - not covered by any finding; PH1-16 covers only retention of uploaded documents. Candidate configurable policy (warn vs block). | PH1-16, CON-08<br>**P2** | REM-36, REM-32 |
| **R1-164** · Timing<br>Input tax may be deducted in a later period than the supply date, but not after five years from the calendar year of the supply.<br>*[IR — VAT Implementing Regulations, consolidated Apr 2025]; [TIG-EN — Tax Invoicing & Records Guideline v3, May 2026] · [IR] Art. 49(8), p.43; [TIG — Tax Invoicing & Records Guideline v3, May 2026] 9.4 p.46* | **PARTIALLY IMPLEMENTED** | Input VAT is recognised in the purchase_date period (PurchaseService.php:502), the default the rule allows. Deferring to a later period is possible only by changing the document date (no separate deduction period); back-dated documents can land in filed periods; there is no five-year limit check.<br>*Evidence:* PurchaseService.php:74,502; StorePurchaseRequest.php:24 (purchase_date nullable\|date, no bounds); B ACC-04 (BPeriodLockProbeTest: back-dated documents change filed periods); B ACC-03 matrix (no period structure). | No deduction-period field distinct from the supply date, no protection of filed periods, no 5-year expiry control.<br>*Risk:* Late supplier invoices either restate filed periods or are dated falsely; deductions beyond five years are not blocked.<br>*Note:* Uncovered minor element: the five-year deduction limit (no finding); low risk where accounting period locks are used. | ACC-04, ACC-03, CON-08<br>**P1** | REM-28, REM-32 |
| **R1-165** · Restricted expenses<br>Not deductible (unless re-supplied as taxable): entertainment/sport/cultural services; hospitality and catering (unless legally required for employees); employee/dependant insurance or health care (unless legally required); purchase/lease, insurance, repair and fuel of restricted vehicles (≤10 persons, not exclusively business/resale/emergency); goods/services for personal use.<br>*[IR — VAT Implementing Regulations, consolidated Apr 2025] · Art. 50(1)-(4), p.44 (amended 24-06-01)* | **NOT IMPLEMENTED** | Expense categories hold only name/description/active (migration 000069:23-30); every expense, purchase and asset posts its VAT as input VAT (ExpenseService.php:32; AssetService.php:34). There is no non-deductible flag for entertainment, hospitality, employee health insurance or restricted vehicles and their fuel.<br>*Evidence:* migration 2025_01_01_000069_create_expense_categories_and_attachments.php:23-30; ExpenseService.php:32,53; grep deductib\|non_deduct\|restricted in app/ -> none; CON-04 (lead finding). | Blocked input VAT cannot be excluded or reported in the box 7 adjustment column.<br>*Risk:* Input VAT on restricted expenses (cars, fuel, hospitality) is over-claimed - a common audit adjustment with penalties.<br>*Note:* Workaround: users enter such costs at 0% (VAT absorbed in the expense) - manual and error-prone. | CON-04, ACC-03<br>**P1** | REM-32, REM-28 |
| **R1-166** · Proportional deduction<br>Input tax exclusively for exempt supplies is non-deductible; mixed-use input tax is apportioned by the default fraction taxable/(taxable+exempt) of the previous calendar year (excluding capital-asset supplies and foreign-branch supplies), with an annual true-up in the final return of the year; alternative methods need ZATCA approval (≤5 years, renewable).<br>*[IR — VAT Implementing Regulations, consolidated Apr 2025] · Art. 51, pp.45-46* | **NOT IMPLEMENTED** | No exempt category and no apportionment: all input VAT is fully deductible; no prior-year ratio, annual true-up or approved alternative method.<br>*Evidence:* grep proportional\|apportion\|deductib in app/ -> none; VAT-03 (exempt sales indistinguishable); CON-04. | Cannot compute the taxable/(taxable+exempt) ratio, restrict mixed-use input VAT, or perform the year-end adjustment.<br>*Risk:* Tenants making exempt supplies over-claim input VAT.<br>*Note:* Requires VAT-03 (exempt classification) first. | CON-04, ACC-03, VAT-03<br>**P1** | REM-32, REM-28, REM-12 |
| **R1-167** · Capital assets<br>Deducted input tax on capital assets is adjusted over 6 years (movable) / 10 years (immovable) when use changes.<br>*[IR — VAT Implementing Regulations, consolidated Apr 2025] · Art. 52(1)-(3), p.47* | **NOT IMPLEMENTED** | AssetService posts acquisition VAT to 1150 once (AssetService.php:34,66-67); there is no capital-asset VAT adjustment period (6/10 years) or change-of-use tracking.<br>*Evidence:* AssetService.php:34,66-67,97; grep capital_asset\|adjustment_period\|vat_adjust\|input_tax_adjust in app/ -> none. | No annual adjustment of deducted input VAT on capital assets when taxable/exempt use changes.<br>*Risk:* Input VAT on assets later used for exempt supplies is not repaid.<br>*Note:* NEW GAP: IR Art. 52 capital-asset adjustment is not named in any finding (CON-04 covers Art. 51 proportional deduction, its prerequisite). Relevant mainly to mixed taxable/exempt tenants (e.g. future leases module). | CON-04, CON-08<br>**P2** | REM-32 |
| **R1-168** · Reverse charge<br>Where the Agreement makes the taxable customer liable for a supply from a non-resident, tax is paid via the reverse charge: the customer declares output tax and any deductible input tax in the same return (box 9).<br>*[IR — VAT Implementing Regulations, consolidated Apr 2025]; [IEG-EN — Imports & Exports Guideline 2nd ed., May 2026]; [TIG-EN — Tax Invoicing & Records Guideline v3, May 2026] · [IR] Art. 47(1), p.38; [IEG — Imports & Exports Guideline 2nd ed., May 2026] 6.3 p.32, 10.4 p.60; [TIG — Tax Invoicing & …* | **NOT IMPLEMENTED** | No reverse-charge mechanism: purchases from non-resident suppliers post only the supplier amount; partners.country and products.type are not used by tax logic; the report has no box 9.<br>*Evidence:* grep reverse_charge\|self_account in app/ -> none; migration 000003:33,69 (country, type) unused in app/Services/Accounting and Reporting (grep); ReportService.php:475-487; CON-04. | The customer cannot self-account output VAT and deduct input VAT on imported services in the same return (box 9).<br>*Risk:* RCM VAT is omitted from returns - an incorrect-return exposure even when net-zero.<br>*Note:* Almost every business imports some services (software, advertising), so practical relevance is high. | CON-04, ACC-03<br>**P1** | REM-32, REM-28 |
| **R1-169** · Marketplaces<br>An electronic marketplace facilitating supplies of non-resident suppliers (services) or unregistered resident suppliers (goods/services) is deemed to buy and re-supply in its own name and is liable for VAT, unless the supplier is explicitly named in the transaction, contracts, invoice and receipt, and the marketplace does not set terms/price, charge/collect, or handle complaints.<br>*[IR — VAT Implementing Regulations, consolidated Apr 2025] · Art. 47(2)-(4), pp.38-39 (added/amended 24-06-01)* | **NOT APPLICABLE** | AWJ Commerce is a single-merchant channel per tenant; marketplace behaviour is explicitly excluded (AWJ_COMMERCE_IMPLEMENTATION_MASTER_PLAN.md:81,828) and «متجرنا» is a channel, not a multi-merchant marketplace (ADR-05 section 18). No marketplace code exists; delivery-platform imported orders stay blocked (OD-DG-3).<br>*Evidence:* none (see reason): outside current product scope; grep marketplace in app/ -> none; docs/plans/store/AWJ_COMMERCE_IMPLEMENTATION_MASTER_PLAN.md:81,828; docs/plans/store/ADR-05-CUSTOMER-MOBILE-IDENTITY-BOUNDARY.md:338-346. | none (not in product scope)<br>*Risk:* none identified<br>*Note:* Re-assess if «متجرنا» becomes multi-merchant or AWJ collects payments in its own name for unregistered tenants (deemed-supplier risk for AWJ). Storefront VAT recognition gap is ACC-08. | —<br>**—** | — |
| **R1-170** · Import VAT<br>Import VAT is collected by Customs on entry; with ZATCA approval (monthly filer, ≥1 import/month, clean 12-month compliance, financial stability) it may instead be accounted for in the return (box 9 instead of box 8).<br>*[IR — VAT Implementing Regulations, consolidated Apr 2025]; [IEG-EN — Imports & Exports Guideline 2nd ed., May 2026] · [IR] Art. 43-44, pp.34-35; [IEG — Imports & Exports Guideline 2nd ed., May 2026] 5.3 p.24* | **NOT IMPLEMENTED** | No customs/import VAT document or classification: import VAT can only be keyed as ordinary input VAT on a purchase/expense, indistinguishable in the report; deferred import VAT accounted in the return is unsupported.<br>*Evidence:* grep customs\|import_vat in app/ -> none (only product-import tooling); ReportService.php:475-487; CON-04; B ACC-03 matrix ('imports/customs VAT -> missing'). | No box 8 (paid at customs) or box 9 (deferred) handling and no customs-declaration evidence.<br>*Risk:* Import VAT is misclassified or omitted in returns; deferral-approved importers cannot self-account. | CON-04, ACC-03<br>**P1** | REM-32, REM-28 |
| **R1-171** · Deemed import<br>A resident non-registered person bringing goods > SAR 10,000 from another GCC state without proof of VAT paid there is deemed to import them.<br>*[IR — VAT Implementing Regulations, consolidated Apr 2025] · Art. 41, p.33* | **NOT APPLICABLE** | The rule addresses non-registered persons bringing goods across GCC borders; AWJ has no customs/border capability and no role in that event.<br>*Evidence:* none (see reason): obligation of a different actor (non-registered importer). | none (rule for a different actor)<br>*Risk:* none identified<br>*Note:* No AWJ capability involved. | —<br>**—** | — |
| **R1-172** · Pre-registration input tax<br>Services received within 6 months before registration and goods held at registration (not fully depreciated, not used/supplied) are deductible subject to conditions and Art. 50 restrictions.<br>*[IR — VAT Implementing Regulations, consolidated Apr 2025] · Art. 49(2)-(4), p.42* | **NOT APPLICABLE** | AWJ has no VAT-registration effective date (grep registration_date\|deregist -> none); pre-registration purchases post input VAT on their own dates, and a first-return claim would need an unclassified manual journal (ACC-11).<br>*Evidence:* none (see reason): one-off taxpayer claim; grep scope app/, database/migrations. | none (taxpayer obligation)<br>*Risk:* none identified<br>*Note:* A VAT period/return workspace (ACC-03 remediation) would be the natural place to evidence the first-return claim and the Art. 50 restrictions. | —<br>**—** | — |
| **R1-173** · Lost/stolen goods<br>Lost, damaged or stolen goods must be disclosed in the accounting records to support the related input tax; ZATCA may require evidence (police report, insurance claim).<br>*[IR — VAT Implementing Regulations, consolidated Apr 2025] · Art. 49(5), p.42* | **PARTIALLY IMPLEMENTED** | Losses can be recorded through stock-issue permits (Dr 5180 / Cr 1140 at average cost) or stocktake variances (Dr inventory_count_variance), both posted via LedgerService with source linkage (StockPermitService.php:295-304; StocktakeService.php:324-334). The loss reason is optional free text shared with internal consumption and samples, and permits/stocktakes cannot hold evidence attachments.<br>*Evidence:* StockPermitService.php:19-31,295-304; StocktakeService.php:324-334; StoreStockPermitRequest.php:21 (reason nullable); migration 2025_01_01_000042:45; DocumentTransactionLink.php:89-90 (links purchase/expense only). | Loss, damage or theft is not a structured, mandatory classification, so losses are indistinguishable from own use or samples; nowhere to attach police/insurance evidence.<br>*Risk:* Input VAT on lost goods may be challenged, and own-use or samples can be booked as 'losses' without output VAT.<br>*Note:* NEW GAP (low): no finding covers loss classification/evidence; overlaps the deemed-supply gap (R1-141/142). ACC-10 (revision log pruned after 2 years) also touches stock_permit/stocktake audit trails. | CON-08<br>**P2** | REM-32 |

### R1 2.J VAT return, periods, payment, corrections

| Official requirement (source · section · version) | Status | AWJ implementation · evidence | Gap · risk | Findings · severity | Tasks |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| **R1-174** · Tax period<br>Monthly tax period if annual taxable supplies exceeded SAR 40,000,000 in the preceding 12 months; otherwise quarterly; others may apply for monthly periods; may revert to quarterly after 2 years if below the threshold.<br>*[IR — VAT Implementing Regulations, consolidated Apr 2025] · Art. 58, pp.54-55* | **NOT IMPLEMENTED** | No tax-period model: VAT figures come only from GET /api/reports/tax-report with free from/to dates (GeneralReportRequest.php:17-23; ReportService.php:475-487). No monthly/quarterly frequency setting and no trailing-12-month SAR 40M threshold tracking; users can only approximate a period by typing dates.<br>*Evidence:* ACC-03 (matrix: 'period selection → free dates, no monthly/quarterly'); ACC-04 (BPeriodLockProbeTest: 'any VAT-period/filing record model exists?: no'); grep vat_return\|tax_period\|VatPeriod\|filing_frequency\|quarterly over app/, database/migrations, routes, config → only recurring-invoice frequency (migration 000019:23); web/src grep → only the 'not a tax filing' hint (ar.json:5236). | No configurable filing frequency, no derivation of monthly vs quarterly from taxable supplies (SAR 40M), no period calendar/entity to which returns and corrections attach.<br>*Risk:* Returns must be assembled manually per period, raising the chance of wrong-period, late or incorrect returns.<br>*Note:* The period is fixed by law/ZATCA; the ERP capability is to hold the assigned frequency and produce per-period figures. Free date filtering is only a workaround. | ACC-03, ACC-04<br>**P1** | REM-28 |
| **R1-175** · Filing & payment deadline<br>The return must be filed, and the net tax paid, no later than the last day of the month following the end of the tax period; the return is a self-assessment.<br>*[IR — VAT Implementing Regulations, consolidated Apr 2025] · Art. 62(1) p.57; Art. 59(1) p.55* | **NOT APPLICABLE** | AWJ neither files nor pays VAT; it offers a 3-figure VAT control summary (ReportService.php:475-487; ReportController.php:198-208) labelled 'not a tax filing' (en.json:5236). No filing calendar, reminder or filed/paid state exists.<br>*Evidence:* none (see reason) — filing/payment deadline is a taxpayer obligation; context ACC-03 (no filing state). | none (taxpayer obligation); AWJ provides no deadline tracking or filing state.<br>*Risk:* none identified for AWJ directly; the taxpayer carries late-filing/payment exposure (R1-201/202).<br>*Note:* Supporting capability: VAT control summary with client CSV/PDF/print export (general-advanced-reports-workspace.tsx:196-206, 255-258). A filing calendar/state would fall under ACC-03 remediation. | ACC-03<br>**P1** | REM-28 |
| **R1-176** · Return content<br>ZATCA may require disclosure of: (a) total standard- and zero-rated supplies and total output tax; (b) total supplies received and total input tax deducted; (c) total deemed supplies; (d) total supplies received under reverse charge; (e) total intra-GCC supplies; (f) total import VAT declared in the return and input tax on all imports; (g) total exempt supplies; (h) any other supplies; (i) input-tax adjustments under Arts 51-52; (j) corrections …<br>*[IR — VAT Implementing Regulations, consolidated Apr 2025] · Art. 62(2)(a)-(j), p.57* | **PARTIALLY IMPLEMENTED** | Only total output tax and total input tax are produced, as net movements of accounts 2120/1150 (ReportService.php:475-487); they reconcile to posted documents. No supply values, no zero-rated/exempt split, no deemed, reverse-charge, intra-GCC or import figures, no Art. 51-52 adjustments and no prior-return corrections.<br>*Evidence:* B PC-5 (BMixedScenarioProbeTest: 2120 net 33,800 = Σ docs; taxReport March {input 11,250, output 33,800}); ACC-03 matrix; VAT-03 (tax_rate=0 conflates Z/E/O); CON-04; ACC-01/ACC-02 (BRoutingVatProbeTest: output VAT 0 after tax_output remap); ACC-11 (manual VAT journal moved report); ACC-08/CON-03 (storefront orders and advances never reach 2120); grep reverse.?charge\|customs\|deemed\|intra.?gcc in app/ → none; AssetService.php has create/post/depreciate only. | Items (a)/(b) supply values, (c) deemed supplies, (d) reverse charge, (e) intra-GCC, (f) import VAT, (g) exempt supplies, (h) other supplies, (i) Art. 51-52 adjustments and (j) corrections are absent; the two totals can be silently wrong after VAT-role remapping, include unclassified manual adjustments, and omit uninvoiced storefront sales and advances.<br>*Risk:* Return disclosures must be compiled outside AWJ from incomplete data, risking an incorrect return and the Art. 42 fine on any tax shortfall.<br>*Note:* NEW GAP (partial): no existing finding covers (c) deemed supplies (no deemed/nominal-supply concept anywhere in app/, grep empty) or (i) IR Art. 52 capital-asset input-tax adjustments (AssetService.php has no change-of-use VAT logic); CON-04 covers Art. 50/51 only. | ACC-03, VAT-03, CON-04, ACC-01, ACC-02, ACC-11, ACC-08, CON-03, CON-08<br>**P1** | REM-28, REM-12, REM-32, REM-05, REM-06, REM-30, REM-29 |
| **R1-177** · Corrections<br>Under-declaration: notify ZATCA within 20 days of becoming aware and correct the prior return; if the net under-declaration is below SAR 15,000 it may instead be added to the return for the period of discovery; over-declaration may be deducted in any later return; no correction after 5 years from the end of the calendar year of the period; a correction states the periods, the output/input tax corrected, and the reason.<br>*[IR — VAT Implementing Regulations, consolidated Apr 2025]; [TIG-EN — Tax Invoicing & Records Guideline v3, May 2026] · [IR] Art. 63(1)-(5), pp.57-58 (amended 24-06-01); [TIG — Tax Invoicing & Records Guideline v3, May 2026] 10.4 p.49* | **NOT IMPLEMENTED** | No return entity, filed snapshot or correction workflow. A back-dated document silently changes a prior period (ledger date = document date, B PC-8); the only barrier is an optional manual company-wide accounting period lock (AccountingDateGuard/AccountingPeriodLockService, B PC-4). Manual journals can move VAT accounts with a free-text description only.<br>*Evidence:* ACC-04 (BPeriodLockProbeTest: 'filed' January output VAT 15,000 → 0 after a back-dated credit note; no VAT-period model); B PC-4 (period locks enforced for post/reverse); ACC-11 (ManualJournalService accepts 1150/2120 without reason code); ACC-03. | Cannot detect post-filing differences per period, quantify net under/over-declaration, apply the SAR 15,000 threshold, track the 20-day notification and 5-year limit, or record corrected periods, corrected output/input tax and reason.<br>*Risk:* Undetected or undocumented prior-period differences expose the taxpayer to incorrect-return fines (up to 50% of the difference).<br>*Note:* Notifying ZATCA within 20 days is a taxpayer act; the ERP gap is detection, quantification and evidencing of corrections. | ACC-04, ACC-03, ACC-11<br>**P1** | REM-28 |
| **R1-178** · Box structure<br>The return form (as shown in the May-2026 Retail guideline) has boxes 1, 1.2, 2-6 (sales), 7-12 (purchases), 13-16 (totals), each with Amount/Adjustment columns in practice.<br>*[RSG-EN — Retail Sector Guideline issue 3, May 2026]; [SFG — Simplified VAT filing guide]; [IEG-EN — Imports & Exports Guideline 2nd ed., May 2026] · [RSG — Retail Sector Guideline issue 3, May 2026] 5.3.2 table pp.29-30; [SFG] pp.6-7, 12-13; [IEG — Imports & Exports Guideline 2nd ed., May 2026] …* | **NOT IMPLEMENTED** | The only VAT output is input VAT, output VAT, net VAT and a payable/recoverable status (ReportService.php:475-487; ReportController.php:198-208); the UI renders two rows plus net (general-advanced-reports-workspace.tsx:196-203, 522). No box 1-16 mapping and no Amount/Adjustment columns.<br>*Evidence:* ACC-03 (box capability matrix); VAT-03 (no category to drive boxes 1-5 and 10-11); CON-04 (boxes 8-9 unsupported); grep vat_return\|tax_period in app/ and web/src → nothing beyond the control summary. | No box classification of sales (1, 1.2, 2-6), purchases (7-12) or totals (13-16), and no Amount vs Adjustment split; box 15/16 carry-forward absent.<br>*Risk:* The return form must be filled manually from GL balances, making misallocation between boxes likely.<br>*Note:* Box list per register §3 (Retail Guideline Issue 3, May 2026). | ACC-03, VAT-03, CON-04<br>**P1** | REM-28, REM-12, REM-32 |
| **R1-179** · Box 2<br>"Sales where the State bears the VAT" (private education/health to citizens) is a separate sales box.<br>*[RSG-EN — Retail Sector Guideline issue 3, May 2026]; [SFG — Simplified VAT filing guide] · [RSG — Retail Sector Guideline issue 3, May 2026] p.29; [SFG] p.7 (category 2 example)* | **NOT IMPLEMENTED** | No VAT category or exemption-reason model (only an integer tax_rate per line, migration 000004:55), so supplies under VATEX-SA-EDU/HEA cannot be recorded or isolated, and the VAT summary has no sales boxes.<br>*Evidence:* VAT-03 (grep VATEX\|tax_category\|exempt in app/ → none; probe S9 'TaxCategory ABSENT'); ACC-03; grep national_id in app/ → Employee model only (no buyer NAT ID). | Cannot record citizens' private education/healthcare supplies with VATEX-SA-EDU/HEA and NAT buyer ID, nor report them in box 2.<br>*Risk:* Private schools and clinics cannot report State-borne-VAT sales correctly, risking incorrect returns and invalid e-invoices.<br>*Note:* Linked to R1-232 (NAT ID mandatory for EDU/HEA). | VAT-03, ACC-03<br>**P1** | REM-12, REM-28 |
| **R1-180** · Box 1.2<br>Standard-rated sales to government entities (15%) are reported in a dedicated sub-box 1.2.<br>*[RSG-EN — Retail Sector Guideline issue 3, May 2026] · 5.3.2 p.29* | **NOT IMPLEMENTED** | Partners carry only entity_type individual\|commercial (StorePartnerRequest.php:20; migration 000003:19); there is no government-entity flag, no B2G transaction handling (ZatcaService.php:254 emits only 0100000/0200000) and no sales boxes in the VAT summary.<br>*Evidence:* ACC-03 (no classification/boxes); PH2-13 (B2G and other transaction flags unsupported); grep government\|b2g\|customer_type in Partner model, partner requests and migrations → none. | Standard-rated sales to government entities cannot be identified or reported in sub-box 1.2, and the Art. 20(5) government tax point cannot be applied.<br>*Risk:* B2G sales would be reported in box 1 and possibly in the wrong period. | ACC-03, PH2-13<br>**P1** | REM-28, REM-16 |
| **R1-181** · Values excl. VAT<br>Sales and purchases are entered exclusive of VAT; VAT is computed by the portal; entering VAT-inclusive purchases overstates deductions (listed as a common error).<br>*[SFG — Simplified VAT filing guide] · pp.4, 6, 8, 12* | **PARTIALLY IMPLEMENTED** | Invoices and purchases store net (excl.-VAT) bases with exact inclusive-price extraction (InvoiceService.php:1188-1196), but the VAT summary shows no sales/purchase values. Expenses and assets are exclusive-only, so a gross receipt typed as amount gets 15% added; generic returns and credit notes treat an inclusive price as net.<br>*Evidence:* A positive control (probe S3: 100.00 incl → 86.96/13.04 exact); ACC-03 (no box values); VAT-14 (probe E1: 115.00 entered → VAT 17.25, ExpenseService.php:53-54); VAT-01 (A-returns.log R2: inclusive 115.00 returned as 115.00 + VAT 17.25); VAT-02 (R4: credit note adds 15% on an inclusive price). | No excl.-VAT sales/purchase values per box; the 'VAT-inclusive amount entered as net' error the guide warns about is built into expense/asset capture and into generic returns/credit notes.<br>*Risk:* Overstated input VAT and over-reversed output VAT flow into the return, under-declaring net tax. | ACC-03, VAT-14, VAT-01, VAT-02<br>**P0** | REM-28, REM-10, REM-01, REM-02 |
| **R1-182** · Box 7 contents<br>Only deductible standard-rated purchases supported by a compliant tax invoice (Art. 53) and meeting Art. 49 go in box 7; non-deductible items (Art. 50) are excluded or entered in the adjustment column; purchases of later periods excluded; invoices of prior periods allowed if not previously deducted.<br>*[SFG — Simplified VAT filing guide] · pp.6, 12-13* | **PARTIALLY IMPLEMENTED** | Input VAT of posted purchases, expenses and assets is aggregated by document date into 1150 and shown as one figure (ReportService.php:475-487; B PC-5, PC-8). There is no deductibility attribute (Art. 50/51), no check that the supplier document is a compliant tax invoice (supplier_invoice_no optional, StorePurchaseRequest.php:26) and no box-7 value.<br>*Evidence:* B PC-5/PC-8 (B-mix: 1150 net 11,250 = Σ purchase+expense−return VAT); CON-04; grep deductib\|non_deductible in app/ → none; ACC-04 (back-dated documents land in their own, possibly filed, period); VAT-14 (inclusive receipts overstate input VAT); ACC-01 (BRoutingVatProbeTest: tax_input remapped to cash accepted). | Restricted (Art. 50) and exempt-related input VAT are not excluded; a late-received prior-period supplier invoice is booked into the prior (possibly filed) period instead of the current return; no evidence check; zero/exempt purchases are not separated from box 7.<br>*Risk:* Box 7 and the deductible input VAT can be overstated, producing an incorrect return.<br>*Note:* IR Art. 49(8) allows deduction in a later period (R1-164); AWJ has no separate deduction-period field. | CON-04, ACC-03, ACC-04, VAT-14, ACC-01<br>**P1** | REM-32, REM-28, REM-10, REM-05 |
| **R1-183** · Boxes 8/9<br>Import VAT paid at customs → box 8; VAT on imports accounted through the return (approved deferral) and reverse-charge VAT → box 9 (output and input on the same return; partial non-deductible portion via the box-9 adjustment column); exempt imports are not disclosed.<br>*[IEG-EN — Imports & Exports Guideline 2nd ed., May 2026]; [TIG-EN — Tax Invoicing & Records Guideline v3, May 2026] · [IEG — Imports & Exports Guideline 2nd ed., May 2026] 5.2 p.23 ("must not be disclosed in the VAT return"), 5.3 p.24, 6.3 p.32 (example), 10.4 p.60; [TIG — Tax Invoicing & Records …* | **NOT IMPLEMENTED** | No import, customs-VAT, deferred-import or reverse-charge mechanism exists; input VAT is only what purchases, expenses and assets post to 1150, and no output VAT is self-assessed on imported services.<br>*Evidence:* CON-04; ACC-03 matrix ('imports/customs VAT → missing; reverse charge → missing'); grep reverse.?charge\|customs\|import_vat\|imported_service in app/ and database/migrations → no matches. | Cannot record import VAT paid at customs (box 8), self-assess reverse-charge or deferred-import VAT with matching output/input (box 9) or its non-deductible part, or keep exempt imports out of the return.<br>*Risk:* Importers and buyers of foreign services would under-declare output VAT or misstate input VAT in boxes 8/9. | CON-04, ACC-03<br>**P1** | REM-32, REM-28 |
