| **R1-184** · Adjustments field<br>Output-tax decreases from credit notes issued after the original return go to the Adjustments field; increases go to the Amount field of the period of the event; corrections of previous periods in box 14.<br>*[TIG-EN — Tax Invoicing & Records Guideline v3, May 2026]; [RSG-EN — Retail Sector Guideline issue 3, May 2026] · [TIG — Tax Invoicing & Records Guideline v3, May 2026] 7.1.1 p.38; [RSG — Retail Sector Guideline issue 3, May 2026] p.30 (box 14)* | **PARTIALLY IMPLEMENTED** | Credit notes and sales returns post on their own note/return date (B PC-8), so the decrease lands in the period of the note, but it is netted invisibly into output VAT: no Amount/Adjustment split and no box 14. Back-dated notes can rewrite a filed period, and credit-note VAT always debits fixed account 2120 (CreditNoteService.php:190-204).<br>*Evidence:* B PC-8; ACC-03 ('adjustments → netted invisibly'); ACC-04 (BPeriodLockProbeTest: back-dated CN changed filed January output VAT 15,000 → 0); CON-02 (no adjustment-period control); ACC-02 (BRoutingVatProbeTest: CN still debits 2120 after remap → report −15,000); ACC-06 (CN of 10× the invoice accepted). | No separation of post-return credit-note decreases into the Adjustments field, no box-14 corrections, no protection of filed periods, no IR Art. 40(5) 'whichever later' control, and adjustment amounts can exceed the original invoice.<br>*Risk:* Adjustments may be reported in the wrong field or period or exceed the declared output VAT. | ACC-03, ACC-04, CON-02, ACC-02, ACC-06<br>**P1** | REM-28, REM-31, REM-06, REM-02 |
| **R1-185** · Payment mechanics<br>Tax is paid to ZATCA's bank account quoting the TIN and the period(s); ZATCA keeps a tax account per taxpayer; payments are applied first to the period paid, then to penalties of earlier periods, then oldest balances; set-off against other taxes allowed.<br>*[IR — VAT Implementing Regulations, consolidated Apr 2025] · Art. 59(3)-(8), p.55* | **NOT APPLICABLE** | AWJ has no VAT-settlement or ZATCA-payment feature and no VAT-payable/settlement account in the seeded chart (ChartOfAccountsSeeder.php:35,49 seed only 1150/2120). Recording the payment needs a manual journal against 2120/1150, which the code-based summary then nets into that period's VAT.<br>*Evidence:* none (see reason) — payment to ZATCA and tax-account application are Authority/taxpayer mechanics; context ACC-11 (BMixedScenarioProbeTest: a manual VAT journal moved the summary until reversed), ReportService.php:475-487. | none (taxpayer/Authority mechanics).<br>*Risk:* none identified for AWJ directly; booking the VAT settlement in AWJ distorts the next period's VAT summary.<br>*Note:* Supporting capability: payment vouchers and manual journals. A dedicated settlement entry excluded from the VAT summary would remove the ACC-11 distortion. | ACC-11<br>**P3** | REM-28 |
| **R1-186** · Refund / carry-forward<br>A credit balance may be claimed for refund at filing or within 5 years of the end of the calendar year; otherwise it is carried forward in the tax account; refund within 60 days of approval; refund may be refused if returns are outstanding.<br>*[IR — VAT Implementing Regulations, consolidated Apr 2025] · Art. 69, pp.63-64* | **NOT APPLICABLE** | When net VAT is negative the summary returns status 'recoverable' (ReportService.php:485), labelled 'VAT recoverable or carried forward' (en.json:5235); no refund claim, carried-forward balance or box-15 tracking exists.<br>*Evidence:* none (see reason) — Art. 69 refund/carry-forward is a ZATCA tax-account process; context ACC-03 ('carry-forward → missing'). | none (Authority/taxpayer process); the box-15/16 figure is part of R1-178.<br>*Risk:* none identified for AWJ directly.<br>*Note:* Box 15/16 computation gap is recorded under R1-178/ACC-03. | ACC-03<br>**P1** | REM-28 |
| **R1-187** · Advances in return<br>Advance payments received must be disclosed in the return of the period received (Agreement Art. 23).<br>*[SFG — Simplified VAT filing guide] · p.11 (common error 6)* | **NOT IMPLEMENTED** | Customer receipts post Dr cash/clearing, Cr accounts receivable only (PaymentService.php:343-358); an advance creates no output VAT and no prepayment (386) invoice, so advances appear in no VAT figure until a later invoice is posted.<br>*Evidence:* CON-03; B §4 ('PaymentService posts receipts to AR only… no advance receipt VAT mechanism'); grep advance\|prepay\|deposit\|عربون in app/Services/Accounting → no advance-VAT feature (only cash-bank deposit scopes). | No tax point at receipt of consideration, no advance-payment invoice/VAT, and no disclosure of advances in the return of the period received.<br>*Risk:* Output VAT on advances is declared late (in the invoice period), an under-declaration for the period of receipt. | CON-03, ACC-03<br>**P1** | REM-29, REM-28 |
| **R1-188** · Multiple CRs<br>All commercial registrations with taxable activity must be reflected in the return, not only the main one.<br>*[SFG — Simplified VAT filing guide] · p.11 (common error 5)* | **PARTIALLY IMPLEMENTED** | Without a branch filter the VAT summary aggregates all branch-tagged and null-branch journal lines of the tenant (ReportService.php:288-293), so CRs run as branches are consolidated. Branch-filtered or branch-restricted views silently drop null-branch VAT (ReportService.php:811-837), and branches carry no CR number (Branch.php:22-26).<br>*Evidence:* BMixedScenarioProbeTest (company-wide summary includes NULL-branch VAT lines) and BPosApiProbeTest (branch-tagged POS VAT included, output 60.00); ACC-12 (filtered summary omits NULL-branch lines; no 'unallocated' bucket unlike incomeStatement); ACC-03. | Branch views under-report company-wide VAT without warning; no CR attribute to evidence that every CR is covered; CRs operated as separate AWJ tenants cannot be consolidated (usage NOT VERIFIED).<br>*Risk:* A user relying on branch-level figures could omit some CRs' VAT from the return.<br>*Note:* The consolidated (unfiltered) view is complete per code and probes. | ACC-12, ACC-03<br>**P1** | REM-28 |

### R1 2.K Records and archiving (IR Art. 66, Resolution Clause Fifth)

| Official requirement (source · section · version) | Status | AWJ implementation · evidence | Gap · risk | Findings · severity | Tasks |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| **R1-189** · Retention period<br>Invoices, records and accounting documents must be kept at least 6 years from the end of the tax period they relate to; records of capital assets for the Art. 52 adjustment period (6/10 years) plus 5 years from acquisition (TIG: real-estate records "not less than 15 years").<br>*[IR — VAT Implementing Regulations, consolidated Apr 2025]; [TIG-EN — Tax Invoicing & Records Guideline v3, May 2026] · [IR] Art. 66(1), p.61; [TIG — Tax Invoicing & Records Guideline v3, May 2026] 8.3.3 p.42* | **PARTIALLY IMPLEMENTED** | Invoices, journals and documents stay in the database (no purge job; no tenant-delete route), but no statutory floor exists: unlinked archived Document-Center files are purgeable after 365 days (config/document_center.php:41; render.yaml:41-42; DocumentRetentionPlanner.php:26-50), policy is settable 1-3650 days (DocumentRetentionPolicyService.php:29), revisions prune after 730 days (DocumentRevision.php:56), and posted records are hard-deletable at model level.<br>*Evidence:* PH1-16; ACC-10; PH1-09 (C-probe1: model delete of posted invoice succeeds, no SoftDeletes); ACC-05 (B-imm: JournalEntry->delete() true, balance left stale); grep forceDelete in app/ → none. | No 6-year floor (15 years for immovable capital assets/real estate) in any retention setting; unlinked tax documents purgeable after 1 year; change log kept 2 years; deletion not blocked at model/DB level; local-disk storage durability unverified.<br>*Risk:* Records required for 6-15 years may be lost, exposing the taxpayer to record-keeping fines per tax period.<br>*Note:* The 3650-day cap cannot express the 15-year real-estate period. Whether retention runs execute in production is NOT VERIFIED (manual/governed runs only). | PH1-16, ACC-10, PH1-09, ACC-05<br>**P2** | REM-36, REM-33, REM-07 |
| **R1-190** · Arabic records<br>Records must be kept in Arabic; all invoices issued in Arabic in addition to any other language.<br>*[IR — VAT Implementing Regulations, consolidated Apr 2025] · Art. 66(2), p.61* | **PARTIALLY IMPLEMENTED** | Arabic is the default and the chart of accounts is seeded with Arabic names (ChartOfAccountsSeeder.php:114); documents support 'ar' and 'bilingual'. However document language 'en' is accepted (StoreInvoiceRequest.php:49; PrintTemplateContract.php:47) and v2 compositions then render English-only labels.<br>*Evidence:* PH1-15 (code-level: modern-bilingual-label.tsx:35-36, use-document-label-mode.ts:16-17; legacy compositions follow the UI locale); InvoiceDocumentLanguageTest (15 passed) asserts language persistence, not Arabic presence. | A tax invoice or note can be issued without Arabic; nothing forces Arabic (or bilingual) output for tax documents.<br>*Risk:* English-only tax invoices breach the Arabic-language rule (IR Art. 53(5), 66(2)) and are not valid tax invoices.<br>*Note:* Rendering was not executed in the sandbox (C §4 item 4). | PH1-15<br>**P2** | REM-34 |
| **R1-191** · Location<br>Invoices, documents and records must be kept in KSA, on paper or electronically through access to the servers/databases holding them via a terminal/connection in KSA.<br>*[IR — VAT Implementing Regulations, consolidated Apr 2025]; [TIG-EN — Tax Invoicing & Records Guideline v3, May 2026] · [IR] Art. 66(3), p.61 (amended 9 Nov 2021); [TIG — Tax Invoicing & Records Guideline v3, May 2026] 8.3.1 p.41 (server in KSA for residents; workstation access for PEs)* | **NOT VERIFIED** | The repo deployment blueprint puts the API and PostgreSQL in Render 'frankfurt' (render.yaml:11,18); deploy/DEPLOY.md:107-110 also describes a Railway environment, and an R2 disk exists but is disabled (config/filesystems.php:61-68; render.yaml DOCUMENT_DURABLE_STORAGE_ENABLED=false). No KSA-residency control, setting or documentation exists.<br>*Evidence:* CON-01 (lead verification log: render.yaml region frankfurt, no KSA-residency statement); no production hosting evidence available in the sandbox. | Production location of DB, compute, file storage and backups is unknown; the repo default is outside KSA and no KSA terminal-access arrangement is documented.<br>*Risk:* If production records sit outside KSA without compliant access, tenants breach the record-location rule (Art. 45 fines per period).<br>*Note:* Settle with: live provider/region of DB, compute, object storage and backups; KSA terminal-access arrangement; legal reading of IR Art. 66(3) as amended 9 Nov 2021 (access via a KSA terminal) vs TIG v3 §8.3.1 (residents' server in KSA). See GM4_conflicts.md. | CON-01<br>**P1** | REM-00A, REM-36 |
| **R1-192** · Electronic storage conditions<br>If kept electronically: (a) accessible and extractable from the computer system on request; (b) data entered in Arabic where possible and identical to the books; (c) original supporting documents for all entries retained and produced on request; (d) closing accounts/balance sheet may be produced directly from the system; adjusting entries in Arabic where possible; (e) document the data-entry and processing system for review; (f) security …<br>*[IR — VAT Implementing Regulations, consolidated Apr 2025] · Art. 66(3)(a)-(h), pp.61-62* | **PARTIALLY IMPLEMENTED** | Met: (a) owner JSON export incl. invoice XML (AccountSettingsController.php:83-169) and report CSV/PDF; (d) TB/IS/BS from the system (ReportService.php:53,96,226); (f) service/API immutability. Not met: export omits credit notes, expenses, assets and scans; (c) scans purgeable; (f) model/DB tampering possible, audit log partial and prunable; (h) e-invoice XML non-compliant, no e-notes, cleared XML unused.<br>*Evidence:* B PC-3, C PC-4; PH1-16; ACC-05, PH1-09, SEC-05 (B-imm, C-probe1, F04: model-level edit/delete of posted records); ACC-10; PH2-04/PH1-17 (SDK 18/18 FAILED); PH2-05/PH1-06 (no note XML); SUB-04 (zatca_cleared_xml has no reader); PH1-02 (reprints use live seller/buyer data). | Tamper controls only at service/API level; audit log partial and prunable; supporting documents purgeable; e-invoices not stored in the prescribed format and notes not e-invoiced; cleared XML not served; bulk export incomplete; (e) taxpayer-facing system documentation NOT VERIFIED.<br>*Risk:* Electronic records may fail ZATCA's extraction, integrity and format conditions at audit.<br>*Note:* (g) ZATCA's right to audit systems is an Authority right (n/a). | PH1-16, ACC-05, PH1-09, SEC-05, ACC-10, PH2-04, PH1-17, PH2-05, PH1-06, SUB-04, PH1-02, CON-09<br>**P0** | REM-36, REM-07, REM-33, REM-16, REM-18, REM-24, REM-14 |
| **R1-193** · Third-party custody<br>A resident taxable person may appoint a KSA-established third party to keep invoices/records but remains responsible; non-residents without a tax representative must appoint one.<br>*[IR — VAT Implementing Regulations, consolidated Apr 2025] · Art. 66(4)-(5), p.62* | **NOT APPLICABLE** | As SaaS host, AWJ's operator is the de facto third-party custodian of tenants' records; nothing in the repo evidences the operator's KSA establishment, and the hosting blueprint is outside KSA (render.yaml:11,18).<br>*Evidence:* none (see reason) — legal/contractual arrangement between taxpayer and custodian; related CON-01. | none (legal obligation); custodian KSA establishment unverified.<br>*Risk:* none identified for the system; tenants stay liable if their custodian is not KSA-established.<br>*Note:* Evidence needed outside the repo: operator's KSA commercial registration, data-processing agreement, KSA hosting/terminal access. | CON-01<br>**P1** | REM-00A, REM-36 |
| **R1-194** · Audit access<br>During audits the taxpayer must allow access to all books, records, invoices and documents; if kept on a computer system, provide paper copies or electronic files on request; ZATCA may assess within 5 years of the end of the calendar year of the period (20 years for evasion/non-registration).<br>*[IR — VAT Implementing Regulations, consolidated Apr 2025]; [TIG-EN — Tax Invoicing & Records Guideline v3, May 2026] · [IR] Art. 64(3)-(9), pp.58-60; [TIG — Tax Invoicing & Records Guideline v3, May 2026] 8.1 p.40* | **PARTIALLY IMPLEMENTED** | Electronic files can be produced: owner-only JSON export of invoices (incl. ZATCA XML/QR), purchases, returns, payments and journals (routes/api.php:357; AccountSettingsController.php:117-168, available even with an expired subscription); CSV/PDF/print of ledger, journal and VAT reports (general-advanced-reports-workspace.tsx:206-258); per-invoice print/PDF. The export omits credit notes, expenses, assets and Document-Center files.<br>*Evidence:* Code read of the export payload; PH1-16 (scans purgeable after 365 days, no archive format); ACC-10 (revision log pruned at 730 days; DocumentRevision.php:55 claims 2 years cover a tax review cycle); ACC-04 (filed figures not reproducible). | Bulk export incomplete; supporting scans and change history may not survive the 5-year (20-year for evasion) assessment window; no reproducible working papers for filed returns.<br>*Risk:* The taxpayer may be unable to produce complete records during an audit within the assessment window.<br>*Note:* Granting ZATCA access is the taxpayer's act; the ERP side is extraction and preservation. | PH1-16, ACC-10, ACC-04<br>**P1** | REM-36, REM-33, REM-28 |
| **R1-195** · E-invoice records<br>Subject persons keep records, e-invoices, e-notes and associated data in the Annex format/timelines and per IR Art. 66, and must provide them to the Authority on request to verify validity and compliance.<br>*[RES-EN — Implementation Resolution (EN), 19 May 2023] · Fifth, p.6; Seventh(4), p.7* | **PARTIALLY IMPLEMENTED** | Each posted invoice stores UUID, ICV, PIH, hash, QR and XML (invoices.zatca_*), retrievable per invoice (GET /invoices/{id}/zatca, InvoiceController.php:341-359) and in bulk via the owner JSON export. The XML is a non-compliant skeleton, credit/debit notes have no e-note at all, and cleared XML is stored but never served.<br>*Evidence:* D PC-1 (hash identical to SDK, 19/19 samples); PH2-04/PH1-17 (SDK validate 18/18 FAILED); PH2-05, PH1-06 (credit_notes/return_documents have no zatca columns); SUB-04 (zatca_cleared_xml has no reader); SEC-05/PH1-09 (stored XML/hash mutable at model level); PH1-16 (no archive format). | E-invoices not kept in the Annex format; e-notes absent; cleared versions not provided; integrity of stored XML neither protected nor verified.<br>*Risk:* On ZATCA request the taxpayer cannot provide valid e-invoices/e-notes (e-invoicing violation #13, R1-218).<br>*Note:* Register row notes cite 'Violation #13 (R1-217)'; #13 is R1-218. | PH2-04, PH1-17, PH2-05, PH1-06, SUB-04, SEC-05, PH1-09, PH1-16<br>**P0** | REM-16, REM-18, REM-24, REM-07, REM-36 |
| **R1-196** · Additional records<br>ZATCA may request additional records by notice with reasonable time (TIG: 20 days or reasonable period).<br>*[IR — VAT Implementing Regulations, consolidated Apr 2025]; [TIG-EN — Tax Invoicing & Records Guideline v3, May 2026] · [IR] Art. 66(6), pp.62-63; [TIG — Tax Invoicing & Records Guideline v3, May 2026] 8.4 p.43* | **NOT APPLICABLE** | Authority right to request additional records; AWJ supports responses through the owner JSON export, report CSV/PDF/print and per-document print/PDF.<br>*Evidence:* none (see reason) — Authority/taxpayer process; related PH1-16 (no archive format; scans purgeable). | none (Authority/taxpayer process).<br>*Risk:* none identified for AWJ directly. | PH1-16<br>**P2** | REM-36 |
| **R1-197** · Records for return<br>The simplified filing guide reminds that records, invoices and documents must be kept for not less than 6 years from the end of the tax period.<br>*[SFG — Simplified VAT filing guide] · p.11 (error 4)* | **PARTIALLY IMPLEMENTED** | Source documents and journals are retained with no purge job, but the VAT figures behind a filed return are not preserved: back-dated postings change past periods and no return snapshot exists. Supporting scans (365 days) and change logs (730 days) have sub-statutory retention.<br>*Evidence:* ACC-04 (BPeriodLockProbeTest: filed figure drift); PH1-16; ACC-10; PH1-09, ACC-05 (model-level deletion of posted records). | No 6-year preservation of return working figures; supporting documents and audit trail may be purged before 6 years.<br>*Risk:* The taxpayer cannot substantiate filed returns for the full retention period. | ACC-04, PH1-16, ACC-10, PH1-09, ACC-05<br>**P1** | REM-28, REM-36, REM-33, REM-07 |

### R1 2.L Penalties and violations

| Official requirement (source · section · version) | Status | AWJ implementation · evidence | Gap · risk | Findings · severity | Tasks |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| **R1-198** · Evasion<br>Submitting false/forged documents, returns, records or information to evade/reduce tax or obtain undue refunds; and moving goods in/out of KSA without paying tax: fine not less than the tax due and not more than three times the value of the goods/services.<br>*[VATL — VAT Law (Royal Decree M/113, as amended)]; [WEB-PEN] · [VATL] Art. 39-40, pp.12-13* | **NOT APPLICABLE** | Penalty provision. AWJ behaviours that would yield understated returns or altered records without user intent are listed as triggers.<br>*Evidence:* none (see reason) — penalty provision (amount/procedure), not a system capability; trigger evidence is in the cited findings. | none (penalty provision; see triggers in findings).<br>*Risk:* System-generated under-statements (e.g., over-reversed output VAT) shift the burden of proving absence of intent onto the taxpayer.<br>*Note:* Triggers: wrong-VAT reversals (VAT-01/02/13), VAT silently leaving the summary after remapping (ACC-01), unbounded credit notes (ACC-06), filed figures that drift (ACC-04), silent model-level alteration of issued records (ACC-05/SEC-05/PH1-09). | VAT-01, VAT-02, VAT-13, ACC-01, ACC-06, ACC-04, ACC-05, SEC-05, PH1-09<br>**P0** | REM-01, REM-02, REM-05, REM-28, REM-07 |
| **R1-199** · Non-registration<br>Failure to apply for registration within the prescribed period: SAR 10,000.<br>*[VATL — VAT Law (Royal Decree M/113, as amended)] · Art. 41, p.13* | **NOT APPLICABLE** | Penalty provision for late VAT registration; AWJ plays no role (it does not monitor the SAR 375,000 threshold, which it is not required to do).<br>*Evidence:* none (see reason) — penalty provision (amount/procedure), not a system capability; trigger evidence is in the cited findings. | none (penalty provision; see triggers in findings).<br>*Risk:* none identified for AWJ.<br>*Note:* No AWJ trigger. | —<br>**—** | — |
| **R1-200** · Incorrect return<br>Filing an incorrect return, amending a return after filing, or submitting any document resulting in tax computed lower than due: fine equal to 50% of the difference; ZATCA may waive or reduce per Board rules. Board rules (as summarised by ZATCA, May 2026): base 25%, increased up to 50% for evasion link / poor record / large differences, reduced down to 0% for good record / small differences / payment within 30 days / micro-enterprise (≤ SAR 3M); …<br>*[VATL — VAT Law (Royal Decree M/113, as amended)]; [TIG-EN — Tax Invoicing & Records Guideline v3, May 2026] · [VATL] Art. 42(1)-(2), p.13; [TIG — Tax Invoicing & Records Guideline v3, May 2026] 11 pp.50-53* | **NOT APPLICABLE** | Penalty provision. AWJ defects that lower the tax computed in a return are listed as triggers.<br>*Evidence:* none (see reason) — penalty provision (amount/procedure), not a system capability; trigger evidence is in the cited findings. | none (penalty provision; see triggers in findings).<br>*Risk:* Each listed defect can produce a lower-than-due return, exposing the taxpayer to 25-50% of the difference.<br>*Note:* Triggers: over-reversed output VAT (VAT-01/02/13, ACC-06), truncated discount VAT (VAT-07), client-set inclusive flag (VAT-09), inclusive receipts overstating input VAT (VAT-14), routing/manual journals distorting the summary (ACC-01/02/11), unreported storefront sales and advances (ACC-08, CON-03), RCM/Art. 50 gaps (CON-04), drifting filed periods (ACC-04). | VAT-01, VAT-02, VAT-07, VAT-09, VAT-13, VAT-14, ACC-01, ACC-02, ACC-04, ACC-06, ACC-08, ACC-11, CON-03, CON-04<br>**P0** | REM-01, REM-02, REM-10, REM-05, REM-06, REM-28, REM-30, REM-29, REM-32 |
| **R1-201** · Late filing<br>Failure to file within the period: 5%-25% of the tax that should have been declared.<br>*[VATL — VAT Law (Royal Decree M/113, as amended)] · Art. 42(3), p.13* | **NOT APPLICABLE** | Penalty provision for late filing; filing happens outside AWJ.<br>*Evidence:* none (see reason) — penalty provision (amount/procedure), not a system capability; trigger evidence is in the cited findings. | none (penalty provision; see triggers in findings).<br>*Risk:* Indirect only: without an in-system return or filing state, preparation depends on manual work outside AWJ.<br>*Note:* Indirect trigger only (ACC-03: no return, no filing state/calendar). | ACC-03<br>**P1** | REM-28 |
| **R1-202** · Late payment<br>Failure to pay within the period: 5% of unpaid tax for each month or part thereof.<br>*[VATL — VAT Law (Royal Decree M/113, as amended)] · Art. 43, p.13* | **NOT APPLICABLE** | Penalty provision for late payment; payment happens outside AWJ.<br>*Evidence:* none (see reason) — penalty provision (amount/procedure), not a system capability; trigger evidence is in the cited findings. | none (penalty provision; see triggers in findings).<br>*Risk:* none identified for AWJ.<br>*Note:* No AWJ trigger. | —<br>**—** | — |

AWJ has no non-registered mode: a tenant with an empty VAT number still posts documents titled 'فاتورة ضريبية' with QR and a ZATCA-compliance footer.<br>*Evidence:* none (see reason) — penalty provision (amount/procedure), not a system capability; trigger evidence is in the cited findings. | none (penalty provision; see triggers in findings).<br>*Risk:* Tenants not (yet) VAT-registered can issue tax invoices through AWJ, exposing them to fines up to SAR 100,000.<br>*Note:* C-probe1: 'no-VAT tenant: status posted \| QR tag2 ""'. ### R1 2.M Seller / buyer identification data (legal-level data rules)

| Official requirement (source · section · version) | Status | AWJ implementation · evidence | Gap · risk | Findings · severity | Tasks |

| **R1-224** · VAT number format<br>Seller VAT registration number (or group number) is mandatory; when present, seller and buyer VAT numbers must contain 15 digits with first and last digit "3".<br>*[XML — XML Implementation Standard v1.2, 19 May 2023] · BR-KSA-39, BR-KSA-40, BR-KSA-44 p.56* | **VERIFIED DEFECT** | tenants.vat_number is a nullable string(15) (migration 000001:16); company update validates only nullable\|string\|max:20 (UpdateCompanyRequest.php:18), registration and partners only size:15 (RegisterRequest.php:28; StorePartnerRequest.php:22). Posting has no seller-VAT precondition and Phase-1 QR/XML fall back to '' (ZatcaService.php:162,296); only the Phase-2 QR encoder checks ^3\d{13}3$ (ZatcaPhaseTwoQrEncoder.php:32-34).<br>*Evidence:* PH1-04 (C-probe1: tenant without VAT → invoice posted, QR tag2 ""; C-probe3: PUT /company vat_number 12345 → 200, next QR tag2 12345; partner 'ABCDEFGHIJKLMNO' stored); PH2-16; PH2-04 (SDK BR-KSA-39 ERROR 18/18: seller VAT without cac:TaxScheme). | Seller VAT not mandatory at issuance; no 15-digit '3…3' validation for seller or buyer; XML seller VAT element structurally invalid; no VAT-group number support (grep vat_group\|group_number → none).<br>*Risk:* Invoices with missing or invalid VAT numbers are invalid tax invoices and fail ZATCA validation; buyers may lose input-VAT deduction.<br>*Note:* Lead: the seller number may be a VAT group's (R1-066: members put the group number on all invoices); no group-number model exists (folded into REM-14). Seller-VAT exposure is carried by PH1-04. | PH1-04, PH2-16, PH2-04<br>**P0** | REM-14, REM-16 |
| **R1-225** · Seller other ID<br>Exactly one seller identification with scheme CRN / MOM / MLS / 700 / SAG / OTH, alphanumeric; branch CR where multiple CRs.<br>*[XML — XML Implementation Standard v1.2, 19 May 2023]; [RES-EN — Implementation Resolution (EN), 19 May 2023] · [XML] BR-KSA-08 pp.48-49; [RES — Implementation Resolution, 19 May 2023] Annex 2 4.4 p.19* | **PARTIALLY IMPLEMENTED** | The tenant CR number (tenants.cr_number; UpdateCompanyRequest.php:19 max:50) is printed on A4/thermal documents (doc-parties.tsx:42; doc-header.tsx:111), but no scheme (CRN/MOM/MLS/700/SAG/OTH) can be chosen, branches carry no CR (Branch.php:22-26), and the XML has no seller cac:PartyIdentification (ZatcaService.php:294-299).<br>*Evidence:* C PC-13 (printed seller CRN); PH2-04 / D Appendix A (BR-KSA-08 WARN 18/18); PH1-17 (C-probe1 'PartyIdentification(CRN)=false'). | Seller other ID absent from the e-invoice; no scheme typing or alphanumeric validation; no branch-level CR for multi-CR taxpayers.<br>*Risk:* E-invoices lack a field ZATCA treats as mandatory from Phase 2, causing validation warnings and audit findings.<br>*Note:* [DG] FAQ p.64: 'Other seller ID is a mandatory field'. | PH2-04, PH1-17<br>**P0** | REM-16 |
| **R1-226** · Buyer other ID<br>If the buyer is not VAT-registered, one buyer identification with scheme TIN / CRN / MOM / MLS / 700 / SAG / NAT / GCC / IQA / PAS / OTH, alphanumeric, in priority order.<br>*[XML — XML Implementation Standard v1.2, 19 May 2023]; [RES-EN — Implementation Resolution (EN), 19 May 2023] · [XML] BR-KSA-14 pp.50-51; [RES — Implementation Resolution, 19 May 2023] Annex 2 5.4 pp.20-21* | **NOT IMPLEMENTED** | Partners store only a free-text cr_number (StorePartnerRequest.php:23); there is no buyer-ID scheme (TIN/CRN/MOM/MLS/700/SAG/NAT/GCC/IQA/PAS/OTH) or national-ID field, the buyer CR is not printed (from-invoice.ts:160-164), and the XML buyer block holds only RegistrationName (ZatcaService.php:300-304).<br>*Evidence:* PH2-04 / D Appendix A (BR-KSA-81 WARN on standard shape); PH1-05 (C-probe1: buyer XML = RegistrationName only); grep national_id\|schemeID\|'NAT' in app/ → Employee model only. | No capture, priority selection or emission of a buyer identifier for buyers without a VAT number.<br>*Risk:* Standard invoices to unregistered buyers carry persistent ZATCA warnings and weak buyer identification at audit.<br>*Note:* Missing buyer ID is a warning, not a rejection ([DG] FAQ p.64). | PH2-04, PH1-05<br>**P0** | REM-16, REM-15 |
| **R1-227** · Seller address<br>Seller address must contain street name, building number (4 digits), postal code (5 digits), city, district and country code — the Saudi National Address elements.<br>*[XML — XML Implementation Standard v1.2, 19 May 2023]; [TIG-EN — Tax Invoicing & Records Guideline v3, May 2026] · [XML] BR-KSA-09 p.49, BR-KSA-37 p.56, BR-KSA-66 p.58; [TIG — Tax Invoicing & Records Guideline v3, May 2026] 4.2.3 p.19* | **PARTIALLY IMPLEMENTED** | National-address fields (building_no, street, additional_no, district, city, postal_code, short_address) live in settings.company (CompanyProfile.php:24-35) and are printed as the seller national address (from-invoice.ts:75-88). Validation is max-length only (UpdateCompanyRequest.php:24-31: building_no max:20, postal_code max:20), and the XML has no seller PostalAddress.<br>*Evidence:* C PC-13; PH2-04 / D Appendix A (BR-08 and BR-KSA-37 WARN 18/18; BR-KSA-09 not reached); PH1-17 (C-probe1 'seller PostalAddress=false'); PH2-16 (no master-data validation before generation). | No 4-digit building / 5-digit postal-code / country-code validation; address absent from the e-invoice and not required before issuing.<br>*Risk:* E-invoices fail the KSA address rules and printed invoices may show an incomplete national address.<br>*Note:* Printed address is read live at render time (PH1-02). | PH2-04, PH1-17, PH2-16<br>**P0** | REM-16, REM-14 |
| **R1-228** · Buyer address<br>Tax invoices/notes: buyer address with street, city, country code; if the buyer country is SA, full national address (street, 4-digit building number, 5-digit postal code, city, district) is mandatory; not required on simplified documents.<br>*[XML — XML Implementation Standard v1.2, 19 May 2023] · BR-KSA-10 p.50, BR-KSA-63 p.58, BR-KSA-67 p.58* | **PARTIALLY IMPLEMENTED** | Partners hold building_no, street, district, city, postal_code and country as free strings (migration 000003:28-33; StorePartnerRequest.php:27-32). Printed documents show only buyer name, VAT and city (from-invoice.ts:160-164), the XML omits the buyer address (ZatcaService.php:300-304), and standard invoices post without it.<br>*Evidence:* PH1-05 (C-probe1: explicit standard invoice without buyer VAT/address posted); PH1-02 (buyer address never printed); PH2-04 / D Appendix A (BR-KSA-10, F-06-C23/C25 WARN on standard). | Buyer address not enforced on standard invoices/notes, no national-address format checks for SA buyers, and the address appears neither in print nor in XML.<br>*Risk:* Standard tax invoices lack mandatory buyer address data, risking invalid invoices and clearance rejection. | PH1-05, PH1-02, PH2-04<br>**P0** | REM-15, REM-14, REM-16 |
| **R1-229** · Buyer name<br>Buyer name is mandatory on tax invoices and their notes; on simplified summary invoices the buyer name must be present.<br>*[XML — XML Implementation Standard v1.2, 19 May 2023]; [RES-EN — Implementation Resolution (EN), 19 May 2023] · [XML] BR-KSA-42 p.56, BR-KSA-71 p.59; [RES — Implementation Resolution, 19 May 2023] Annex 2 5.1* | **PARTIALLY IMPLEMENTED** | Invoices and credit notes require partner_id (StoreInvoiceRequest.php:17; StoreCreditNoteRequest.php:18) and partners require a name (StorePartnerRequest.php:17); the invoice XML emits buyer RegistrationName (ZatcaService.php:302) and prints show it. Notes have no XML at all and summary invoices are unsupported.<br>*Evidence:* D Appendix A (BT-44 pass on all shapes); PH2-05 (no note XML/QR); PH2-13 (summary and other transaction flags unsupported; ZatcaService.php:254). | Buyer name cannot appear on e-notes because e-notes do not exist; simplified summary invoices cannot be issued.<br>*Risk:* Credit/debit notes do not meet e-note content rules.<br>*Note:* Invoice-level buyer name requirement is met; printed buyer name is read live (PH1-02). | PH2-05, PH2-13<br>**P0** | REM-18, REM-16 |
| **R1-230** · Codes<br>Country codes ISO 3166-1 alpha-2; currencies ISO 4217 alphabetic; TaxCurrencyCode must exist; payment means per UNCL4461.<br>*[XML — XML Implementation Standard v1.2, 19 May 2023] · 11.2.2-11.2.5 pp.31-34; BR-KSA-68 p.58* | **PARTIALLY IMPLEMENTED** | The XML emits DocumentCurrencyCode 'SAR' (valid ISO 4217) fixed regardless of tenant currency (ZatcaService.php:273), but no TaxCurrencyCode, no country codes (no addresses) and no PaymentMeans. Currency/country master data are only length-checked (UpdateCompanyRequest.php:20-21; partner country free text, StorePartnerRequest.php:32).<br>*Evidence:* PH2-04 / D Appendix A (BR-KSA-68 ERROR 18/18: TaxCurrencyCode missing); PH2-13 (no TaxCurrencyCode/FX); PH1-18 (QR/XML assume SAR); PH1-17. | TaxCurrencyCode absent (certain SDK error); no ISO-validated country/currency master data; no UNCL4461 payment means; non-SAR tenants produce SAR-labelled amounts.<br>*Risk:* Every e-invoice fails ZATCA validation on BR-KSA-68. | PH2-04, PH2-13, PH1-18, PH1-17<br>**P0** | REM-16, REM-14 |
| **R1-231** · Export/self-billing flags<br>Export invoices must not carry a buyer VAT number; self-billing flag may not be set on export invoices.<br>*[XML — XML Implementation Standard v1.2, 19 May 2023] · BR-KSA-46 p.57; BR-KSA-07 p.48* | **NOT IMPLEMENTED** | Only transaction codes 0100000/0200000 are produced (ZatcaService.php:254); there is no export or self-billing flag and no export/zero-rate category, so export invoices cannot be represented and BR-KSA-46/BR-KSA-07 are never checked.<br>*Evidence:* PH2-13; VAT-03 (no category/VATEX); code read ZatcaService.php:254 (only standard/simplified codes). | Cannot issue export or self-billed e-invoices or enforce their buyer-VAT and flag constraints.<br>*Risk:* Exporters cannot issue compliant export invoices through AWJ. | PH2-13, VAT-03<br>**P1** | REM-16, REM-12 |
| **R1-232** · Citizen ID for EDU/HEA<br>When the exemption reason is VATEX-SA-EDU or VATEX-SA-HEA, the buyer other ID is mandatory and must be a National ID (NAT).<br>*[XML — XML Implementation Standard v1.2, 19 May 2023] · BR-KSA-49 p.57* | **NOT IMPLEMENTED** | No exemption-reason codes (incl. VATEX-SA-EDU/HEA) and no buyer national-ID field exist; partners have no NAT ID (grep national_id → Employee model only) and the XML emits no buyer other ID.<br>*Evidence:* VAT-03 (grep VATEX\|tax_category in app/ → none); PH2-04 / D Appendix A (buyer other ID not emitted). | Cannot issue citizen education/health invoices carrying VATEX-SA-EDU/HEA with the mandatory NAT buyer ID.<br>*Risk:* Private education/healthcare providers cannot issue compliant State-borne-VAT invoices.<br>*Note:* Links to R1-179 (box 2). | VAT-03, PH2-04<br>**P0** | REM-12, REM-16 |

## Part 2 — Technical requirements (R2)

| Official requirement (source · section · version) | Status | AWJ implementation · evidence | Gap · risk | Findings · severity | Tasks |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| **R2-001** · Validation phases<br>An invoice is compliant only after (1) XSD/UBL 2.1 syntax validation and (2) content validation: EN 16931 subset then KSA CIUS rules; KSA rules override EN rules, EN overrides UBL.<br>*D1 (XML Implementation Standard v1.2, 19 May 2023) v1.2 2023-05-19 · §4 pp.5–6; D6 (Developer Portal Manual v3, Nov 2022) §4.2.4 Q1* | **VERIFIED DEFECT** | AWJ assembles the UBL as a string template (`app/Services/Accounting/ZatcaService.php:234-315`, self-described as not validated against the ZATCA XSD at `:232`) and hashes/stores it at posting without any XSD, EN 16931 or KSA Schematron check; no validator exists in `app/` or `config/` (grep xsd\|schematron\|schemaValidate: namespace constants only).<br>*Evidence:* D stream S3, SDK 238-R3.4.8 `-validate` on 18 AWJ outputs (`work/D/sdk/validate-awj-outputs.summary.txt`): GLOBAL FAILED 18/18; XSD FAILED 18/18 (PartyTaxScheme lacks TaxScheme; SigningCertificateV2 on signed); EN FAILED (BR-CO-04/15/18 errors); KSA FAILED (BR-KSA-68/39 errors); QR and SIGNATURE FAILED on simplified; PIH FAILED vs official pih.txt (lead `official/R2-work/run-lead/awj-*-unsigned.validate.log`). PH2-14: no test uses the SDK or XSD. | No validation gate before hashing and storing; every document shape fails the first (XSD) stage and the EN and KSA content stages.<br>*Risk:* Every AWJ e-invoice would be rejected at compliance checks, clearance or reporting, and the non-compliant XML is irreversibly hashed into the PIH chain.<br>*Note:* The SDK is the executable reference ZATCA ships (R2 §0); live platform behaviour not exercised (D §4.1). Remediation: SDK/Schematron gate in CI and before posting (PH2-04 recommendation). | PH2-04, PH2-02, PH2-01, PH2-03, PH2-11, PH1-17, PH2-14, VAT-03, VAT-06<br>**P0** | REM-16, REM-17, REM-11, REM-12 |
| **R2-002** · Document types<br>`cbc:InvoiceTypeCode` ∈ {388 invoice, 386 prepayment invoice, 383 debit note, 381 credit note}; UBL message type is always `Invoice` (also for credit notes).<br>*D1 (XML Implementation Standard v1.2, 19 May 2023) §11.2.1 p.30–31 · BR-KSA-05* | **PARTIALLY IMPLEMENTED** | The root element is `Invoice` and `cbc:InvoiceTypeCode` is hard-coded `388` (`app/Services/Accounting/ZatcaService.php:258,272`). Credit notes and sales returns post with no XML/QR/ICV (no ZATCA call in `CreditNoteService`/`ReturnService`), no sales debit-note model exists, and no 386 prepayment invoice is modelled (grep prepay\|prepaid\|386 in `app/` and migrations: 0).<br>*Evidence:* SDK S3: no BR-KSA-05 on 18/18 (388 accepted); PH2-05 (probe P2: posted CN-2026-00001 carries no zatca attributes, max ICV unchanged); C-probe1.log:47-48 (credit_notes/return_documents have no zatca/qr columns); SUB-04 (`requestManual()` accepts Invoice only); CON-03. | 381 credit notes, 383 debit notes and 386 prepayment invoices cannot be produced; only 388 exists.<br>*Risk:* Credit/debit notes and advance-payment invoices fall outside e-invoicing, so VAT reductions and advance-payment tax points are invisible to ZATCA.<br>*Note:* 388 output passes BR-KSA-05. The 386 obligation applies only to tenants invoicing advance payments (CON-03; B §4). | PH2-05, PH1-06, SUB-04, CON-03<br>**P0** | REM-18, REM-24, REM-29 |
| **R2-003** · Transaction code<br>`cbc:InvoiceTypeCode/@name` = `NNPNESB`: NN=01 tax / 02 simplified; P 3rd-party; N nominal; E export; S summary; B self-billed.<br>*D1 (XML Implementation Standard v1.2, 19 May 2023) §11.2.1 + BR-KSA-06 p.48 · BR-KSA-06* | **PARTIALLY IMPLEMENTED** | `@name` is `0100000` when `zatca_document_type` is 'standard', otherwise `0200000` (`app/Services/Accounting/ZatcaService.php:254`); the subtype is suggested from a 15-digit buyer VAT but any explicit value is accepted (`app/Services/Accounting/InvoiceService.php:429-447`). No third-party, nominal, export, summary, self-billed, continuous-supply or B2G flag exists (grep in `app/Services/Accounting`, `app/Models/Invoice.php`: 0).<br>*Evidence:* SDK S3: no BR-KSA-06/BR-KSA-F-06-C40 on 18/18 (7-character code valid); D Appendix A row KSA-2 'pass (basic)'; PH2-13; PH1-05 (`ZatcaTest::an_explicit_document_type_overrides_the_customer_suggestion`; C-probe1.log:35-39: standard without buyer VAT posted, alphanumeric VAT classed simplified). | Positions 3-9 are always 0, so special transactions are mis-flagged; the 01/02 subtype is not validated against buyer data.<br>*Risk:* Exports, summary, third-party, self-billed and B2G sales are reported with wrong flags, and B2B/B2C misclassification sends documents down the wrong clearance/reporting route.<br>*Note:* SDK 3.4.3+ accepts 7-9 characters; AWJ's 7-character code is syntactically valid. | PH2-13, PH1-05<br>**P1** | REM-16, REM-15 |
| **R2-004** · Self-billing limits<br>Self-billed not allowed for exports (position 5=1 ⇒ position 7≠1) [error]; simplified documents cannot be self-billed (BR-KSA-31, error). SDK 3.4.6 relaxed BR-KSA-31 to allow simplified **exports** (B2C).<br>*D1 (XML Implementation Standard v1.2, 19 May 2023) BR-KSA-07, BR-KSA-31; S2 (SDK release notes 3.3.8→3.4.8) (3.4.6) · pp.48, 55* | **NOT APPLICABLE** | AWJ never sets position 7 (self-billed): the code is always `0100000` or `0200000` (`app/Services/Accounting/ZatcaService.php:254`), so the self-billed-export and simplified-self-billed prohibitions cannot be breached.<br>*Evidence:* Code read `ZatcaService.php:254`; SDK S3: no BR-KSA-07/BR-KSA-31 on 18/18 outputs. | none for the limits; the self-billing capability itself is absent (see R2-003, PH2-13).<br>*Risk:* none identified<br>*Note:* Binds only documents flagged self-billed. If self-billing is added, BR-KSA-07/31 (with the SDK 3.4.6 simplified-export relaxation) must be enforced at document creation. | PH2-13<br>**P2** | REM-16 |
| **R2-005** · B2G<br>If @name position 9 = 1 (B2G) then Contract ID (BT-12, `cac:ContractDocumentReference/cbc:ID`) mandatory [error, BR-KSA-92]; Company legal form KSA-40 should be `GOV` [warning, BR-KSA-91].<br>*S1 (ZATCA SDK 238-R3.4.8) KSA XSL; S2 (SDK release notes 3.3.8→3.4.8) (3.4.3) · BR-KSA-91/92* | **NOT IMPLEMENTED** | No B2G flag (position 9), `cac:ContractDocumentReference` (BT-12) or `cbc:CompanyLegalForm` (KSA-40) is modelled or emitted; the transaction code is fixed at seven positions (`app/Services/Accounting/ZatcaService.php:254`).<br>*Evidence:* grep `ContractDocumentReference\|CompanyLegalForm` in `app/`: 0; grep `b2g\|government` in `app/Services/Accounting`, `app/Models/Invoice.php`: 0; PH2-13 (business process lists B2G; requirement cites BR-KSA-92). | Government-customer invoices cannot carry the B2G flag, contract ID or GOV legal form.<br>*Risk:* Tenants supplying government entities cannot issue B2G-conformant e-invoices.<br>*Note:* Rule is SDK-only (3.4.3+, not in the 2023 PDF); whether every B2G sale must set position 9 is NOT VERIFIED from the register. Relevance depends on the tenant's customer base. | PH2-13<br>**P2** | REM-16 |
| **R2-006** · Profile<br>`cbc:ProfileID` must be `reporting:1.0` [error].<br>*D1 (XML Implementation Standard v1.2, 19 May 2023) BR-KSA-EN16931-01 p.66* | **VERIFIED IMPLEMENTED** | `cbc:ProfileID` is emitted as `reporting:1.0` for every document type, standard and simplified (`app/Services/Accounting/ZatcaService.php:267`).<br>*Evidence:* SDK S3: no BR-KSA-EN16931-01 on 18/18 AWJ outputs (both subtypes); D Appendix A row BT-23 'pass'; all 19 official SDK samples, including standard invoices and notes, use `reporting:1.0` (38/38 occurrences in `official/R2-reference-samples/derived/`); Data Dictionary BT-23 M x6 with default `reporting:1.0`. | none<br>*Risk:* none identified<br>*Note:* Lead (GM5 review): AWJ's `reporting:1.0` ProfileID on standard invoices is correct (BR-KSA-EN16931-01 is an error; all 19 official samples carry it); the PH1-17 sub-claim that it is wrong is withdrawn. | —<br>**—** | — |
| **R2-007** · UUID<br>`cbc:UUID` (KSA-1) mandatory, letters/digits/dashes only [error].<br>*D1 (XML Implementation Standard v1.2, 19 May 2023) BR-KSA-03 p.47; D2 (Data Dictionary v1.2, 19 May 2023) KSA-1* | **VERIFIED IMPLEMENTED** | A fresh v4 UUID (hex digits and dashes) is generated at each posting and emitted as `cbc:UUID` (`app/Services/Accounting/ZatcaService.php:43,269`), stored once in `invoices.zatca_uuid`; service paths never regenerate or reassign it.<br>*Evidence:* SDK S3: no BR-KSA-03 on 18/18; D Appendix A row KSA-1 'pass (v4)'; ZatcaPhase2Test (UUID v4 assertion) 8/8 on SQLite and PostgreSQL (`logs/test-table-full.md`); `work/D/out/manifest.json` shows a distinct uuid per invoice. | none for the element; after a rejection AWJ can only re-send the same rejected UUID/ICV and has no guided re-issue (SUB-07).<br>*Risk:* none identified for the element itself.<br>*Note:* A corrected document can only be produced via duplicate then post (new UUID/ICV), so no UUID is reused across documents; the correction workflow belongs to SUB-07 / R2-061. | SUB-07<br>**P1** | REM-24 |
| **R2-008** · Issue date/time<br>`cbc:IssueDate` mandatory, `YYYY-MM-DD` (BR-KSA-F-01, **error** since SDK 3.4.2), ≤ current date (BR-KSA-04, error); `cbc:IssueTime` (KSA-25) mandatory, `hh:mm:ss` or `hh:mm:ssZ` (BR-KSA-70, error). Dates carry no timezone; local KSA time or UTC with `Z`.<br>*D1 (XML Implementation Standard v1.2, 19 May 2023) §7.3 rows 7,10 p.13; BR-KSA-04/70/F-01* | **VERIFIED DEFECT** | `IssueDate`/`IssueTime` are formatted `Y-m-d`/`H:i:s` from the draft's `created_at` in the application time zone (`app/Services/Accounting/ZatcaService.php:253,270-271`; signed path `:90`), not from the posting instant, the tenant time zone or `invoice_date` (ledger `entry_date`, `app/Services/Accounting/InvoiceService.php:1005`); no `Z` is appended.<br>*Evidence:* Format passes (SDK S3: no BR-KSA-F-01/04/70). PH2-08: probe P2 `backdated_invoice_date` invoice_date 2026-01-15 vs IssueDate 2026-10-10; C-probe1.log:19-24 (app.timezone UTC; draft created 2026-10-01 09:00 and posted later gives IssueTime 09:00:00, journal date 2026-09-15); S3 `[WARN] timeStamp` mismatch between QR (Z) and XML. | Issue date/time reflect draft creation, not issuance; under a UTC app zone the un-suffixed time is not KSA local time.<br>*Risk:* E-invoices carry issue dates/times that contradict the books and the print, distorting the 24-hour reporting window and the VAT period, and the hashed values cannot be corrected later.<br>*Note:* Production APP_TIMEZONE NOT VERIFIED (render.yaml sets none, C §4.1). Back-dating policy is an owner decision (D §4.7). | PH2-08, PH1-01, PH1-14<br>**P1** | REM-13 |
| **R2-009** · Currency<br>`cbc:DocumentCurrencyCode` mandatory ISO 4217; `cbc:TaxCurrencyCode` mandatory and must be `SAR` (BR-KSA-68 error, BR-KSA-EN16931-02 error); all `@currencyID` = document currency except BT-111 (BR-KSA-CL-02 error). Non-SAR invoice: BT-110 ≠ BT-111 expected (BR-KSA-97 warning); exactly one `cac:TaxTotal` with subtotals (BR-KSA-EN16931-08) and one without when tax currency present (…-09). Tax exchange rate (KSA-36…39): source = BT-5, target = …<br>*D1 (XML Implementation Standard v1.2, 19 May 2023) §9.1, §11.2.3, rules pp.57,63–66; S1 (ZATCA SDK 238-R3.4.8)* | **VERIFIED DEFECT** | `cbc:DocumentCurrencyCode` and every `@currencyID` are hard-coded `SAR` (`app/Services/Accounting/ZatcaService.php:245,247,273,306-311`) although `tenants.currency` is editable (`app/Http/Requests/UpdateCompanyRequest.php:20`); no `cbc:TaxCurrencyCode`, second `TaxTotal` or exchange-rate data is emitted (grep `TaxCurrencyCode` in `app/`: 0).<br>*Evidence:* SDK S3: BR-KSA-68 ERROR on 18/18 and BR-KSA-EN16931-08 WARN on 18/18 (`work/D/sdk/validate-awj-outputs.summary.txt`); D Appendix A rows BT-5/BT-6; PH1-18 (print shows tenant currency, `web/src/modules/documents/builder/from-invoice.ts:133`); PH2-13 (no FX). | Mandatory BT-6 = SAR absent; non-SAR invoices are labelled SAR without BT-111 or an exchange rate.<br>*Risk:* Every document fails KSA validation, and a non-SAR tenant would report foreign-currency amounts as riyals.<br>*Note:* Fix requires BT-6 plus the TaxTotal-with-subtotals structure (BR-KSA-EN16931-08/09); FX support only if non-SAR invoicing is in scope. | PH2-04, PH2-13, PH1-18, PH1-17<br>**P0** | REM-16, REM-14 |
| **R2-010** · ICV<br>Invoice counter value KSA-16 in `cac:AdditionalDocumentReference[cbc:ID='ICV']/cbc:UUID`, mandatory, digits only (BR-KSA-33/34, errors); one sequence per EGS unit covering standard+simplified; never reused; gaps investigated.<br>*D1 (XML Implementation Standard v1.2, 19 May 2023) BR-KSA-33/34 p.55; D5 (Detailed Technical Guideline v2, Nov 2022) §4.3 p.48 & FAQ pp.71,76,78* | **PARTIALLY IMPLEMENTED** | ICV is emitted as digits in `AdditionalDocumentReference[ID='ICV']/cbc:UUID` (`app/Services/Accounting/ZatcaService.php:274-277`), allocated max+1 under a tenant-row lock in the posting transaction (`:59-75`) and backed by unique `(tenant_id, zatca_icv)` (`database/migrations/2025_01_01_000053_branch_scoped_document_numbering.php:69`). One tenant-wide counter covers standard and simplified invoices across all environments; notes never consume it.<br>*Evidence:* D PC-2/P3 `DIcvPostgresConcurrencyProbeTest` (6 forked posts give ICV 1..6, PIH verified, failed post rolls back); F PC-9 (`logs/F-pg-race2.log`); ZatcaIcvScopeTest 10/10 SQLite+PG; SDK S3: no BR-KSA-33/34 on 18/18; PH2-10/SUB-10/SEC-06 (tenant-wide chain spans environments); PH2-05 (credit note leaves ICV unchanged). | Sequence is per tenant, not per EGS unit/environment; notes excluded; no gap-investigation tooling; non-reuse relies on service-level immutability only.<br>*Risk:* Production chains would start mid-sequence after simulation documents, multi-device EGS setups are impossible, and credit/debit notes are never sequenced.<br>*Note:* Latent: a model-level hard delete of the latest posted invoice (PH1-09 probe) would let the next post reuse its ICV. ZATCA-side storage of accepted documents and rejected hashes is Authority behaviour, not an AWJ capability. | OPS-02, SUB-15<br>**P1** | REM-27, REM-21 |
| **R2-059** · Clearance output<br>For standard documents ZATCA applies its own stamp and generates/updates the QR and returns the document; the **cleared document (with ZATCA stamp + QR) is the valid tax invoice to give the buyer**; seller's own stamp/QR optional on standard documents (ZATCA then adds a second stamp). Clearance does not change the document hash, so the next document can be generated without waiting.<br>*D5 (Detailed Technical Guideline v2, Nov 2022) §4.3.2 pp.50–51, FAQ p.72; D4 (Detailed E-Invoicing Guidelines v2, May 2023) FAQ p.59* | **PARTIALLY IMPLEMENTED** | On accepted clearance AWJ stores the returned XML in `invoices.zatca_cleared_xml` (`app/Services/Accounting/ZatcaSubmissionDispatcher.php:39-52`) and leaves its own XML/hash/PIH chain unchanged, so the next invoice is not blocked. Nothing reads the cleared XML: print, PDF, share, POS and API serve the local QR/XML, and standard invoices are released before clearance. The cleared XML is not checked against the submitted UUID/hash.<br>*Evidence:* Code read; E PC-9 + probe P01 (cleared XML stored, QR unchanged, cleared XML not exposed; next PIH == hash(previous zatca_xml)); E Appendix C (no release path consults ZATCA status); lead verification-log (E / SUB-01 CONFIRMED); SEC-11 (`ZatcaSubmissionDispatcher.php:109-118`: no DOCTYPE rejection, no UUID/hash check). | The ZATCA-stamped cleared document is never what the buyer receives; no clearance gate before release; returned XML not integrity-checked.<br>*Risk:* Every B2B invoice given to buyers is invalid from ZATCA's perspective, exposing tenants to penalties and buyers to input-VAT challenges.<br>*Note:* AWJ self-signs standard invoices when a credential exists (allowed: seller stamp optional). The `clearedInvoice` field name is RECALLED-UNVERIFIED. | SUB-01, SUB-04, SEC-11<br>**P0** | REM-24, REM-38 |
| **R2-060** · Reporting timing<br>Simplified documents reported within **24 hours** of issue (SDK BR-KSA-98 warns when IssueDate+IssueTime older than 24 h); seller must include its own stamp and QR; ZATCA does not stamp simplified documents. Continuous-supply simplified invoices: notify at end of supply month. Tax invoices may be issued within 15 days after the month of supply but must be cleared before being given to the buyer.<br>*D5 (Detailed Technical Guideline v2, Nov 2022) §4.3.1 p.49; D4 (Detailed E-Invoicing Guidelines v2, May 2023) pp.52,59, FAQ p.59; S1 (ZATCA SDK 238-R3.4.8) BR-KSA-98* | **NOT IMPLEMENTED** | Simplified invoices are reported only through manual per-invoice API calls (`routes/api.php:681-684`, 10/min); there is no posting hook, scheduler, UI caller or 24-hour tracking, and production runs `QUEUE_CONNECTION=sync` with no worker. AWJ's own stamp/QR exists only once a credential exists and fails the SDK; issue time is the draft's `created_at`. Standard invoices are released before clearance.<br>*Evidence:* SUB-05 (E grep: no scheduler/observer/UI caller; probe P08: 3-day-old pending attempt) and lead verification-log (E / SUB-05 CONFIRMED); OPS-01 (`Dockerfile:47-53`, `deploy/entrypoint.sh:98`); PH2-08/PH1-01 (`ZatcaService.php:90,163,253`; C probe 1); D S3 (QR/SIGNATURE FAILED); SUB-01 (E Appendix C); PH2-13 (no continuous-supply flag). | No mechanism to report simplified documents within 24 h; no continuous-supply handling; no valid seller stamp/QR; standard invoices not held until cleared.<br>*Risk:* Every B2C/POS invoice breaches the 24-hour rule and every B2B invoice the clear-before-sharing rule, with penalty exposure.<br>*Note:* Settings offer an 'automatic' submission mode that nothing implements (SUB-05). SDK BR-KSA-98 warns on documents older than 24 h; a draft-time IssueDate makes this likelier. | SUB-05, OPS-01, OPS-04, SUB-01, PH2-08, PH1-01, PH2-13, PH2-01, PH2-03<br>**P0** | REM-23, REM-27, REM-24, REM-13, REM-16, REM-17 |
| **R2-061** · Rejections & chaining<br>After a rejection: keep the rejected document's hash/ICV (never reuse), generate a new document with new UUID, ICV, hash, timestamp; its PIH = hash of the immediately preceding generated document (which may be the rejected one). Simplified: original was already issued, keep transaction in VAT return; standard: non-cleared invoice is not a valid tax invoice — reissue with updated date. Duplicate submission is not rejected by the API but ZATCA …<br>*D5 (Detailed Technical Guideline v2, Nov 2022) FAQ pp.70–71,76–77* | **PARTIALLY IMPLEMENTED** | Rejected invoices keep their ICV/hash and stay in the chain; the next PIH is the hash of the last generated XML, even if rejected (`app/Services/Accounting/ZatcaService.php:69-75,140-151`). No correction flow exists: a new attempt re-sends the byte-identical XML (`ZatcaSubmissionService.php:48-71`) and the original stays posted and printable. Credit notes are not e-invoiced, so NOT_REPORTED cannot be cancelled by an e-note.<br>*Evidence:* E PC-9 + probe P10 (next PIH == hash(rejected xml); resubmission resends identical XML and is rejected again); SUB-07; SUB-01 (E Appendix C); PH2-05 (D probe P2: posted credit note has no ZATCA artefacts, ICV unchanged); code read at cited lines. | No re-issue of a new document (new UUID/ICV/hash/date) after rejection; rejected standard invoices stay with buyers; no e-invoiced credit-note cancellation path.<br>*Risk:* Rejected B2B invoices remain in circulation as invalid tax invoices, and rejected B2C invoices cannot be properly corrected.<br>*Note:* Re-sending an identical document after a timeout matches this row (duplicates are not rejected, counted once); see conflicts on SUB-06. Status strings other than NOT_REPORTED are RECALLED-UNVERIFIED. Rejected simplified invoices stay in the ledger/VAT figures, as required. | SUB-07, SUB-01, PH2-05<br>**P0** | REM-24, REM-18 |

No CSID response is ever parsed.<br>*Evidence:* Code read at cited lines; the register marks these JSON names RECALLED-UNVERIFIED (Swagger not obtained); E probes P01/P02 used assumed shapes under `Http::fake()`. | **R2-070** · QR (phase 1 vs 2)<br>QR base64 TLV ≤ 700 chars (D3) / field ≤1000 chars (SDK rule). Tags 1–5 mandatory since 2021-12-04 (phase 1); tags 6–8 from 2023-01-01 (phase 2, in waves); tag 9 for simplified documents only. TLV: 1-byte tag, 1-byte length (UTF-8 byte count), value.<br>*D3 (Security Features Standards v1.2, 19 May 2023) §4 pp.25–26; D5 (Detailed Technical Guideline v2, Nov 2022) §6 pp.58–63* | **PARTIALLY IMPLEMENTED** | Phase 1 (no credential): five TLV tags, 1-byte tag and 1-byte UTF-8 byte length, base64, no length guard (`app/Services/Accounting/ZatcaService.php:156-168,181-184`). | **R2-071** · QR tag values<br>1 seller name (BT-27); 2 seller VAT (BT-31); 3 `IssueDate + 'T' + IssueTime` (samples: no `Z`; D3/D5 examples with `Z`); 4 `TaxInclusiveAmount` (BT-112) text; 5 `cac:TaxTotal/cbc:TaxAmount` (BT-110) text; 6 invoice hash **base64 text** (44 bytes); 7 `ds:SignatureValue` **base64 text** (e.g. 96 bytes); 8 **raw DER** SubjectPublicKeyInfo of the signing key (88 bytes secp256k1, bytes `30 56 30 10 06 07 2a 86 48 ce 3d 02 01 06 05 2b 81 04 00 0a 03` …<br>*D5 (Detailed Technical Guideline v2, Nov 2022) §6.2 worked example (hex) + XPath table pp.60–62; S1 (ZATCA SDK 238-R3.4.8) samples (decoded and matched); D3 (Security Features Standards v1.2, 19 May 2023) Table 3* | **VERIFIED DEFECT** | Tags 1, 2, 4, 5 come from tenant name/VAT and invoice totals and match the XML; tag 9 is the DER certificate signature (simplified only). SDK S3 `[QR] FAILED` hashedXml / digital signature / publicKey, timeStamp WARN. D PC-8 (tags 1,2,4,5,9 accepted). C probes 1/3 (tag 3 = draft time; tag 2 empty or `12345`). SUB-04 probe P01 (standard QR never replaced). | Tags 3, 6, 7, 8 wrongly sourced or encoded; tag 2 unvalidated in phase 1; standard invoices carry AWJ's QR instead of the cleared QR.<br>*Risk:* QR verification fails for every simplified invoice, and the QR contradicts the invoice data.<br>*Note:* Tag 3 may carry `Z` only if the XML IssueTime does (samples omit `Z`, D3/D5 examples include it); the binding rule is textual equality with the XML (SDK timeStamp check). | PH2-03, PH2-01, PH2-08, PH1-01, PH1-14, PH1-04, PH2-16, SUB-04<br>**P0** | REM-17, REM-13, REM-14, REM-24 |
| **R2-072** · QR common mistakes<br>Tag/length are binary bytes (not ASCII digits), values converted to bytes before base64, no separators/padding between TLVs, UTF-8 for Arabic.<br>*D5 (Detailed Technical Guideline v2, Nov 2022) §6.3 p.64* | **PARTIALLY IMPLEMENTED** | Both encoders write binary tag and length bytes (`chr($tag).chr($len)`), concatenate TLVs without separators, use UTF-8 byte counts and base64 the raw bytes (`app/Services/Accounting/ZatcaService.php:181-184`; `ZatcaPhaseTwoQrEncoder.php:95-103`). The phase-1 encoder does not bound the length byte, so values over 255 bytes wrap silently.<br>*Evidence:* Code read; C PC-2 (ZatcaTest TLV tests; probe 1 decode of Arabic values); C probe 1 `tlv_length_byte_overflows` (260 bytes gives length byte 4); phase-2 1-255 guard (`ZatcaPhaseTwoQrEncoder.php:98-100`, ZatcaPhaseTwoQrEncoderTest). | Phase-1 length byte not validated (wraps modulo 256) for long Arabic seller names.<br>*Risk:* Long legal names produce a corrupt, unreadable QR without any error.<br>*Note:* Register §9 P5: a seller name over 255 UTF-8 bytes cannot be encoded; reject it at company-profile save. | PH1-07, PH2-12<br>**P2** | REM-17 |
| **R2-073** · Platform QR check<br>Simplified-document QR tags are checked against XML values by the SDK (seller name, VAT, timestamp, totals, hash, signature, public key) — error codes `sellerName`, `hashedXml`, etc.<br>*S1 (ZATCA SDK 238-R3.4.8) run (`[QR] validation … CODE : sellerName / hashedXml`)* | **VERIFIED DEFECT** | AWJ's simplified-invoice QR fails the SDK's cross-check against the XML: the hash, signature and public-key tags are mis-encoded and the timestamp differs from IssueDate/IssueTime; seller name, VAT and totals match. ## Part 3 — BR-KSA / XML element coverage (what AWJ emits versus what the SDK validator requires)

Source: stream D coverage matrix, evidenced against `ZatcaService.php` and the SDK run on AWJ-generated documents (deliverable A §8.2). "18/18" refers to the 18 AWJ outputs validated in that stream.

Legend: required-for column uses Data Dictionary 2023 (M mandatory / C conditional / O optional) for **Std** = standard invoice & notes, **Simp** = simplified invoice & notes. "SDK result" = what the official SDK reported on AWJ's output (S3) or "n/a" when the rule did not fire because a prerequisite element is absent. **ERROR** = fails the stage (certain rejection by the SDK); **WARN** = reported as warning in this 2021-08-19 XSL set (does not fail the stage; the production platform's treatment of warnings is NOT VERIFIED). Level split taken from my own 18-file log (`validate-awj-outputs.log`, `[ERROR]`/`[WARN]` prefixes) and confirmed by the lead's calibration run.

| Element / rule | Required for | Emitted by `buildXml`? | Evidence (`ZatcaService.php`) | SDK result on AWJ output |
|---|---|---|---|---|

| `cbc:ID` (BT-1) | M | yes (`invoice.number`) | `:268` | pass |
| `cbc:UUID` (KSA-1, BR-KSA-03) | M | yes (v4) | `:269`, `:43` | pass |

## Part 5 — VAT return boxes (register R1 §3): what AWJ can produce

Form as reproduced in the Retail Sector Guideline Issue 3 (May 2026) §5.3.2. AWJ's only tax report is `ReportService::taxReport` (output VAT = net of account 2120, input VAT = net of 1150, net payable) — three numbers, no boxes, no categories, no period freeze (ACC-03, ACC-04).

| Box | Label | AWJ today | Status | Findings |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| 1 | Standard-rated sales (15 %) | Sales exist as invoices; no category dimension, no tax-point/credit-note-period split, no drill-down | NOT IMPLEMENTED | ACC-03, VAT-03 |
| 1.2 | Standard-rated sales to government entities | No customer-type or tax-point rule | NOT IMPLEMENTED | ACC-03 |
| 2 | Sales where the State bears the VAT (education/health, VATEX-SA-EDU/HEA) | No reason codes | NOT IMPLEMENTED | VAT-03 |
| 3 | Zero-rated domestic sales | `tax_rate = 0` is indistinguishable from exempt | NOT IMPLEMENTED | VAT-03 |
| 4 | Exports | No export flag/category | NOT IMPLEMENTED | VAT-03, PH2-13 |
| 5 | Exempt sales | No exempt category | NOT IMPLEMENTED | VAT-03 |
| 6 | Total sales | Not derivable per box | NOT IMPLEMENTED | ACC-03 |
| 7 | Standard-rated purchases (15 %) | Purchase VAT posts to 1150; no deductibility/adjustment split | PARTIALLY IMPLEMENTED | CON-04, ACC-03 |
| 8 | Standard-rated imports — VAT paid at import | Not supported | NOT IMPLEMENTED | CON-04 |
| 9 | Imports subject to reverse charge | Not supported | NOT IMPLEMENTED | CON-04 |
| 10 | Zero-rated purchases | No category | NOT IMPLEMENTED | VAT-03 |
| 11 | Exempt purchases | No category | NOT IMPLEMENTED | VAT-03 |
| 12 | Total purchases | Not derivable per box | NOT IMPLEMENTED | ACC-03 |
| 13 | Total VAT due for the period | GL net output − input from `taxReport` (fixed codes 1150/2120; branch filter drops null-branch lines) | PARTIALLY IMPLEMENTED | ACC-03, ACC-01, ACC-12 |
| 14 | Adjustments for previous periods | No period snapshot or amendment | NOT IMPLEMENTED | ACC-04 |
| 15 | VAT carried forward | Not supported | NOT IMPLEMENTED | ACC-03 |
| 16 | Net VAT payable / refundable | Only as the GL net in box 13 terms | PARTIALLY IMPLEMENTED | ACC-03 |
